This patient-grouped benchmark identifies contactless overnight sensing as a promising biomedical engineering direction for agitation-risk research in hospitalized dementia cohorts.

</details>


### [202] [CrabOS: An Operating System for Human-AI Co-inhabitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28165)
*Qi Yang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"人类-AI共居"(Human-AI Co-inhabitation)概念，通过设计并实现CrabOS操作系统，使人类和AI在同一工作环境中无缝交替主导任务执行。CrabOS将工作状态表示为自然语言可读的文本对象，供人机共享并直接操作，消除了传统Agent系统中人机环境分离所需的额外桥接成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI Agent系统为人类和AI提供分离的工作环境，导致任务交接时要么依赖开发者构建任务特定接口，要么依赖用户手动通过截图或文本描述传递工作状态。这两种方式都使交接成本高昂且难以扩展，无法支撑真实世界中人类与AI需要交替主导执行任务的场景。

Method: 提出Human-AI Co-inhabitation工作环境概念，使人类和AI能在同一任务上无缝交替工作。具体实现为CrabOS系统：将工作状态建模为自然语言可读的文本对象，人类和AI可通过同一可审计接口直接访问和操作这些对象，无需额外桥接。

Result: 案例研究表明，CrabOS将支持人类与AI交替主导的复杂任务从依赖桥接的应用程序级解决方案提升为操作系统原生能力，为开发和运行AI Agent提供了新的基础。

Conclusion: CrabOS证明了Human-AI Co-inhabitation的可行性，将人机协作的任务交接能力下沉到操作系统层面，为未来AI Agent系统的基础设施设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: AI agents are evolving into long-running computational entities that can invoke tools, maintain memory, and complete complex tasks across applications. In real-world settings, completing a task often requires humans and AI to take turns leading its execution. Such alternation depends on the seamless handoff of the work state of the task between humans and AI. Existing agent systems, however, provide humans and AI with separate work environments. AI agents must therefore rely on additional bridges to continue work: either developers build task-specific interfaces to access the work state, or users manually transfer relevant parts of it through screenshots or textual descriptions. Both approaches make handoffs costly and scale poorly.
  We propose Human-AI Co-inhabitation, a type of work environment that enables humans and AI to seamlessly take turns continuing work on the same task, and design and implement CrabOS to realize this concept. CrabOS represents the work state as natural-language-readable text objects shared by humans and AI, allowing both to access and manipulate it directly through the same auditable interface without bridges. Case studies show that CrabOS elevates support for complex tasks with alternating human and AI leadership from bridge-dependent application-level solutions to native operating-system capabilities, which provide a new foundation for developing and running AI agents.

</details>


### [203] [Expert Knowledge & Machine Understanding: Bridging Reactome's Ontology with LLM Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28178)
*Susanna Bravi,Riccardo De Luca,Rosa Sicilia,Christine Nardini,Mario Santoro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨能否利用Reactome数据库中人类撰写的通路文本描述来推断专家定义的整体层级结构。通过将SPECTER2句子变换模型与改进的凝聚嵌套算法和图重构算法相结合，从文本元数据重建语义层级结构，并通过定量和定性分析验证了该假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物知识库（如Reactome）提供高质量通路，但人工审核注释存在显著的可扩展性挑战。尽管已有众多NLP工具利用文本信息自动扩展生物知识库，但鲜有研究评估文本描述之间的关系能否反映更高层次的生物学关系。本文旨在探究文本元数据是否可用于推断专家定义的通路全局层级结构。

Method: 从Reactome中提取人类（Homo Sapiens）通路及其反应的层级结构（Reactome Hierarchy），利用文本元数据重建语义层级结构（Semantic Hierarchy）。具体结合了SPECTER2句子变换模型、改进的凝聚嵌套算法（modified agglomerative nesting algorithm）和图重构算法（graph reconstruction algorithm）来构建语义层级。

Result: 定量分析（Laplacian Spectral Distance和Bootstrapping）及定性分析（全局拓扑指标）均验证了研究假设，表明专家撰写的文本元数据可以推断出通路的全局层级结构。

Conclusion: 人类撰写的文本描述能够有效推断生物知识库中专家定义的全局层级结构，这为自动化扩展和维护生物知识库提供了新的可能性，有助于缓解人工审核的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Biological knowledgebases like Reactome provide high-quality pathways that include biological elements' relationships and textual descriptions (metadata). The quality of such pathways is granted by manual curation, that presents, however, significant scalability challenges. Lately, numerous NLP tools have been proposed to cope with this issue, leveraging textual information to automatically expand biological knowledgebases. However, little exploration has been done so far to assess whether relationships among textual descriptions mirror higher order biological relationships. This study explores whether human-written descriptions in Reactome can be used to infer the experts' defined global hierarchical structure. To test this, we extracted from Reactome the Homo Sapiens hierarchy of pathways and their reactions (Reactome Hierarchy), and used textual metadata to reconstruct a Semantic Hierarchy, combining a sentence transformer model (SPECTER2) with a modified agglomerative nesting algorithm and a graph reconstruction algorithm. Quantitative (Laplacian Spectral Distance and Bootstrapping) and qualitative (global topological metrics) analyses confirm our hypothesis and indicate that the global hierarchical structure of pathways can be inferred by experts textual metadata.

</details>


### [204] [Generative AI Alignment with Hinduism's Theological Plurality and Sacred Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28228)
*Dipto Das,Arpita Kundu,Nusrat Jahan Mim,Shion Guha,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过15次对孟加拉国印度教用户的半结构化访谈，研究他们如何与生成式AI互动以支持其宗教知识、信仰与实践，发现AI既提供可及性也带来伦理问题，并主张生成式AI需要"解释性对齐"以解决宗教对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐和伦理讨论主要围绕世俗、西方及亚伯拉罕宗教假设展开，对非亚伯拉罕信仰传统（如印度教）关注不足。随着生成式AI日益介入宗教性日常实践，有必要审视非西方宗教用户如何解读和使用这些系统，以填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用质性研究方法，对15名孟加拉国印度教参与者进行半结构化访谈，分析用户如何解释AI生成的宗教表征、经文阐释、 devotional 互动及合成宗教媒体。

Result: 研究发现AI既能支持经文探究、宗教可视化与宗教叙事，也引发了神学扁平化、文化误表征、宗教操纵以及神圣在场与权威的模拟等伦理担忧。

Conclusion: 作者主张生成式AI中的宗教对齐需要"解释性对齐"（interpretive alignment）：系统应披露自身局限、保留多元性，并避免模拟神圣权威和谄媚式个性化。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly used to answer personal questions and mediate everyday practices, including religion. However, existing discussions around AI alignment and ethics have largely centered secular, Western, and Abrahamic assumptions about religion, offering limited attention to other faith-based traditions. In this paper, we examine how Hindu users engage with generative AI systems in relation to their religious knowledge, belief, and practice. Drawing on 15 semi-structured interviews with Bangladeshi Hindu participants, we analyze how users interpret AI-generated religious representations, scriptural explanations, devotional interactions, and synthetic religious media. We found that AI can be both accessible and ethically troubling. While AI supported scriptural inquiry, devotional visualization, and religious storytelling, our study also identified concerns about theological flattening, cultural misrepresentation, devotional manipulation, and the simulation of sacred presence and authority. We conclude by arguing that religious alignment in generative AI requires interpretive alignment: systems that disclose their limits, preserve plurality, and avoid simulating sacred authority and sycophantic personalization.

</details>


### [205] [Stay Within Your Bounds: Distance-Guided Decoding for Guaranteed Context-Free Grammar Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28229)
*Vincenzo Collura,Karim Tit,Eleonora Giunchiglia,Mike Papadakis,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于下推自动机的、带前瞻引导的上下文无关文法约束解码框架，通过离线计算有界下推摘要（含可达性标签和到可接受状态的上界距离），在线用于水平感知剪枝和束搜索，保证输出语法正确性，并在JSON、SQL和LTL任务上同时提升语法有效性和完成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于局部前缀可行性的文法约束解码器（仅确保每个token使当前前缀可扩展为某个合法完成）在分词器与文法不匹配以及有限token预算的情况下，仍然可能导致前缀无法最终达到接受状态。需要一种能前瞻到最终接受状态的解码方法来避免这种失败。

Method: 提出一个框架：(1) 离线阶段，将上下文无关文法转换为下推自动机，并计算有界下推摘要，附带可达性标签（区分能否到达接受状态）以及到接受状态的上界距离；(2) 在线解码阶段，利用这些估计值指导水平感知（horizon-aware）剪枝与束搜索。整个解码过程保证语法健全性（syntactically sound），即每个输出都被目标文法接受。

Result: 在JSON、SQL和线性时序逻辑（LTL）三个任务上进行了实验，结果表明该方法在保持一致的语法有效性的同时，相比现有基线改善了完成质量（completion quality）。

Conclusion: 基于下推自动机的有界摘要前瞻能够有效解决传统局部前缀可行性解码在token预算有限和分词器不匹配场景下的不足，实现了语法健全且完成质量更优的文法约束解码。

Abstract: Grammar-constrained decoding helps large language models produce syntactically valid structured outputs, such as code, JSON, and SQL. For context-free grammars, many practical decoders enforce local prefix feasibility: each token must keep the current prefix extendable to some valid completion. Yet, under tokenizer-grammar mismatch and finite token budgets, feasible prefixes may still fail to reach acceptance. We propose a lookahead-guided decoding framework for context-free grammars based on pushdown automata. Offline, we compute bounded pushdown summaries with reachability labels and upper-bound distances to acceptance. Online, these estimates guide horizon-aware pruning and beam search. The resulting decoder is syntactically sound: every output is accepted by the target grammar. Experiments on JSON, SQL, and Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) show both consistent syntactic validity and improved completion quality over existing baselines.

</details>


### [206] [REINS: Refusal-Enhanced Inhibitory Steering with Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28233)
*Kai-Xuan Ding,Hao-Xiang Xu,Ji-Hua Peng,Zi-Qi Chen,Jiaqi Wang,Zhen-Hua Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REINS方法，通过在稀疏自编码器特征空间中同时抑制有害延续特征和增强安全拒答特征来实现大语言模型的推理时安全控制，显著优于现有SAE引导方法，并构建了GUISE数据集用于评估复杂包装下的有害提示安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE引导方法在复杂包装的有害提示下无法可靠地触发拒答，单一的拒答增强过于薄弱，无法有效对抗仍然活跃的有害延续路径，需要更系统的评估方法和更强的干预策略。

Method: (1) 构建GUISE数据集，包含带复杂包装的有害提示； (2) 提出REINS方法，在同一SAE特征空间中同时进行"双轨"干预——抑制有害延续特征并增强安全拒答特征； (3) 在GUISE及其他数据集上对比现有SAE引导方法进行系统评估。

Result: 现有SAE引导方法在复杂包装的有害提示下要么干预过弱无法产生拒答，要么仅通过模型坍塌获得表面上的安全性；REINS显著减少了有害响应，大幅提升了安全拒答率，同时基本保持了一般能力不受损。

Conclusion: 仅增强拒答特征的单一方向SAE引导策略在复杂场景下不足以保证安全，需要在特征层面同时抑制有害路径并强化安全路径，REINS为此提供了一种有效且轻量的推理时安全控制方案。

Abstract: Steering with Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) offers a lightweight inference-time path for adapting the behavior of large language models without retraining. By exposing sparse and interpretable features, SAE steering provides a promising interface for safety control that guides harmful continuations toward refusal. However, we observe that complex wrappers can still undermine existing SAE steering methods on harmful prompts. To evaluate this failure mode systematically, we construct Generalized Undercover Instruction Safety Evaluation (GUISE), a dataset of harmful prompts with complex wrappers. Existing single direction SAE steering methods do not reliably produce refusals on harmful prompts, suggesting that refusal enhancement alone can be too weak when the harmful continuation path remains active. This motivates us to propose Refusal-Enhanced INhibitory Steering (REINS), which suppresses harmful continuation features and enhances safe refusal features in the same SAE feature space. Experiments on GUISE and other datasets show that prior methods either intervene too weakly or achieve only apparent safety through collapse, while REINS substantially reduces harmful responses, markedly improves safe refusals and largely preserves general capabilities.

</details>


### [207] [Beyond Task-Only Matching: Personalized Skill Routing with Counterfactual Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28241)
*Tianle Wang,Yanghe Zou,Xiang Liu,Ziyao Huang,Chenchen Fu,Weiwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化技能路由问题，突破了传统仅基于任务语义匹配的技能路由局限，引入用户画像作为检索条件。作者构建了画像反事实基准，并提出SkillFeed框架——一个渐进式检索-重排方法，先建立任务-技能对齐，再学习画像条件判别，显著提升了技能路由的top-1检索准确率（75.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能路由方法仅基于任务语义匹配，忽略了用户画像中可能存在的约束条件。当具有不相容约束的用户发出相同请求时，纯任务语义匹配会将任务相关性与技能适用性混为一谈，可能选出语义合理但不适合当前用户的技能，这一失败模式尚未被识别和解决。

Method: 将个性化技能路由形式化为画像条件检索问题。具体包括：(1) 提出画像反事实基准，固定任务同时改变用户画像以诱导参考技能变化；(2) 构建配对的画像反事实监督数据；(3) 提出SkillFeed框架，采用渐进式检索-重排策略，先建立任务-技能对齐，再学习画像条件判别，通过检索整体证据并重排语义相似但画像冲突的候选，选出同时满足任务要求和用户约束的技能。

Result: 在SkillFeed-Bench上，SkillFeed达到75.1%的top-1检索准确率，相比对应的预训练路由基线提升了23.1个百分点。在用户画像改变参考技能的查询子集上，加入画像条件化带来35.1个百分点的增益。

Conclusion: 用户画像在改变技能适用性时最为关键，这证明了个性化技能路由的必要性。任务与用户画像应联合作为技能检索的条件，而非仅依赖任务语义匹配。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of reusable skill repositories makes skill routing a critical capability for large language model (LLM) agents. Existing methods treat routing as task-only semantic matching. However, when users with incompatible constraints issue an identical request, this assumption conflates task relevance with skill suitability: a task-only router can select a semantically plausible skill that is unsuitable for the requesting user. To expose this failure mode, we formulate \textit{personalized skill routing} as profile-conditioned retrieval, in which relevance depends jointly on the task and the user profile. We first introduce a profile-counterfactual benchmark, in which the task is held fixed while changes in the user profile induce changes in the reference skill. We further construct paired counterfactual supervision and propose SkillFeed, a progressive retrieve-and-rerank framework that first establishes task--skill alignment and then learns profile-conditioned discrimination. By retrieving body-level evidence and reranking semantically similar but profile-conflicting candidates, SkillFeed identifies skills that satisfy both task requirements and user constraints. On SkillFeed-Bench, SkillFeed attains 75.1\% top-1 retrieval accuracy, a 23.1-point improvement over the corresponding pretrained routing baseline. Adding profile conditioning yields a 35.1-point gain on queries where user profile changes the reference skill. This contrast shows that user profiles are most consequential precisely when they change skill suitability. Our website is publicly available at http://www.aiskillfeed.com .

</details>


### [208] [Regime-Aware Portfolio Management via Retrieval-Augmented LLM-Guided Expert Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28252)
*Ahmad Asadi,Reza Safabakhsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种检索增强的专家切换框架（retrieval-augmented expert-switching），用于非平稳金融市场中动态选择投资组合管理专家。该框架通过双流VAE建模资产级与市场级信息，利用检索知识库存储历史行情与专家表现，并由指令微调的LLM基于检索证据推理选择最合适的专家。实验在加密货币、股票和外汇市场中均取得最高累计收益和夏普比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场本质上具有非平稳性，单一的投资组合管理策略在不同市场条件下的有效性差异显著，固定的专家策略难以适应动态变化的市场环境，因此需要能够根据市场状况自适应切换专家的机制。

Method: 提出检索增强的专家切换框架：1）双流变分自编码器分别编码资产级和市场级信息；2）检索式知识库存储历史市场情形及对应专家的绩效记录；3）推理阶段使用指令微调的大语言模型基于检索到的证据推理并选择最合适的专家，而非直接生成投资操作；4）从理论上建立了单调性性质，证明加入局部更优的专家不会降低切换机制的表现。

Result: 在加密货币、股票和外汇三个市场中，所提选择器均获得最高的累计回报率和夏普比率。以股票市场为例，累计收益从最优固定专家的26%提升至34%，夏普比率从0.74提升至0.96。消融实验验证了检索和LLM推理两个组件的必要性，不同专家池规模的实验表明互补性专业知识具有价值。

Conclusion: 检索引导的专家切换（retrieval-grounded expert switching）是应对非平稳金融环境自适应投资组合管理的有效方法，兼具理论保障与实证优势。

Abstract: Financial markets are inherently non-stationary, making the effectiveness of individual portfolio-management strategies highly dependent on changing market conditions. This work proposes a retrieval-augmented expert-switching framework that dynamically selects portfolio management experts based on their historical performance under similar market situations. A dual-stream variational autoencoder represents asset-level and market-wide information, while a retrieval-based knowledge base stores historical situations and expert performance. During inference, an instruction-tuned LLM reasons over the retrieved evidence to identify the most appropriate expert rather than directly generating portfolio actions. We further establish a monotonicity property showing that adding a locally superior expert cannot degrade the switching mechanism's performance. Experiments across cryptocurrency, stock, and foreign-exchange markets show that the proposed selector achieves the highest cumulative return and Sharpe ratio among the evaluated selection strategies in all three markets. In the stock market, for example, cumulative return increases from 26% for the best fixed expert to 34%, while the Sharpe ratio improves from 0.74 to 0.96. Ablation results confirm the importance of both retrieval and LLM reasoning, while experiments with different expert-pool sizes demonstrate the value of complementary expertise. Overall, the findings support retrieval-grounded expert switching as an effective approach to adaptive portfolio management in non-stationary financial environments.

</details>


### [209] [Physics-Guided Flow Matching for CT Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28256)
*Davide Evangelista*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用大型强可逆流匹配模型作为CT重建中扩散模型的替代方案。在Mayo Clinic低剂量CT数据集上训练256x256胸部图像的模型中，采用两阶段训练策略（先强数据增强后弱增强微调）缓解过拟合。实验表明基于流匹配的重建方法（PnP-Flow, FlowDPS, Flower, ICTM）在PSNR、SSIM和感知质量上优于基于扩散的方法（DDRM, DPS, DiffPIR），且所需采样步数更少，具有更高的计算效率和数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然在CT逆问题重建中取得最先进性能，但其依赖随机采样过程、长推理轨迹和精细调参的噪声调度，在高分辨率场景下计算效率和数值稳定性受限。作者旨在探索流匹配（Flow Matching）作为更稳定高效的替代生成先验。

Method: 训练一个高分辨率Rectified Flow Matching模型（256x256胸片）。采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段使用强解剖学感知的数据增强防止过拟合，第二阶段减少或去除增强以精炼结构保真度。随后评估多种为流匹配设计的重建算法（Plug-and-Play Flow, FlowDPS, Flower, Flow-Priors/ICTM），并与基于扩散的重建方法（DDRM, DPS, DiffPIR）对比。模型和代码公开释放。

Result: 在多种CT逆问题设置下，基于流匹配的重建方法在PSNR、SSIM和感知质量指标上一致优于基于扩散的重建方法，且需要的采样步骤更少。

Conclusion: 流匹配为高分辨率CT图像重建提供了一种稳定、高效且有效的扩散模型替代方案，具备更好的计算效率和数值稳定性，同时能生成解剖学连贯的高质量CT图像。

Abstract: Deep generative models have recently emerged as powerful priors for solving ill-posed inverse problems in CT, with diffusion-based approaches achieving state-of-the-art reconstruction performance. However, diffusion models typically rely on stochastic sampling procedures, long inference trajectories, and carefully tuned noise schedules, which can limit computational efficiency and numerical stability, especially at high spatial resolutions. In this work, we investigate Flow Matching as an alternative generative prior for CT reconstruction. We train a high-resolution Rectified Flow Matching model on 256x256 chest images from the Mayo Clinic Low-Dose CT dataset. To mitigate overfitting and limited anatomical variability, we employ a two-stage training strategy consisting of an initial phase with strong, anatomically informed data augmentation, followed by a fine-tuning phase with reduced or no augmentation to refine structural fidelity. The resulting model is capable of generating high-quality and anatomically coherent CT-like images, serving as a strong learned prior. We then evaluate multiple reconstruction methods specifically designed for Flow Matching models, including Plug-and-Play Flow, FlowDPS, Flower, and Flow-Priors (ICTM), and compare them against state-of-the-art diffusion-based reconstruction algorithms such as DDRM, DPS, and DiffPIR. Experimental results across several CT inverse problem settings show that Flow Matching-based approaches consistently outperform diffusion-based methods in terms of PSNR, SSIM, and perceptual quality, while requiring fewer sampling steps. Finally, we publicly release the trained Flow Matching model and accompanying code to facilitate reproducibility and future research. Overall, this work demonstrates that Flow Matching provides a stable, efficient, and effective alternative to diffusion models for high-resolution CT image reconstruction.

</details>


### [210] [Finding Where the Buck Stops: An Automated Failure Attribution-Based Reflection Framework for Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28264)
*Xiaoqing Wang,Keman Huang,Bin Liang,Hongyu Li,Xiaoyong Du,Wuqiong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DoCtOR（Diagnose-then-Correct PPO-enhanced Reflection）框架，通过自动归因识别多智能体系统中的"决定性错误智能体"，仅让该智能体进行针对性反思，避免对正常执行任务的智能体造成记忆污染。在HotPotQA、ChartQAPro和Mind2Web上分别提升22%、26%和27%的成功率，优于现有反思方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型驱动的多智能体系统自我反思方法要求所有智能体对失败进行反思，但事实上失败通常源于单个特定智能体（决定性错误智能体）的错误引导，其他智能体只是正常履行职责。迫使行为正常的智能体反思会污染其记忆并引入错误见解，因此需要精确识别错误源头并仅让相关智能体反思。

Method: DoCtOR采用三步流程：（1）通过自动化失败归因识别决定性错误步骤和决定性错误智能体；（2）利用反事实推理生成修正后的决定性错误步骤；（3）仅让决定性错误智能体产生针对性反思。此外，框架结合了PPO（近端策略优化）增强来进一步优化反思过程。

Result: 在HotPotQA、ChartQAPro和Mind2Web三个数据集上，DoCtOR相比初始成功率分别提升22%、26%和27%，全面优于Reflexion、Retroformer和COPPER等基线方法。研究还验证了diagnose-then-correct范式的泛化性，并证明在低资源场景下，仅对决定性错误步骤之后的推理步骤进行反思即可达到与完整失败轨迹反思相当的效果。

Conclusion: DoCtOR通过精准定位并仅反思决定性错误智能体，避免了无关智能体的记忆污染，显著提升了多智能体系统的反思效率和任务成功率。其核心范式（先诊断后纠正）具有良好的泛化能力，且在低资源设定下仍能保持高质量反思效果。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by large language models have shown promise for complex tasks but suffer from high failure rates. Current self-reflection methods for MAS require all agents to reflect upon failure, overlooking a critical reality: failures typically stem from a specific agent leading the task astray, namely the decisive error agent, while others merely fulfill their regular duties. Forcing regular-behaving agents to reflect contaminates their memory with wrong insights. Hence, we propose DoCtOR (Diagnose-then-Correct PPO-enhanced Reflection), a novel reflection framework that enhances multi-agent collaboration. DoCtOR first identifies the decisive error step and decisive error agent through automated failure attribution, then employs counterfactual reasoning to generate a corrected decisive error step, and finally engages only the decisive error agent to produce targeted reflections. Experimental results show DoCtOR achieves 22%, 26%, and 27% improvements over initial success rates on HotPotQA, ChartQAPro, and Mind2Web datasets, outperforming Reflexion, Retroformer, and COPPER. We further establish the generalizability of our diagnose-then-correct paradigm and demonstrate that in low-resource settings, focusing reflection on reasoning steps after the decisive error step achieves comparable quality to reflecting on the complete failure trajectory.

</details>


### [211] [RECAST: Recent & Context-Aware Sampling for Test-Time Adaptation in Streaming Biosignals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28271)
*Yong-Yeon Jo,Junho Song,Joon-myoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECAST是一个轻量级的采样模块，用于流式生物信号的测试时自适应（TTA）。它根据时间新近性、上下文相似性和预测可靠性三个信号构建自适应批次，在不改变模型或训练目标的前提下，通过选择最合适的数据样本来提升血压监测的准确性和趋势追踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式生物信号存在跨受试者差异和随时间漂移的问题，人口级（population-level）训练模型在长期监测中精度会下降。虽然TTA可以在线个性化模型，但在流式数据中，哪些样本应驱动每次模型更新这一问题仍未解決。使用所有缓冲样本会引入无关片段噪声，只使用最新片段则导致更新不稳定且噪声大。理想的样本应该是近期、与当前生理状态一致且可靠的。

Method: RECAST构建每个自适应批次时结合三个信号：时间新近性（temporal recency）、上下文相似性（contextual similarity）和预测可靠性（predictive reliability）。该模块仅改变样本选择策略，不修改底层模型或训练目标，作为即插即用的采样模块嵌入现有缓冲式TTA框架中。

Result: 在两个血压数据集上，RECAST在估计精度和趋势追踪方面优于基线和消融实验。两个数据集上的逐患者增益均具有统计显著性：在常规基准上提升范围广泛，在急诊科场景中增益集中在最难的患者子集上。实用性强，在单GPU和CPU核心上每个片段仅增加亚秒级延迟。

Conclusion: RECAST通过智能样本选择解决了流式TTA中"用哪些样本更新"的关键问题。实验证明其在血压监测任务上的有效性和统计显著性，同时保持极低的计算开销，是一种实用的流式TTA改进方案。

Abstract: Streaming biosignals vary across subjects and drift over time, so population-trained models lose accuracy during long-term monitoring. Test-time adaptation (TTA) enables online personalization by updating the model on incoming samples. But in a stream, a basic question is left open: \emph{which samples should drive each update?} Using all buffered samples blurs the update with irrelevant segments. Using only the latest segment makes the update noisy and unstable. The most useful samples are recent, aligned with the current physiological state, and reliable enough to learn from. We propose \textbf{RECAST} (REcent \& Context-Aware Sampling for TTA), a lightweight sampling module for buffered TTA frameworks. RECAST builds each adaptation batch from three signals: temporal recency, contextual similarity, and predictive reliability. It changes only which samples are used, leaving the model and the training objective unchanged. On two blood-pressure datasets, RECAST improves estimation accuracy and trend tracking over baselines and ablations. The per-patient gains are statistically significant on both datasets, with broad improvement on the regular benchmark and gains concentrated on the hardest patients in the emergency-department setting. RECAST stays practical, adding only sub-second latency per segment on a single GPU and CPU core.

</details>


### [212] [LoopArena: Benchmarking Models as Runtime Controllers for Loop Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28281)
*Yi Wang,Haopeng Zhang,Chengxiang Huang,Rui Dai,Kaikui Liu,Piotr Koniusz,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoopArena是一个用于评估"控制器"模型如何引导独立编码代理完成长时任务的基准测试，区分了循环策略与编码代理能力的贡献。在完整任务上最佳严格成功率仅24.69%，平均推理成本降低64.4%，Type II与Type III排序高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环工程（Loop Engineering）逐渐成为围绕编码代理组织开发工作的实践方式，但一次端到端运行的结果无法区分成功或失败究竟源自循环的指导还是编码代理的执行能力。因此需要一个基准来单独评估"控制器"的引导能力。

Method: 提出LoopArena基准，将被评估的模型设为Controller，在每轮编码后接收结构化运行摘要，并指示一个固定的Worker代理下一步做什么或验证什么，或决定是否停止。提供三种互补评估设置：Type I通过执行验证的问题打分，无需在评估时运行Worker；Type II在完整任务的选定片段上执行重复控制；Type III从原始状态评估完整任务配对。

Result: 在完整任务上，最佳严格成功率为24.69%；不同Controller之间的配对推理成本平均降低64.4%；Type II在主要Core准则下与Type III产生相似排序（Spearman相关系数ρ=0.9747）。基准数据和评估代码已公开。

Conclusion: LoopArena提供了一个系统化评估循环控制能力的基准，结果表明当前模型在长时循环控制方面仍有较大改进空间，同时证实了便宜的Type II设置可以作为完整任务评估的高保真近似。

Abstract: Loop Engineering is emerging as a practice for organizing development work around coding agents. Instead of writing each prompt by hand, practitioners design loops that monitor progress, assign work, run checks, and decide what the agent should do next. Even with a capable coding agent, a loop may trust a stale progress note, skip needed verification, spend its budget in the wrong direction, or stop before the task is safe to submit. Yet the final outcome of one end-to-end run cannot tell whether success or failure reflects the loop's guidance or the coding agent's ability to carry out the task. We introduce LoopArena, a benchmark for evaluating how well one model can guide a separate coding agent through a long-running task. The model under evaluation is the \textbf{Controller}: after each coding round, it receives a structured summary of the run and instructs a separate, fixed coding agent, the \textbf{Worker}, on what to do or verify next, or decides whether to stop. LoopArena evaluates this ability in three complementary settings that differ in execution scope and cost. Type I scores next-step Loop Contract selection through execution-validated questions without running the Worker at evaluation time. Type II executes repeated control over a selected slice of a full task, while Type III evaluates the paired full task from its original state. On full tasks, the best observed Strict Success Rate is \textbf{24.69\%}, leaving substantial room for improvement in long-horizon loop control. Across Controllers, the paired reduction in estimated inference cost averages \textbf{64.4\%}, and Type II produces a similar ordering under the main Core criterion (Spearman's \(ρ=\textbf{0.9747}\)). We release the benchmark data and evaluation code at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/LoopArena .

</details>


### [213] [Memristive-Friendly Hadamard Reservoir Computing: Structured, Multiplier-Free Recurrences at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28295)
*Andrea Ceni,Gianluca Milano,Carlo Ricciardi,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用结构化正交算子（由符号对角线、置换和快速Walsh-Hadamard变换构成）替代储层计算（RC）中昂贵的稠密循环矩阵。该算子无乘法器、需要O(N)参数和O(N log N)运算，在标准和忆阻友好型Echo State Network中均可实例化，且在20个分类和7个回归基准上达到与稠密正交储层相当的性能，循环步在硬件上比稠密乘积快高达50倍、内存小10^4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存储计算（Reservoir Computing）依赖固定的循环层，是神经形态硬件的自然候选。忆阻友好型储层虽从忆阻器件动力学导出神经元动态，但仍依赖物理实现昂贵的稠密循环矩阵。作者旨在用结构化正交算子替代稠密矩阵，以降低硬件实现成本和计算开销。

Method: 将稠密循环矩阵替换为结构化正交算子，由符号对角线、一个置换和快速Walsh-Hadamard变换构成。该算子无乘法器、需O(N)参数和O(N log N)每步运算，从不显式物化为矩阵。作者将其实例化在标准及忆阻友好型Echo State Network中，每个单元仅用一个二进制输入连接。

Result: 数学分析表明精确正交性产生紧致的回声状态条件（在循环缩放上）和设计时可预测的噪声响应。在20个分类和7个回归基准（储层大小至N=8192）上的实验显示，结构化模型匹配稠密正交储层，且比循环储层平均性能更优，差距随规模增大。循环步在三个硬件平台上比稠密乘积快最多50倍，内存小10^4倍。同时进行了算子的消融实验，并测量了对噪声、量化、器件失配和离散故障的响应。

Conclusion: 结构化正交算子为储层计算提供了一种高效、可扩展且硬件友好的替代方案，在保持精确正交性（带来紧致回声状态条件和可预测噪声响应）的同时大幅降低计算和存储成本，有望推动神经形态硬件上大规模储层计算的实用化。

Abstract: Reservoir Computing (RC) designs Recurrent Neural Networks around a fixed, i.e., untrained, recurrent layer, and is a natural candidate for neuromorphic hardware. Memristive-friendly reservoirs derive the neuron dynamics from memristive-device kinetics, but still rely on dense recurrent matrices, which are expensive to realize physically. In this paper, we replace the dense matrix with a structured orthogonal operator, built from sign diagonals, a permutation, and a fast Walsh-Hadamard transform. The operator is multiplier-free, requires $O(N)$ parameters and $O(N\log N)$ operations per step, and is never materialized as a matrix. We instantiate it in a standard and in a memristive-friendly Echo State Network, with one binary input connection per unit.
  Our mathematical analysis shows that exact orthogonality yields an echo state condition that is tight in the recurrent scaling, and a noise response that is predictable at design time. Moreover, the operator mixes the whole state in a single application. Experiments on twenty classification and seven regression benchmarks, at reservoir sizes up to $N = 8192$, show that the structured models match dense orthogonal reservoirs, and achieve better mean performance than the cycle reservoir by a margin that widens with size. Furthermore, we time the recurrent step on three hardware platforms, where it is up to $50\times$ faster than a dense product and $10^4\times$ smaller in memory. Finally, we ablate the operator and measure the response to noise, quantization, device mismatch and discrete faults.

</details>


### [214] [MAIL: Memory-driven, Adaptive, Incremental, and Literature-grounded Framework for Hypothesis Generation in Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28315)
*Mahdi Babaei,Xueshen Li,Yutao Kuang,Jolene P. Reid,Yu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAIL框架，一种用于化学领域自动假设生成的记忆增强、自适应、增量式且基于文献的方法。MAIL将假设生成建模为时间驱动的记忆推理过程，在公开TOMATO-Chem数据集和新建的高新颖性HN-NS数据集上均取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学文献量剧增为产生新假设提供了机会，但现有LLM方法依赖静态灵感语料、预定义启发式规则或繁琐的人工参与流程，限制了可扩展性和新颖性。

Method: MAIL框架将假设生成形式化为时间驱动的记忆推理过程：通过持续积累和重新解读先前知识的演化概念路径来生成假设，整合记忆增强、自适应、增量学习以及文献基础四种机制。

Result: 在TOMATO-Chem和新构建的HN-NS数据集上，MAIL生成结构连贯且机制合理的假设，在两个指标（MIOS和MPOS）上均达到最高，能更有效地恢复历史目标假设的核心思想和方法要素，并获得最高的专家评估科学质量得分。

Conclusion: 结果表明LLM具备自主探索化学领域并生成既具创新性又化学上合理的假设的潜力。

Abstract: The ever-expanding volume of the chemical literature offers unprecedented opportunities to generate novel and impactful hypotheses. However, the bottleneck lies in efficiently navigating this vast knowledge base to formulate high-quality, experimentally meaningful insights.
  While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for this task, existing methods often rely on static inspiration corpora, predefined heuristics, or laborious human-in-the-loop pipelines and decision-support frameworks that limit scalability and novelty. In this work, we propose an automated approach, a Memory-augmented, Adaptive, Incremental, and Literature-grounded (MAIL) framework for hypothesis generation in chemistry. Our MAIL method formulates hypothesis generation as a temporally grounded, memory-driven reasoning process, where hypotheses emerge from an evolving conceptual path that continuously accumulates and reinterprets prior knowledge. We evaluated the MAIL framework on a public TOMATO-Chem dataset and a newly curated and disseminated high-novelty nature/science challenge (HN-NS) dataset. Across both datasets, MAIL generates structurally coherent and mechanistically plausible hypotheses, achieves the highest MIOS and MPOS by more effectively recovering the central ideas and methodological elements of the historical target hypotheses, and obtains the highest overall expert-evaluation scores for scientific quality. These results demonstrate the potential of LLMs to autonomously explore chemical domains and generate hypotheses that are both innovative and chemically plausible.

</details>


### [215] [Real-Valued Hyperdimensional Sequence Representations with Hadamard Product Binding and Shift Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28334)
*Kenny Schlegel,Dmitri A. Rachkovskij,Denis Kleyko,Amy Loutfi,Stefan Streif,Evgeny Osipov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三种基于随机傅里叶特征的实值位置编码（逆傅里叶基线、正弦变体和仅余弦表示），用于超维计算中的序列表示，在保持分数幂编码的核心性质（如相似性保持和位移等变性）的同时，支持实数向量的Hadamard积绑定操作。Sinusoid变体提供了显式代数移位算子，可在向量域直接进行时间位移，无需重新编码序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的分数幂编码主要支持循环卷积或复数乘法等绑定操作，与实数向量上的Hadamard积绑定不兼容。这限制了超维计算在需要高效、实数域运算场景下的序列表示能力。

Method: 基于随机傅里叶特征设计三种实值位置编码变体：1) 基于逆傅里叶变换的实值基线；2) 从RFF推导出的Sinusoid表示，提供显式代数移位算子；3) 仅余弦表示。这些编码在保留分数幂编码的相似性保持与位移等变性的同时，支持Hadamard积绑定。

Result: 在时间序列分类数据集上的实验表明，所提出的实值表示在性能上与标准分数幂编码相当，同时实现了计算高效的Hadamard积绑定。其中Sinusoid变体提供了最佳权衡，兼具高效实值实现与精确的位移等变变换。

Conclusion: 实值位置编码成功弥补了分数幂编码与Hadamard积绑定之间的兼容性缺口，Sinusoid变体是最有前景的方案，为序列表示中超维计算提供了兼顾效率与功能性的选择。

Abstract: Encoding temporal order is a fundamental requirement for sequence representations in Hyperdimensional Computing. Fractional Power Encoding provides similarity-preserving position vectors whose inner products approximate shift-invariant kernels, and it supports shift-equivariant transformations of encoded sequence representations. However, standard formulations of Fractional Power Encoding are primarily designed for binding operations such as circular convolution or complex-valued multiplication, which limits their compatibility with Hadamard product binding of real-valued vectors. This paper develops real-valued position encodings motivated by Random Fourier Features, aiming to retain the desirable properties of Fractional Power Encoding while supporting Hadamard-based operations. We propose three real-valued position-encoding variants: a real-valued baseline based on the inverse Fourier transform, and Sinusoid and Cosine-only representations derived from Random Fourier Features. Among them, the Sinusoid variant provides an explicit algebraic shift operator, allowing temporal shifts to be applied directly to the vector-encoded sequence representation without re-encoding the shifted sequence. Experiments on time-series classification datasets show that the proposed real-valued representations achieve performance comparable to standard Fractional Power Encoding while enabling computationally efficient Hadamard product binding. The Sinusoid variant offers the most favorable trade-off, combining efficient real-valued implementation with exact shift-equivariant transformations.

</details>


### [216] [AGENT-O: A Semantic Agent Card Framework for Interoperable and Governed Healthcare AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28345)
*Pengze Li,Cui Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGENT-O是一个模块化本体框架，用于定义面向健康领域AI智能体系统的语义Agent Card，并通过自动化流程评估科学出版物中的报告完整性。该本体包含1,962条RDF三元组和1,922条Protege公理，在279篇论文的评估中发现运行时/架构、治理/安全、溯源/可复现性报告最不完整，而评估和基准流程一致性报告相对较好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在健康领域AI智能体系统快速发展的背景下，缺乏标准化的语义表示框架来刻画这类系统，且科学出版物中关于智能体系统的报告完整性参差不齐，亟需一个能同时支持语义表示和报告完整性评估的本体框架。

Method: 基于OWL 2/RDF构建模块化本体，覆盖运行时、模型、工作流、工具、临床应用、评估、溯源、治理和报告评估等维度；使用OWL-RL推理、三套SHACL约束套件和12个SPARQL能力查询进行验证；通过模型辅助的报告完整性评估方法对279篇论文在五个维度上进行评分。

Result: 本体包含1,962条RDF三元组和1,922条Protege公理，252个活动类、198个活动对象属性和51个数据类型属性；所有SHACL套件在示例图上正确，所有能力查询返回预期证据，279篇论文全部完成评分。报告缺失率最高的维度为运行时/架构(84.6%)、治理/安全(82.8%)和溯源/可复现性(78.1%)，最低为评估(25.8%)和基准流程一致性(29.8%)。

Conclusion: AGENT-O提供了可复用的本体、语义Agent Card配置文件和报告完整性评估工作流，支持结构化报告和差距识别；同时揭示了评估-规范差距：评估和基准流程的报告比运行时架构、治理和可复现性更一致。但该框架不评估智能体质量或部署就绪度。

Abstract: AGENT-O is a modular ontology framework that defines a semantic Agent Card for representing health-oriented AI agent systems and supports assessment of reporting completeness in scientific publications. AGENT-O was developed as an OWL 2/RDF ontology covering runtime, models, workflow, tools, clinical use, evaluation, provenance, governance, and reporting assessment. Evaluation included ontology inventory, OWL-RL reasoning, three SHACL suites, 12 SPARQL competency queries, three cases, and model-assisted reporting-completeness assessment of 279 papers across five dimensions. The ontology contained 1,962 RDF triples and 1,922 Protege axioms, with 252 active classes, 198 active object properties, and 51 datatype properties. All SHACL suites conformed on example graphs, all competency queries returned prespecified evidence, and all 279 papers were scored. Incomplete reporting was highest for runtime/architecture (84.6%), governance/safety (82.8%), and provenance/reproducibility (78.1%), compared with evaluation (25.8%) and benchmark-process alignment (29.8%). AGENT-O supported semantic Agent Card representation and reporting assessment while revealing an evaluation-specification gap: evaluation and benchmark procedures were reported more consistently than runtime architecture, governance, and reproducibility. AGENT-O provides a reusable ontology, semantic Agent Card profile, and reporting-completeness workflow for structured reporting and gap identification, but does not assess agent quality or deployment readiness.

</details>


### [217] [Propagating construction-time knowledge quality into medical question answering: A framework grounded in clinical guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28360)
*Jie Hu,Junjie Wang,Shan Lu,Yifang Hu,Gong Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种质量感知框架，将临床指南知识图谱构建阶段的三元组质量信号（结构符合度SchemaConf与证据支持度EvidScore融合为Q(t)）持久化到图中，在下游子图检索和证据提示中传播该质量信息，弥合了构建期质量控制和推理期证据使用之间的鸿沟。在中国糖尿病临床指南上的实验表明，该框架显著降低知识遗漏和冲突输出，提升证据可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型从临床指南构建知识图谱时提取的三元组在结构有效性和证据支持度上参差不齐，而现有图增强问答系统在检索时仅优化查询相关性，未能复用知识图谱构建阶段产生的质量信息，导致构建期质量控制与推理期证据使用之间存在割裂。

Method: 提出质量感知框架：(1) 建模结构符合度(SchemaConf)与证据支持度(EvidScore)两个互补维度，融合为逐三元组质量信号Q(t)；(2) 将Q(t)及质量等级作为图属性持久化，而非仅用于过滤；(3) 在子图检索中加权传播质量信息，在证据提示中依据质量等级进行条件化提示；(4) 保留段落级溯源以保证可核查性。

Result: 在中国糖尿病临床指南上的实验显示：融合后的Q(t)在三元组质量判别上优于任一单独维度（AUC 0.748 vs. EvidScore 0.703 vs. SchemaConf 0.645）；在指南问答中，构建期质量的传播使必需知识遗漏从16.3%降至5.3%，冲突输出从16.3%降至2.7%，证据基于精确率达81.6%，无效引用接近零；盲法临床医生评分中，完整框架（4.68）优于无检索基线（4.21），接近上界oracle条件（4.80）；跨生成器实验呈现一致趋势。

Conclusion: 构建期三元组质量可作为下游证据选择与呈现的持久信号，其效用具有分布依赖性；在跨版本和跨模型偏移下，融合质量信号表现最为稳健。该框架有效弥合了知识图谱构建质量控制与推理期证据使用之间的断点。

Abstract: Large language models have facilitated knowledge graph (KG) construction from clinical guidelines, but extracted triples vary in structural validity and evidential support. Meanwhile, graph-augmented question answering (QA) systems typically optimize query relevance during retrieval, with limited reuse of quality information produced during KG construction. This creates a disconnect between construction-time quality control and inference-time evidence use. We investigate whether construction-time triple quality can serve as a persistent signal for downstream evidence selection and presentation. We propose a quality-aware framework that models structural conformance (SchemaConf) and evidential support (EvidScore) as complementary dimensions and fuses them into a per-triple quality signal, Q(t). Rather than using quality solely for filtering, the framework retains Q(t) and derived quality tiers as graph attributes and propagates them into quality-weighted subgraph retrieval and tier-conditioned evidence prompting, while preserving passage-level provenance. Experiments on Chinese diabetes clinical guidelines show that the utility of the quality signal is distribution dependent. Under cross-version and cross-model shift, the fused Q(t) provides stronger triple-quality discrimination than either component alone (AUC 0.748 vs. 0.703 for EvidScore and 0.645 for SchemaConf). In guideline-grounded QA, propagating construction-time quality reduces required-knowledge omission from 16.3% to 5.3% and conflicting outputs from 16.3% to 2.7%, with an evidence-grounded precision of 81.6% and near-zero invalid citations. Blinded clinician ratings favor the full framework over no retrieval (4.68 vs. 4.21 on a five-point scale) and approach the oracle condition (4.80), while cross-generator experiments show consistent trends.

</details>


### [218] [GRACE:Gradient-guided Coreset Selection for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28361)
*Praveen Bushipaka,Andrea D'Angelo,Lucia Passaro,Tommaso Cucinotta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE，一种梯度引导的核心集选择方法，用于大型语言模型的机器遗忘任务。GRACE从少数种子示例中推断遗忘集和保留集，通过梯度方向匹配选择紧凑的遗忘核心集，并在投影掉遗忘方向后选择保留示例，在保持遗忘质量的同时提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘方法通常假设遗忘集和保留集是预先指定的，但在实际场景中用户往往只提供少量不良行为的示例，需要从异构语料库中推断遗忘集和保留集。作者旨在解决这一数据选择问题。

Method: GRACE采用两阶段方法：(1)从引发不良行为的种子示例计算遗忘方向，利用非负正交匹配追踪选择紧凑的遗忘核心集，使其梯度近似该方向；(2)在投影掉遗忘方向后的剩余梯度空间中，利用聚类正交匹配追踪选择保留示例，以保持模型效用。

Result: 在两个目标领域、两个模型家族和四种遗忘算法上，GRACE在保持可比遗忘质量的同时提升了模型效用，特别是在与先前的基于梯度的选择方法相比，表现出一致性的改进。

Conclusion: GRACE验证了从少量种子示例推断遗忘集和保留集的有效性，表明梯度引导的核心集选择方法能够在LLM遗忘任务中同时兼顾遗忘质量和模型效用的保持。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning methods for Large Language Models typically assume pre-specified forget and retain sets. In realistic settings, however, requests may provide only a few examples of undesired behavior, requiring forget and retain sets to be inferred from heterogeneous corpora. We study this data-selection problem and propose GRACE , a gradient-guided coreset selection method that constructs both forget and retain sets for LLM unlearning. GRACE first computes a forget direction from seed examples that elicit the undesired behavior, then selects a compact forget coreset whose gradients approximate this direction using non-negative orthogonal matching pursuit. To preserve model utility, it selects retain examples after projecting out the forget direction and applying clustered orthogonal matching pursuit in the remaining gradient space. Across two target domains, two model families, and four unlearning algorithms, GRACE improves model utility while maintaining comparable forget quality, with particularly consistent gains over prior gradient-based selection methods.

</details>


### [219] [EvoUndo: Recoverability-Constrained Self-Evolution for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28363)
*Tanmay Sah,Dolly Sah,Harshul Jain,Tanya Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoUndo框架，用于表示、综合、诊断和独立验证LLM智能体自我修改（self-evolution）的可恢复性。在600个任务中发现197个能力提升但无法恢复的突变，通过扩展恢复演算、精确状态地址定位和协议锁定实验，将恢复率从0/197提升到191/197，揭示可靠的智能体自我进化需要协同设计验证、状态接地、见证语义和恢复语言表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在运行时越来越多地修改自身提示词、工具、中间件、资源和执行框架。这种自我进化能提升能力，但成功的突变可能留下持久影响，在与其创建状态不同的状态中无法安全逆转，导致不可恢复的风险。现有方法依赖迭代提示（iterative prompting）难以解决这一问题。

Method: 提出EvoUndo框架，包含四个组件：(1) 表示（representing）自我修改及其恢复机制；(2) 综合（synthesizing）恢复策略；(3) 诊断（diagnosing）恢复失败原因；(4) 独立验证（independently verifying）跨反事实状态的可恢复性。通过扩展恢复演算（recovery calculus）增强语言表达能力，并使用协议锁定的2x2接地-表达性干预实验来分离两类瓶颈：状态地址接地（state-address grounding）和恢复语言表达性（expressivity）。

Result: 在600个未见过的一次性自我进化任务中，识别出197个能力提升但无法恢复的自然失败。传统修复策略恢复0/197；确定性oracle分析在原语言L0下恢复48/197，扩展恢复演算将经验oracle恢复提升到191/197。在协议锁定2x2实验中，当原语言充分时，精确状态地址接地使恢复从0/48提升到38/48（79.2%）；扩展语言在S1层使142/143（99.3%）失败得以恢复。在gpt-oss-120b主干上，丰富语言中添加精确地址诊断使恢复降至133/143（93.0%），该负交互在Qwen3.8-27B上未复现，表明其依赖模型。

Conclusion: 可靠的智能体自我进化需要协同设计验证机制、状态接地、见证语义和恢复语言表达能力，而非仅依赖迭代提示。扩展恢复语言和精确状态接地是恢复能力的关键因素，但某些负交互效应具有模型依赖性，提示需要针对不同模型定制化地权衡恢复框架的各组件。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly modify their own prompts, tools, middleware, resources, and execution harnesses at runtime. Such self-evolution can improve capability, but a successful mutation may leave persistent effects that cannot be safely reversed in states different from the one in which it was created. We introduce EvoUndo, a framework for representing, synthesizing, diagnosing, and independently verifying recoverability of model-generated self-modifications across counterfactual states. Across 600 unseen one-shot self-evolution tasks, we identify 197 capability-improving mutations that fail recoverability verification. Under the original recovery representation, conventional repair strategies recover 0/197 of these natural failures. Deterministic oracle analysis recovers 48/197 under the original recovery language L0, while the extended recovery calculus increases empirical oracle recovery to 191/197. A protocol-locked 2x2 grounding-by-expressivity intervention then separates two bottlenecks: exact state-address grounding increases successful recovery from 0/48 to 38/48 (79.2%) when the original language is sufficient, while extending the recovery language enables recovery on 142/143 (99.3%) failures in the oracle-defined S1 stratum. On the primary gpt-oss-120b backbone, adding exact-address diagnostics to the richer language reduces recovery to 133/143 (93.0%); a Qwen3.8-27B replication preserves the grounding and expressivity effects but not this negative interaction, indicating that the latter is model-dependent. These results indicate that reliable agent self-evolution requires co-designing verification, state grounding, witness semantics, and recovery-language expressivity rather than relying on iterative prompting alone.

</details>


### [220] [MAP: A Benchmark on Multimodal Accessibility Planning for Real World Places](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28384)
*Jason Armitage,Ioannis Tsochantaridis,Linda Mazzone,Chuqiao Yan,Srini Narayanan,Sarah Ebling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MAP，这是首个用于评估多模态AI系统作为无障碍需求用户规划实地访问助手时的基准测试。它包含两项新颖的评估任务：一是对地点及无障碍声明进行核验并推荐符合要求的地点，二是检索地点与无障碍特征对应的视觉证据。基准支持定时刷新地面真值数据，结合自动评分与人工评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无障碍需求用户（如轮椅使用者、视障人士等）在规划实地出行时，需要依赖AI系统验证或推荐符合其无障碍要求的地点。然而，现有的多模态AI评估基准缺乏专门针对这一场景的评估框架。此外，地点信息和无障碍设施可能随时间变化，需要一种能够适应这种动态变化的评估方法。

Method: MAP基准包含两项评估任务：(1) 声明核验——评估系统能否判断关于地点及其无障碍特征的信息是否得到支持，并识别满足所需无障碍特征的地点；(2) 视觉证据检索——评估多模态AI系统能否为指定的地点和无障碍特征选择相应的视觉证据。基准采用定时刷新地面真值数据的机制以适应信息变化，并采用自动评分与人工评分相结合的方式对系统响应进行评估，以支持系统间的横向比较。

Result: MAP作为首个针对无障碍出行规划场景的多模态AI基准测试，提供了声明核验和视觉证据检索两项评估任务，并通过可定时刷新的地面真值数据和混合评分机制（自动+人工）实现了对系统在动态信息环境下的可靠性评估。

Conclusion: MAP填补了多模态AI在无障碍出行规划评估领域的空白，其动态更新机制和混合评分方法为评估AI系统在此类真实场景中的表现提供了可复现且可比较的标准化框架。

Abstract: We introduce MAP, the first benchmark to evaluate multimodal AI systems as assistants for users with accessibility requirements when planning visits to places in the real world. In our evaluation, systems are presented with requests to verify or recommend a point of interest meeting an accessibility requirement. MAP contains two novel assessments: Claim verification for accessibility planning assesses if information on places and stated accessibility features is supported and identifies places that satisfy requested accessibility features. Visual evidence retrieval for accessibility planning checks if a multimodal AI system can select visual evidence for the requested place and accessibility feature. Our methodology supports comparison of AI systems in a setting where place information and accessibility information can change over time by evaluating systems and refreshing ground truth data at scheduled times. The benchmark is based on automatic rating and human rating for a proportion of responses.

</details>


### [221] [Timing-Aware Repurchase Prediction for Web-Scale E-Commerce: Survival Models for Multi-Surface Grocery Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28393)
*Akshay Kekuda,Shreeranjani Srirangamsridharan,Ishan Bhatt,Yanan Cao,Sinduja Subramaniam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kaushiki Nag,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将电子商务中的复购推荐从"W天内是否复购"的二元分类问题重构为直接预测复购时间的生存分析（AFT）模型。在大型生鲜电商平台的数百万用户和30余种消融配置上验证，发现边际风险略递减（k≈0.9），Log-Normal排名最佳而Exponential校准最优（ECE~1e-4），单个AFT模型可替代三个不同时间窗的二元分类器且树数量减少3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复购推荐采用"W天内是否购买"的二元分类范式，每个预测时间窗都需要单独训练模型，无法直接建模复购时间本身。这导致模型堆叠冗余、时间窗之间缺乏一致性，且难以捕捉复购行为的时序特性。

Method: 使用生存分析中的加速失效时间（AFT）模型直接预测复购时间；对风险函数进行经验估计以判断危险率趋势；比较Weibull、Log-Normal、Exponential等AFT分布族；引入4参数参数化校准将原始生存CDF映射为各时间窗概率；在超过30种消融配置中对模型进行系统评估。

Result:  (1) 经验风险分析显示边际风险略递减（k≈0.9），不同于常见直觉；Log-Normal边际拟合最佳（R²=0.998）且排名最佳。(2) 单个AFT模型以约3x更少的树数量匹配或超越三个独立二元分类器；特征重要性在生存目标下重新排布。(3) Exponential AFT校准误差（ECE~1e-4）比Log-Normal低约10倍，排名指标差异在0.3%以内。

Conclusion: 提出单模型生存分析框架替代多时间窗二元分类器堆叠，揭示了校准与排名在同一AFT家族内部的内在权衡：Exponential适用于概率消费场景，Log-Normal适用于纯排名场景。这一框架在效率、可解释性和跨时间窗一致性上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Repurchase recommenders in e-commerce are commonly framed as a binary question asking "will this customer buy this item within W days", a formulation that requires a separately trained model for every horizon of interest. We replace this stack with survival models that predict time-to-repurchase directly, and evaluate them on millions of customers from a major grocery e-commerce platform across more than thirty ablation configurations. Our study makes three contributions. First, an empirical hazard analysis reveals a slightly decreasing marginal hazard (k ~ 0.9), differing from the common intuition that grocery items become more likely to be repurchased the longer since the last purchase (increasing hazard, k > 1). Log-Normal achieves the best marginal fit (R^2 = 0.998) and the best ranking, despite Weibull providing the best conditional residual fit, revealing an apparent discrepancy we analyze in detail. Second, a single Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model replaces three per-horizon binary classifiers, matching or exceeding each at its own horizon while using roughly 3x fewer total trees. Feature importance reshuffles under the survival objective: channel-cadence and recency signals rise while aggregate frequency counts fall. Third, a 4-parameter parametric calibration maps raw survival CDFs to per-horizon probabilities with zero cross-horizon monotonicity violations. Calibration quality varies by an order of magnitude across the AFT family: Exponential AFT (Weibull k=1) achieves expected calibration error (ECE) ~1e-4, roughly 10x lower than Log-Normal, while ranking metrics agree within 0.3% relative. We adopt Exponential AFT for probability-consuming surfaces and Log-Normal for pure ranking, exposing a principled calibration-ranking trade-off within a single AFT family.

</details>


### [222] [RetailAgent: Structured Adverse Timing in Self-Conditioned Multimodal LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28399)
*Yupeng Zhang,Liuyuan Jiang,Hongyi Huang,Bingheng Li,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过RetailAgent实验框架研究了LLM智能体在金融市场的序贯决策是否存在可预测的方向性结构。研究发现LLM在做多/空仓决策时表现出持续性负向择时，且该效应在不同模态、时间跨度、状态和模型家族中均稳定存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融市场中，对价格变动做出系统性反应的序贯策略可能被其他市场参与者预测。现有研究较少关注LLM智能体在金融决策中是否存在此类可预测的方向性结构，这对理解AI参与的市场的可预测性和行为信号具有重要意义。

Method: 构建RetailAgent实验框架：LLM观察匿名化的日内股价历史及允许的状态，反复选择做多（持有多头）或空仓（不参与），随后揭示区间收益。采用暴露匹配度量——剔除整体做多比例后，比较同一股票日内路径中做多区间与空仓区间的收益，并通过对已保存行动序列进行打乱来验证行动与后续收益之间的对齐效应，同时测试加入自撰记忆对决策的影响。

Result: 发现持续的负向择时效应，该效应在模态、时间跨度、状态和模型家族间均一致存在；打乱行动序列会显著削弱该效应，表明行动与后续收益的对齐驱动了负向得分；使用自撰记忆会增加政策持续性，在同时使用两种行动的股票日中择时变得更负。

Conclusion: LLM的序贯金融决策中存在稳定、可恢复的方向性结构，该研究提供了一种行为信号，可用于研究其他参与者如何应对可预测的策略。

Abstract: In financial markets, a sequential policy that reacts systematically to price movements may become predictable to other market participants. This paper studies whether large language model (LLM) agents exhibit such directional structure through RetailAgent, an experimental framework in which an LLM observes anonymized intraday equity price histories and permitted state, then repeatedly chooses long (hold the stock) or flat (stay out) before the subsequent interval return is revealed. We compare returns during long and flat intervals along the same stock's intraday path after removing the overall fraction of long decisions. This exposure-matched measure reveals persistent negative timing across modality, horizon, state, and model family. Shuffling saved action sequences substantially attenuates the effect, showing that alignment between actions and subsequent returns drives the negative score. Feeding self-authored memories into decisions further increases policy persistence, while timing becomes more negative among stock-days on which the agent uses both actions. These results reveal stable, recoverable directional structure in sequential LLM financial decisions and a behavioral signal for studying how another participant could respond to a predictable policy.

</details>


### [223] [VERA-8B: Evidence-Grounded Audit Risk Reasoning from SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28402)
*Menghan Liu,Elynn Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VERA-8B，一个端到端的审计推理系统，通过统一SFT和GRPO实现基于证据的审计风险预测，并引入弃权机制和AuditBridge将原始文件转化为可审计、可审阅的报告，解决标准金融语言模型在审计场景中缺乏证据支撑的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准金融语言模型以流畅性而非证据性为优先，可能产生看似合理但模糊的答案，存在"接地缺口"（grounding gap），无法满足审计工作中对证据支撑的严格要求。审计判断必须由合理证据支持，因此需要专门针对审计场景的推理系统。

Method: 构建VERA-8B端到端审计推理系统，主要包含三个核心组件：(1) 统一SFT（监督微调）与GRPO（组相对策略优化）来训练证据接地的审计推理模型；(2) 引入弃权（abstention）和不确定性限定机制，在证据不充分或不确定的情况下拒绝作答；(3) 设计AuditBridge模块，将原始申报文件转换为经过验证的记录，再生成审阅人可直接使用的报告，实现金融与计算的桥接。

Result: VERA-8B在预执法审计预测任务上超越了所有评估的基线方法，实现了优于现有方法的性能表现。

Conclusion: VERA-8B通过证据接地、弃权机制和AuditBridge三个组件的协同，能够生成可审计、可审阅、适用于实际审计工作的输出，填补了审计领域缺乏专门ML预测系统的空白。

Abstract: Across audit applications, judgments must be supported by reasonable evidence. However, standard financial language models prioritize fluency over evidence. They are built for general financial reasoning and may produce plausible but ambiguous answers, creating a grounding gap that makes them unsuitable for audit work. We address this gap with VERA-8B, a new end-to-end audit reasoning system that identifies audit risks before enforcement actions occur. Constructing such a model raises several challenges, as no prior machine learning work targets pre-enforcement audit prediction. To our knowledge, we are the first to unify SFT and GRPO for evidence-grounded audit reasoning under one evidence standard, achieving performance that surpasses all evaluated baselines. Because auditing cannot tolerate unsupported claims, we introduce abstention and uncertainty qualification to defer uncertain or evidence-incomplete cases. Finally, we design an AuditBridge to ground model reasoning for practical audit work. It transforms raw filings into verified records and then into reviewer-ready reports, bridging finance and computation with broad generality. Together, these components produce auditable, review-ready outputs suitable for practical audit work.

</details>


### [224] [Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards: Symbolic Backpropagation for Post-Training LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28421)
*Vishvesh Bhat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PLVR（可验证奖励的程序学习），一种将推理能力从模型权重中移出、以显式程序形式置于模型外部的后训练方法。PLVR使用符号反向传播，通过类型推理在程序结构上进行稠密逐步骤信用分配，在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上以30B基座模型超越同预算RL平均27.8分，并超越大一个数量级的前沿模型13.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后训练（SFT和RL）将推理能力固化在模型权重中，导致能力不可检查、不可逐步验证、且无法迁移到其他模型。作者主张对于中间步骤可验证的任务，推理应作为由确定性与神经原语组成的显式程序置于模型权重之外，以获得可解释性、可验证性和可复用性。

Method: PLVR从输入-输出示例直接学习显式程序。其核心机制是符号反向传播：每个程序层携带类型化本体，在输出端计算损失，然后通过原语签名的类型推断将所需的输入本体反向传播——这是链式法则的类比物，其中信用分配是推导过程而非估计过程。与RLVR验证终端结果不同，PLVR的奖励是逐步骤契约判定，在程序结构上是稠密的。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B基座模型用PLVR在同等预算下超越RL平均27.8个点，超越大一个数量级的前沿模型13.6个点。单一原语库服务两个基准，新任务的边际成本仅为100个程序搜索示例且无需新的微调数据。消融实验显示，用均匀采样替代损失引导搜索后，中位数程序从65.6降至17.5，证明反向传播而非类型系统是优势来源。作者还发布了符号反向传播库和一致性检查器。

Conclusion: 推理能力可以且应当从模型权重中外部化为显式程序。PLVR证明了符号反向传播作为密度奖励机制的有效性，其模块化设计（共享原语库+程序搜索）大幅降低了新任务的后训成本，为可验证、可迁移、可解释的推理能力构建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Post training a language model to reason means updating its weights. Supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning both place the acquired capability inside the model where it cannot be inspected cannot be checked step by step and cannot be moved to another model. We argue that for tasks whose intermediate steps admit verification, reasoning is better placed outside the base models weights as an explicit program composed from deterministic and neural primitives. We introduce PLVR (Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards): a post training method that learns such programs directly from input-output examples. Its mechanism is symbolic backpropagation: each program layer carries a typed ontology a loss is computed at the output against ground truth and required input ontologies are propagated backward by type inference over primitive signatures: an analogue of the chain rule in which credit assignment is a derivation rather than an estimate. Where RLVR verifies a terminal outcome, PLVRs reward is a per step contract verdict dense over program structure. On LiveCodeBench v6 and Tau2Bench, 30B base models with PLVR outperform RL at matched budget by 27.8 points on average and frontier models an order of magnitude larger by 13.6 points. A single primitive library serves two benchmarks, so the marginal cost of a new task is 100 examples of program search and no new finetuning data. Replacing the loss guided search with uniform sampling over the same type admissible space at equal budget collapses the median program from 65.6 to 17.5, identifying the backward pass rather than the type system as the source of the advantage. We release the symbolic backpropagation library and a conformance checker so the method can be applied to primitive libraries other than our own.

</details>


### [225] [Prove2Me: An Open Collaborative Platform for Scaling Math Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28433)
*Shuze Chen,Kunal Marwaha,Xiaoyang Lu,Henry Yuen,Tianyi Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Prove2Me，一个开放、协作的数学形式化平台，它利用AI编码智能体通过自然语言提示在Lean 4中编写形式化证明，旨在实现互联网规模的、由机器验证的人机协作数学形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Lean 4等证明助手承诺了形式化验证数学的范式，但大规模形式化项目面临两大进入壁垒：需要形式化验证和数学双重专业知识，以及编写形式化证明耗时长。AI编码智能体已显著降低了这些壁垒。

Method: 提出Prove2Me平台，用户发起形式化"任务"，AI智能体贡献形式化证明。平台设计了专门的机制和工具框架（harness）支持大规模协作，使智能体能够基于彼此的工作并自由复用已有结果。

Result: Prove2Me有望将数学形式化转变为可扩展的、众包式的努力，向任何拥有AI智能体的用户开放。

Conclusion: Prove2Me旨在利用AI智能体与人类协作，实现机器检查正确性的互联网规模数学形式化协作。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean 4 promise the paradigm of formally verified mathematics, but large-scale formalization projects have faced major barriers to entry, including the need for expertise in formal verification (as well as the underlying mathematics) and the significant time required for writing formal proofs. AI coding agents have dramatically reduced these barriers; human users can now use natural language to prompt agents to write complex proofs in Lean. This opens up the intriguing possibility of internet-scale mathematical collaboration involving both humans and AI agents, where correctness is machine-checked.
  To realize this possibility, we introduce Prove2Me (https://prove2.me), an open collaborative platform for formalizing mathematics. Users launch formalization "missions", to which AI agents contribute formal proofs toward completion. We designed mechanisms and a specialized harness in Prove2Me that enable large-scale collaboration so that agents can build on one another's work and freely reuse existing results. In doing so, Prove2Me aims to turn math formalization into a scalable, crowd-sourced effort open to anyone with an agent.

</details>


### [226] [Learning to Use Tools: Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28447)
*Minghui Xu,Zi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在Countdown任务中集成计算器工具调用以提升大语言模型的数学推理能力。通过SFT和多种RL方法（RLOO、RLOO++、GRPO、DAPO）训练，发现计算器工具集成使SFT和RL基线均提升约10个百分点，其中Tool-DAPO最优，pass@1从35.8%提升至66.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要可靠计算和验证的任务中容易出错，尤其是在数学推理场景下计算错误占错误响应的重要比例。作者希望借助外部工具（计算器）集成来减少算术和验证错误，提升模型的数学推理能力。

Method: 首先分析推理失败模式，确认计算错误是主要障碍之一。随后构建SFT数据集以教授模型有用的工具调用模式及输出解释能力。在此基础上，应用多种on-policy强化学习方法（RLOO、RLOO++、GRPO、DAPO），使用可自动验证的最终答案奖励进行训练。为可靠评估，构建了一个1,024道题的独立Countdown基准测试集，与训练数据无重叠。

Result: 计算器工具集成使SFT和RL基线均提升约10个百分点（pass@k）。Tool-DAPO表现最佳，pass@1从Tool-SFT的35.8%提升至66.0%。分析还显示，即使仅提供最终答案奖励，RL也能鼓励更有效的工具使用。

Conclusion: 工具集成能显著减少算术和验证错误，而RL能提升正确推理轨迹的概率。两者结合（尤其是Tool-DAPO）能实现最佳的数学推理性能，表明工具调用与强化学习的协同作用对提升LLM推理能力具有重要价值。

Abstract: Current large language models (LLMs) increasingly benefit from external tool integration, especially for tasks requiring reliable computation and verification. Motivated by this, we study calculator tool calling for improving mathematical reasoning on the Countdown task. We first analyze reasoning failures and find that calculation errors account for a substantial portion of incorrect responses. We then construct supervised fine-tuning datasets to teach the model useful tool-use patterns and how to interpret returned outputs. Building on this tool-formatted policy, we apply several on-policy reinforcement learning methods, including RLOO, RLOO++, GRPO, and DAPO, using automatically verifiable final-answer rewards. To enable a more reliable evaluation, we construct a fresh 1,024-problem held-out Countdown benchmark with no exact overlap with the training data. Our results show that calculator tool integration consistently improves both SFT and RL baselines, yielding roughly 10 percentage-point gains across pass@k. Among the RL methods, Tool-DAPO achieves the strongest performance, improving pass@1 from 35.8% for Tool-SFT to 66.0%. Further analysis shows that RL encourages more effective tool use even when only final-answer rewards are provided. These findings suggest that tool integration reduces arithmetic and verification errors, while RL increases the probability of correct reasoning traces.

</details>


### [227] [COVER: Identifiable Evaluation of Coalition Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28475)
*Raghul Sugumar,Amrit Gopinath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COVER评估契约，通过固定公共信息边界、下游栈G和有限合法团队族，在生成结果前设定评估条件，从而精确识别多智能体系统中基于路由的端到端准确率差距，而非仅归因于原始答案变化。实验表明COVER能暴露选择余量（selection headroom）而不伪造路由收益，是一种可审计的测量方法而非对通用路由优势的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统变更团队时，其消息和最终答案都会改变，因此端到端准确率差距本身无法识别出"路由效应"。需要一个能分离出路由选择的真实贡献的评估方法，避免将团队变更带来的整体变化误判为路由改进。

Method: COVER引入"评估契约"（evaluation contract），在生成结果前固定三个要素：公共信息边界（public information boundary）、下游栈G（downstream stack）以及有限合法团队族（finite legal team family）。通过完整覆盖（complete coverage）识别给定栈条件下的精确有限基准预言机遗憾（oracle regret）。对于任意有限冻结策略集合，执行其不同选定团队的并集是为每对策略对比提供最小无假设支持的方法（但不适用于绝对预言机遗憾）。实验采用源ID分离（source-ID-disjoint）的训练/测试划分。

Result: 在MuSiQue-12上，预先设定的privileged positive control将遗憾从0.532降至0.402；后续公共接口对照达到0.424（对比0.554），但为回顾式。在HotpotQA-4上，预先设定的公共直接评分器将遗憾从0.313降至0.110。固定栈Llama执行中，验证后的路由遗憾改善0.190，而原始答案增益仅0.010且置信区间跨越零。ToolSandbox五个变体族校验中，完整评估16个声明团队×14个未触及任务变体（224/224有效行）：声明族预言机达到0.768安全证据完成率，前瞻性冻结路由仅0.637（遗憾0.131），未达到预先声明的0.10标准；回顾式对照达0.655，与全工人结果一致（平均4.57对5.00个工人）。交叉栈诊断显示绝对分数依赖G，但未检测到路由×最终器的可察觉交互。

Conclusion: COVER是一种可审计的测量方法论，旨在暴露选择余量而非制造路由胜利。它不声称栈不变性或普适智能体路由优越性。核心教训是：路由效应必须通过严格的评估契约来分离，否则端到端差距可能被混淆因素污染。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system changes its team, it also changes the messages and final answer it produces, so an end-to-end accuracy gap does not by itself identify a routing effect. We introduce method, an evaluation contract that fixes a public information boundary, downstream stack G, and finite legal team family before outcomes are generated. Complete coverage identifies exact finite-benchmark oracle regret conditional on that stack. For any finite collection of frozen policies, executing the union of their distinct selected teams is the minimal assumption-free support for every pairwise policy contrast, though not for absolute oracle regret. Two controlled tables with source-ID-disjoint splits test the instrument. On MuSiQue-12, a pre-specified privileged positive control improves regret from 0.532 to 0.402; a later public-interface control reaches 0.424 versus 0.554 but is retrospective. On HotpotQA-4, a pre-specified public direct scorer improves regret from 0.313 to 0.110. In fixed-stack Llama execution, verified route regret improves by 0.190, while the raw-answer gain is 0.010 with an interval crossing zero. A five-family ToolSandbox variant-shift validation exhaustively evaluates 16 declared teams on 14 untouched task variants (224/224 valid rows): the declared-family oracle reaches 0.768 safe-evidence completion, while the prospectively frozen router gets 0.637 (regret 0.131), failing the predeclared 0.10 criterion. A later retrospective comparator reaches 0.655, matching all-workers with 4.57 versus 5.00 workers on average. Thus COVER exposes selection headroom without manufacturing a routing win. A crossed-stack diagnostic shows absolute scores depend on G but finds no detectable router-by-finalizer interaction. COVER is an auditable measurement methodology, not a claim of stack-invariant or universal agent-routing superiority.

</details>


### [228] [Training Communication-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Language Models with Layer Re-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28511)
*Simeng Sun,Roger Waleffe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通信高效MoE模型（CE-MoE），通过采用异构分层模式将token混合层和通道混合层解耦，将专家能力集中在少数路由MoE层中，在匹配总参数量和激活参数量的情况下，相比全MoE基线显著降低训练成本，同时保持甚至提升验证损失和下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家并行训练MoE语言模型时，all-to-all的token分发与合并通信操作会消耗大量端到端训练时间，成为训练效率的瓶颈。为此，需要设计通信高效的MoE架构以降低通信开销。

Method: 提出CE-MoE架构，采用异构层模式解耦token混合与通道混合的深度。与传统模型在每个token混合层（如attention、Mamba-2）后交错插入MoE层不同，CE-MoE将专家容量集中在少数几个路由MoE层中，同时通过增加额外的token混合层和稠密FFN层来维持模型深度。

Result: 在从2B到31.5B总参数的规模阶梯上，在匹配总参数量和激活参数量的条件下，CE-MoE相比全MoE基线一致地降低了训练成本，同时匹配验证损失和下游基准性能。在31.5B规模下，CE-MoE节省33.3%的GPU小时，同时提升了平均下游分数和推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: 将专家层集中而非交错分布，可以有效减少MoE模型训练中的通信开销，在保持模型质量的同时实现更高的训练和推理效率。

Abstract: When training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models with expert parallelism, all-to-all token dispatch and combine collectives can consume a substantial fraction of end-to-end training time. In this work, we study communication-efficient MoE models (CE-MoE), in which we adopt a heterogeneous layer pattern that decouples token-mixing and channel-mixing depth. Compared to conventional models which interleave MoE layers after each token-mixing layer (e.g., attention, Mamba-2), CE-MoE models concentrate expert capacity in a select few routed MoE layers, while maintaining depth by adding additional token-mixing and dense-FFN layers. Across a scaling ladder from 2B to 31.5B total parameters, under matched total and activated parameters, CE-MoE models consistently reduce training cost while matching validation loss and downstream benchmarks with full-MoE baselines. At the 31.5B scale, CE-MoE uses 33.3\% fewer GPU-hours while improving average downstream score and inference throughput.

</details>


### [229] [InstructMesh: Selective Refinement of Generative 3D Models for Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28534)
*Faraz Faruqi,Ahmed Katary,Demircan Tas,Theresa Hradilak,Ning Zhang,Jiaji Li,Fabian Manhardt,Martin Nisser,Vrushank Phadnis,Ruofei Du,Federico Tombari,Megan Hofmann,Stefanie Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InstructMesh 是一个交互式后期编辑工具，允许用户通过区域选择和定向操作（如开孔、封孔、调整厚度）修复生成式 3D 模型中的几何缺陷，使其可用于实际制造。它直接操作中间潜空间表示，支持自然语言和滑块控制两种交互方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式 AI 3D 建模工具优先考虑视觉可信度而非几何精度，生成的模型常存在制造相关的缺陷（如孔洞、厚度不均等问题），影响其制造后的实际使用。作者希望通过分析这些常见故障模式，设计一种无需专业建模技能即可进行选择性几何修复的工具。

Method: 首先分析现有先进生成工具输出中常见的制造相关故障模式；然后设计 InstructMesh 工具，直接在中间潜空间表示上操作，支持通过自然语言提示或滑块控件进行区域选择和定向编辑操作（如开孔、封孔、调整局部厚度）；最后通过两个用户研究评估其可用性。

Result: 两项用户研究表明，新手用户能够使用 InstructMesh 识别并执行生成式输出中与制造相关的修复操作；同时发现用户倾向于使用滑块控制与自然语言输入相结合的混合界面。

Conclusion: InstructMesh 有效填补了生成式 3D 建模与制造可用性之间的鸿沟，为非专业用户提供了一种直观的几何修复方案，且混合交互界面是更受用户欢迎的设计方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI allow users to create 3D models from text or images. However, these models prioritize visual plausibility over geometric accuracy, often generating results with flaws that compromise their intended use post-fabrication. We present InstructMesh, an interactive post-generation refinement tool that enables selective repair of generative 3D models through region selection and targeted operations, such as opening or sealing voids, or adjusting local thickness. Users can invoke edit operations via natural language prompts or slider controls. By operating directly on the intermediate latent representation, InstructMesh allows users to apply robust geometric corrections without requiring expert modeling skills. To inform our design, we first analyze common fabrication-related failure modes in outputs from state-of-the-art generative tools. We then conduct two user studies, demonstrating that novices can identify and perform fabrication-relevant repairs on generative outputs using InstructMesh, and revealing user preference for hybrid interfaces that combine slider controls with natural language input.

</details>


### [230] [Logos: An Agent Harness on a Cross-Process Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28553)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Hao Cheng,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Logos框架，证明智能体系统可以不绑定于单一进程，通过将每个插件作为独立进程运行并以追加式日志作为唯一共享状态，实现了跨进程的智能体编排，实现了故障隔离和会话恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体系统在运行时动态组合能力，虽然已有时空可组合性演算的完整形式化处理，但该框架将插件形式承载于单一进程共享的上下文中，导致所有组件处于同一物理故障域，一次故障会使所有组件和会话同时中断。本文旨在打破这一进程绑定的限制。

Method: 论文首先指出模型和演算并不将智能体绑定于单进程，语言模型的无状态性将所有跨步状态置于模型之外，可靠性不变量仅定义在状态空间上。基于这四个观察提炼出四条引理，其前提是演算的假设和语言模型推理的无状态性。在此基础上构建Logos——一个类ROS的跨进程智能体编排框架，其中每个插件是一个独立进程，唯一共享状态是追加式日志。

Result: 在工具调用周期的四个边界处设置kill后进行80个会话的恢复实验，均无重复效应。与单进程参考配置的同故障对比显示：单进程配置下一次故障中断所有同驻会话，而同类进程构造下一次故障只终止于单个节点。

Conclusion: Logos证明了将智能体插件重构为独立进程可实现故障域隔离，显著提升系统鲁棒性，同时保持演算的可靠性保证不变。

Abstract: Modern agent systems assemble capabilities at runtime, and this dynamic composition has recently received a complete formal treat ment in the spatiotemporal-composability calculus, in which a capability is a component carrying a tracked inverse, and agents are assembled as plugins. This plugin form is carried by a single process sharing one context, a carrier that places all components in one physical failure domain, a fault suspends every component at once, and process death interrupts every session the process hosts. This paper shows that neither the modeling nor the calculus binds an agent to one process, the statelessness of the language model keeps all cross-step state outside the model, and the soundness invariant is defined on the state space alone. These observations condense into four lemmas whose premises are the hypotheses of the calculus and the statelessness of language-model inference. On these lemmas this paper constructs Logos, a ROS-like cross process agent harness in which a plugin is a process and the only shared state is an append-only transcript. Eighty sessions resume with no repeated effect after kills placed at the four boundaries of the tool-call cycle, and a same-fault comparison with a single process reference configuration shows one fault interrupting every co-resident session while under the peer-process construction one fault ends at one node.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Dark energy: the cost of function in protein evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27711)
*Ezequiel A. Galpern,Federico Caamaño,Ignacio E. Sánchez,Diego U. Ferreiro*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 本文综述了蛋白质进化中折叠稳定性与生物学功能之间的双重约束及其带来的"暗能量"——即蛋白质序列的进化能量与物理折叠能量之间的差异。文章讨论了深度学习突变扫描、蛋白质语言模型和逆折叠模型等计算与实验方法如何在大规模范围内量化这一暗能量成分，揭示生物信息从序列到结构再到功能并反馈回序列的流动机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质的进化命运同时受折叠稳定性和生物学功能驱动，这两种约束常常相互冲突，产生"挫折"并带来超出稳定性的功能代价。作者提出需要一种统一的框架来量化这些代价——即"暗能量"概念，并利用最新的计算与实验工具在蛋白质组层面揭示这一被传统折叠能量分析所忽略的成分。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性论文，系统梳理了三种关键技术路径：(1)深度学习突变扫描(DMS)用于大规模实验测量功能与稳定性的耦合效应；(2)蛋白质语言模型(pLMs)用于从序列数据中提取进化能量信号；(3)逆折叠模型用于弥合序列-结构之间的映射。通过这些方法的整合，作者展示了如何在蛋白质宇宙范围内解耦折叠与功能约束，进而量化暗能量。

Result: 综述表明，通过整合计算与实验方法，能够在蛋白质组尺度上识别出独立的"暗能量"成分，即序列携带的进化能量中超出物理折叠能量需求的部分。这一成分反映了生物功能对蛋白质序列施加的额外进化压力，为理解折叠-功能权衡提供了定量基础。

Conclusion: 暗能量框架为理解蛋白质进化中折叠与功能的冲突提供了一个统一的定量视角。该方法有望深化对生物信息编码如何从序列流向结构、功能并反馈回序列这一循环过程的理解，并为蛋白质工程设计提供新的理论指导。

Abstract: The evolutionary fate of proteins is driven by both folding stability and biological function, dual constraints that often conflict, creating frustration and imposing functional costs beyond stability. These costs can be captured by a "dark energy": the difference between the evolutionary energy of protein sequences and their physical folding energy. Recent advances in deep mutational scanning, protein language models, and inverse-folding models have enabled the quantification of dark energy across the protein universe. We review the computational and experimental approaches that disentangle folding and function at scale, revealing a dark energy component and providing new insights into how biological information flows from sequence to structure to function and back to sequence.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Characterizing the I/O Behavior of HPC Applications through Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27642)
*Njoud O. Almaaitah,David E. Singh,Taylan Özden,Jesus Carretero,Raffaele Montella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于ElastiSim批处理系统模拟器的框架，用于在模拟环境中再现真实HPC应用的I/O访问模式。该框架利用HPCIO分析仓库中捕获的真实应用I/O轨迹生成模拟应用，并通过对真实应用轨迹的详细案例研究，展示了该框架如何提供对I/O应用性能特征（包括基于访问模式的I/O拥塞分析）的洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行应用产生大量数据，导致密集的并行I/O操作，这些操作表现出不同的复杂度，包括多种I/O访问模式、数据暂存和争用风险。为了高效利用高性能计算系统并优化I/O性能，理解应用的I/O行为至关重要。

Method: 开发了一个框架，在ElastiSim模拟环境中再现真实应用的I/O访问模式。模拟应用基于从HPCIO分析仓库捕获的真实应用I/O轨迹生成，该数据库包含结合了不同并行I/O库和I/O栈各层真实应用性能信息的轨迹。

Result: 通过对真实世界应用轨迹的详细案例研究，展示了该建模框架能够提供对I/O应用性能特征的洞察，包括基于应用I/O访问模式的I/O拥塞分析。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效模拟真实HPC应用的I/O行为，并为分析和优化高性能计算系统中的I/O性能提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Parallel applications process large amounts of data, leading to intensive parallel I/O operations. These operations can exhibit different levels of complexity, including, among others, multiple I/O access patterns, data staging, and contention risks. Therefore, in order to exploit high-performance computing (HPC) systems efficiently and optimize the I/O performance, it is crucial to consider the I/O behaviour of the HPC applications. In this work, we have developed a framework that reproduces the I/O access pattern of real applications in a simulated environment provided by ElastiSim, a batch-system simulator for rigid, malleable, and evolving workloads. The simulated applications are generated based on I/O traces captured from real applications provided by the HPC Input/Output (HPCIO) analysis repository. The HPCIO analysis database includes traces combined with information about real applications' performance across different parallel I/O libraries and layers of the I/O stack. We have conducted detailed case studies of real-world applications' traces to demonstrate how the proposed modeling framework can provide insights into the performance characteristics of I/O applications, including the I/O congestion analysis based on the application's I/O access pattern.

</details>


### [233] [TerraceMoE: A Cost Model for Hierarchical MoE All-to-All Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27874)
*Weicheng Xue,Bingqiang Wang,Li Yuan,Huihui Zhou,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于专家并行混合专家（MoE）训练的通信级成本模型，用于评估分层两跳调度（hierarchical two-hop dispatch）与直接通信在降低慢速fabric流量方面的权衡。该模型由多个验证门（validation gates）约束，验证失败时会在代码中撤回相关能力而非仅报告注意事项。参考几何配置下（16组×8秩、q=3、H=2048、每秩4096 tokens），修正后的有效盈亏平衡分层比率为3.98（实际PyTorch到达链）、1.49（假设融合目标）、1.10（零实现开销）。作者强调这是仅比率的敏感性结果而非部署预测（平台A为1.03，平台B无快慢分离测量，两平台均未达到分层区间）。四个通信级语料库通过门限验证，而漂移探针和步级门失败并已在代码中强制执行，因此不给出训练吞吐率预测。使能的路由约束固定每token扇出和所选组的配额，聚合的每对端计数仍依赖数据，其验证损失代价很小但非零（+0.0034 nats）；下游等价性报告存在估算来源不完整问题，无法从工件独立重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层两跳调度可减少专家并行MoE训练中的慢速fabric流量，但引入了额外的通信集合和到达侧算子链。需要建立通信级成本模型来评估这一权衡，并采用可执行的验证门机制确保模型可靠性，而非仅以注释形式报告局限性。

Method: 构建通信调用级别的成本模型，通过验证门（validation gates）约束模型有效性——当门失败时在代码层面撤回能力。使用比率先验（ratio-only sensitivity）方法，在参考几何上计算分层比率的盈亏平衡点，并比较不同实现开销（实际PyTorch到达链、假设融合目标、零开销）下的阈值。同时引入路由约束固定每token扇出与所选组配额，并通过实验测量验证损失代价。

Result: 参考几何下修正有效盈亏平衡分层比率为3.98（实际PyTorch到达链）、1.49（假设融合目标）、1.10（零实现开销）。平台A测得1.03，平台B无快慢分离测量，两平台均未达到分层区间。四个通信级语料库通过验证门，漂移探针和步级门失败并在代码中强制撤回。路由约束的验证损失代价为+0.0034 nats，下游等价性估算来源不完整。

Conclusion: 当前测量平台上分层两跳调度未达到使其实用化的层次区间；由于步级验证门失败已被代码强制执行，不提供训练吞吐率预测。路由约束虽可行但存在非零损失代价，且下游等价性结论不可独立重建。工作开放了代码、校准常数和验证门以支持复现与后续改进。

Abstract: Hierarchical two-hop dispatch can reduce slow-fabric traffic in expert-parallel Mixture-of-Experts training, but it adds a second collective and an arrival-side operator chain. We present a cost model for screening that trade at the communication-call level, bounded by validation gates that withdraw a capability in code when they fail rather than reporting a caveat. At a reference geometry with 16 groups of 8 ranks, $q=3$, $H=2048$, and 4096 tokens per rank, the corrected effective breakeven hierarchy ratio is 3.98 for the measured PyTorch arrival chain, 1.49 for a hypothetical fused target, and 1.10 at zero implementation overhead. These are ratio-only sensitivity results, not deployment predictions: platform A measures 1.03, platform B has no separated fast/slow measurement, and neither machine measured here reaches the hierarchical regime. Four communication-level corpora pass their gates; a drift probe and the step-level gate fail. The latter failure is enforced in code, so we make no training-throughput prediction. The enabling routing constraint fixes per-token fan-out and per-selected-group quota, while aggregate per-peer counts remain data-dependent. Its measured validation-loss cost is small but nonzero (+0.0034 nats); downstream equivalence is reported with incomplete estimator provenance and is therefore not independently reconstructible from the artifact. Code, calibration constants and the validation gates are at https://github.com/weich97/TerraceMoE-simulator.

</details>


### [234] [Learning-Augmented Heuristics: Simple, yet Smart, Robust and Interpretable Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27975)
*Haocheng Xia,William Nixon,Bintang Dwi Marthen,Pranav Bhandari,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Learning-Augmented Heuristics (LAH)框架，通过学习静态缓存启发式算法的参数来构建智能缓存策略。基于该框架实现的S4-FIFO算法在1035个生产轨迹上比S3-FIFO提升26%平均效率，比最先进的3L-Cache提升8%，同时具有更强的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓存淘汰策略分为静态启发式（如2Q、S3-FIFO）和智能算法（如ARC、LRB）两类。智能缓存虽能适应工作负载变化并具有更高效率和鲁棒性潜力，但存在目标失配（objective mismatch）和不稳定性问题，需要一种既能自适应学习又能保持稳定高效的缓存框架。

Method: LAH框架通过解耦数据平面和控制平面来学习静态启发式算法的缓存级参数：数据平面负责简单、高速的读写操作，控制平面利用缓存级特征进行异步学习。作者通过S4-FIFO（Smart S3-FIFO）验证该框架，在4140个生产轨迹上预训练单一模型并嵌入S4-FIFO以学习最优缓存参数。

Result: 在1035个评估轨迹上，S4-FIFO相比S3-FIFO提升26%平均效率，相比最先进的3L-Cache提升8%。在鲁棒性方面，S4-FIFO在最差轨迹上仅使FIFO的失配率增加0.8%，而3L-Cache增加了8.8%。此外，语言模型能够为S4-FIFO的配置决策提供可解释的理由。

Conclusion: LAH框架通过解耦数据与控制平面并结合学习与启发式的优势，有效解决了智能缓存的目标失配和不稳定问题。S4-FIFO在效率、鲁棒性和可解释性三个维度上均优于现有方法，展示了学习增强启发式在缓存系统中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Caching is widely used across the system stack to improve performance and efficiency, with eviction algorithms at its core. Existing cache eviction policies fall into two broad categories: static heuristics (e.g., 2Q, S3-FIFO) and smart algorithms (e.g., ARC, LRB). Smart caches can adapt to workloads and have the potential to achieve higher efficiency and robustness than static heuristics. However, we find that existing smart caches suffer from objective mismatches and instability. We introduce Learning-Augmented Heuristics (LAH), a framework that learns the cache-level parameters of static heuristics. By decoupling the data and control planes, LAH supports simple, high-speed data reads and writes on the data plane, while performing occasional asynchronous learning on the control plane using cache-level features. We demonstrate the effectiveness of LAH through S4-FIFO, a Smart S3-FIFO cache eviction algorithm. We pre-train a single model on 4,140 production traces and embed it in S4-FIFO to learn optimal cache parameters. On 1,035 evaluation traces, S4-FIFO improves the mean efficiency by 26% compared to S3-FIFO and by 8% compared to 3L-Cache, the best state-of-the-art algorithm. S4-FIFO is also robust---increasing miss ratio over FIFO by 0.8% on the worst trace, whereas 3L-Cache increases FIFO's miss ratio by 8.8%. Finally, S4-FIFO's decisions are also interpretable: a language model can provide a rationale for why a particular configuration was chosen.

</details>


### [235] [Great Expectations: Benchmarking the Real-World Performance of RVV 1.0 in HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28097)
*Stepan Nassyr,Prateek Chawla,Daniel Seibel,Jayesh Badwaik,Kaveh Haghighi Mood,Andreas Herten*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过标准HPC基准测试（BLAS、FFTW、HPL、HPCG）和合成工作负载，对四款最新的RVV 1.0硬件（SiFive X280、SpacemiT X60/X100/A100、T-Head C920v2）进行评测，并与NVIDIA Grace（ARM64）对比，发现RVV 1.0相对标量执行有显著性能提升，但仍存在硬件实现层面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RISC-V向量扩展（RVV 1.0）已获批准并被新一代商用芯片采纳，但其在高性能计算（HPC）领域的实际竞争力尚待验证。作者旨在重新审视RISC-V在HPC中的可行性。

Method: 采用标准HPC基准测试套件（BLAS、FFTW、HPL、HPCG）和合成工作负载（STREAM、FMA吞吐量），对四款RVV 1.0芯片（SiFive X280/Tenstorrent Blackhole、SpacemiT X60 K1、X100/A100 K3、T-Head C920v2/Sophon SG2044）进行系统评测，并以NVIDIA Grace作为ARM64对标芯片。

Result: RVV 1.0相比纯标量执行带来了显著的性能提升，但与NVIDIA Grace相比仍存在差距，且各硬件平台在向量实现层面存在具体挑战和性能特征差异。
