Conclusion: 尽管RVV 1.0展现了在HPC方向上的潜力，硬件实现上的诸多问题仍是RISC-V（包括RVV）成为HPC主流架构的主要障碍，文章详细讨论了这些剩余挑战。

Abstract: Following the ratification of the RISC-V Vector Extension (RVV 1.0), new commercially available silicon has been adopting the extension. This paper revisits the question of RISC-V viability for High-Performance-Computing (HPC) by benchmarking the latest RVV 1.0-capable hardware (SiFive X280 (Tenstorrent Blackhole), SpacemiT X60 (K1) and X100/A100 (K3), and T-Head C920v2 (Sophon SG2044)). We assess these platforms using standard HPC benchmarks (BLAS, FFTW, HPL, HPCG) and synthetic workloads (STREAM, FMA throughput) and compare them to a state-of-the-art HPC ARM64 chip (NVIDIA Grace). Our findings show that while RVV 1.0 delivers significant performance improvements over scalar execution, hardware-specific implementation challenges remain. We detail these performance characteristics and discuss the remaining hurdles for RISC-V, including RVV, to become a mainstay in the HPC landscape.

</details>


### [236] [Memory-efficient GPU pipelines for real-time non-line-of-sight reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28183)
*Alfonso López-Ruiz,Diego Royo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文重新构建了两种基于波的非视距（NLOS）成像算法（f-k 迁移和 phasor-fields）的 GPU 执行流程，通过离线预计算解析函数核、融合核、warp 级光子分箱、批处理变换、CUDA 图重放和选择性 FP16 存储等优化，将重建速度提升至比参考流式管线快 42 倍、比最快已发表 GPU 基线快 14 倍，同时将内存需求降至 2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLOS 成像从拐角后的场景重建需要处理数十亿个光子时间戳，SPAD 阵列提高了采集吞吐量后，重建成为瓶颈阶段。现有的 GPU 实现存在内存带宽浪费、密集传播核占用巨大存储空间、流水线冗余数据传输等问题，亟需更高效的执行方案以支持更大规模、更精细的重建以及未来的视频级 NLOS 处理。

Method: 1) 对 phasor-fields 算法，利用环的解析傅里叶变换离线组装 ring-and-radius 核，使传播核在运行时从不以密集形式存在，大幅降低内存和带宽开销；2) 对 f-k 迁移和 phasor-fields 两个算法进行流水线重组，包括融合核设计、warp 级光子分箱、批处理变换、CUDA 图重放；3) 仅在真正瓶颈处应用 FP16 存储，避免精度损失同时减小内存；4) 支持流式与离线两种处理模式；5) 提出三种基于帧预算的去噪策略用于下一代 NLOS 视频处理。

Result: 实现相比参考流式管线快 42 倍，比最快已发布 GPU 基线快 14 倍；内存使用降至原来的 2.5%；能够在相同硬件上进行更大规模和更精细的重建，或在更低内存预算下完成同等规模重建。论文还提供了每项实现选择（如 FP16、融合核、批处理等）的消融实验。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的 GPU 执行优化，重建速度不再是 NLOS 成像的瓶颈环节，为下一代高吞吐量 SPAD 阵列和 NLOS 视频级处理铺平了道路。文中提出的三种去噪策略进一步表明帧率预算的释放为后续算法创新提供了空间。

Abstract: Non-line-of-sight (NLOS) imaging reconstructs scenes hidden around a corner from indirect light recorded by a single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD). A single reconstruction is a large inverse problem: billions of photon timestamps must be binned, moved through memory, transformed and inverted. As SPAD arrays raise acquisition throughput, reconstruction becomes the limiting stage. We rebuild the GPU execution of two established wave-based algorithms, f-k migration and phasor-fields, for both streaming and offline processing. On the phasor-fields side we assemble the ring-and-radius kernels of previous work once and offline, using the analytic Fourier transform of a ring, so the propagation kernel never exists in dense form at runtime, reducing the memory and bandwidth. We reorganize the pipeline of both algorithms with fused kernels, warp-level photon binning, batched transforms, CUDA graph replay, and FP16 storage applied only where it reduces the actual bottleneck. Our implementations are up to 42x faster than the reference streaming pipeline and up to 14x faster than the fastest published GPU baseline, all while using a fraction of the memory (down to 2.5%), enabling vastly larger and finer reconstructions on the same hardware, or comparable ones within a much lower memory budget. We report an ablation of each implementation choice and propose three denoising strategies enabled by the resulting frame budget for next-generation NLOS video processing.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Non-standard memory models with indexed retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27479)
*Gabriele Scheler,Martin L. Schumann,Johann Schumann*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经元中心假说的局部主义一次性学习方法，通过高信息神经元作为索引实现按索引检索的神经记忆模型，突破了Hopfield网络只能进行联想式向量检索的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hopfield风格神经网络记忆模型（联想记忆）无法像计算机内存那样通过索引直接寻址，而是依赖输入向量与存储向量之间的相似度进行内容寻址的联想检索。作者希望恢复按索引直接寻址的能力。

Method: 基于神经元中心假说（神经元自身调节其突触的塑性组织方式），实现了一个局部主义（localist）、神经元中心的一次性学习（one-shot learning）方法，并在MNIST模式分类问题上进行验证，寻找高信息神经元作为表征中的索引。

Result: 该方法尽管极简单且源自生物学观察，但实现了显著的概念性进展和技术优势，能够通过索引化检索恢复完整模式，而非从吸引子进行联想式向量检索。

Conclusion: 通过神经元中心假说引导的局部主义一次性学习，可以实现按索引寻址的神经记忆，替代传统的联想式吸引子检索方式，为神经网络的记忆模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: The standard memory models for neural networks are variants of the Hopfield network, where feature representations are stored as vectors in a matrix. Retrieval happens based on similarity between an input vector and the set of stored vectors in a content-addressable manner such that the network evolves towards the closest stored attractor. In other words, the useful property of addressing items in memory directly by index is lost in Hopfield-style neural network models ("associative memory"). In this extended abstract, we present a new model which is extremely simple, derived from biological observation, yet introduces a significant conceptual advance and technical benefits. The goal is to establish adaptivity based on the neuron-centric hypothesis: Plasticity is organized by the neuron which regulates its own synapses. Accordingly we implemented a localist, neuron-centric one-shot learning method and applied it to a simple pattern classification problem (MNIST). We were looking for the existence of high information neurons, to act as indices into the representations. The idea was that we would be able to restore full patterns by indexed retrieval, instead of associative vector retrieval from attractors.

</details>


### [238] [Multiscale Community-Based Fingerprinting of Signed Functional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27483)
*Sema Athamnah,Selin Aviyente*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多尺度社区的功能连接组指纹识别框架，利用带符号多层社区检测方法来刻画个体大脑功能网络的介观结构，从而在会话和任务间实现可靠的个体识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能连接组指纹识别方法主要依赖边缘级特征，这些特征对噪声敏感、难以解释，且在跨任务跨数据集泛化方面能力有限。

Method: 提出多尺度社区级功能连接组指纹识别框架：(1) 引入带符号多层社区检测方法，同时整合相关和反相关的脑活动；(2) 在跨任务和跨会话的情境下识别个体专属的社区结构；(3) 从联合社区结构中计算图论度量，构建低维的社区级指纹表征。

Result: 在人类连接组计划（HCP）的810名健康对照受试者上评估，结果表明社区级指纹在跨会话和跨任务中提供了可靠且可解释的个体化大脑表征。

Conclusion: 介观尺度的社区结构能够提供有意义且具有判别力的个体特异性指纹，该框架为精准神经影像学和个性化神经科学应用奠定了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Objective: Recent studies demonstrate that functional connectomes contain subject-specific signatures, or \textit{fingerprints}, that can identify individuals across repeated sessions and tasks. Existing methods mostly rely on edge-level features that are sensitive to noise, difficult to interpret, and limited in their ability to generalize across tasks and datasets. Methods: We propose a multiscale community-based functional connectome fingerprinting framework that characterizes each individual by the mesoscale structure of their functional networks. We introduce a signed multilayer community detection framework that incorporates both correlated and anti-correlated brain activity to identify subject-specific community structures across tasks and sessions. Graph-theoretic metrics are then computed from the resulting joint community structures to derive low-dimensional community-level fingerprint representations. Results: The proposed framework is evaluated on 810 healthy control subjects from the Human Connectome Project (HCP). The results show that community-based fingerprints provide a reliable and interpretable substrate for individualized brain characterization across sessions and tasks. Conclusion: Mesoscale community structure provides meaningful and discriminative subject-specific fingerprints. Significance: The proposed framework offers a promising foundation for precision neuroimaging and personalized neuroscience applications.

</details>


### [239] [A weighted model of perception and decision-making between targets of finite size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27670)
*W. Christopher Strickland,Andrew J. Bernoff*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉神经环模型与动物导航行为之间的数学和生物学联系，提出了一个三阶段模型（视觉预处理识别目标、主导目标选择、导航后处理），并通过能量最小化框架简化了模型分析与数值实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于实验的研究已将视觉神经环模型与动物在复杂吸引目标景观中的导航行为联系起来，但现有模型在目标获取和追踪的生物学一致性方面仍有不足，需要一个能够反映自然界现象的定量框架。

Method: 提出三阶段处理模型：1) 前处理阶段对视觉刺激进行预处理以识别离散目标集；2) 处理阶段选择主导目标；3) 后处理阶段利用这些信息进行导航。模型中纳入有限目标尺寸和神经密度，并将整个模型转化为能量最小化问题进行数学分析和数值实现。

Result: 模型在数学上可表述为能量最小化问题，简化了分析复杂度并便于数值实现；在生物学上与生理学动机一致，能够复现实验观察结果；通过经验数据可解码神经几何结构，并为未来模型扩展提供了分析流程，同时提出了具体的实验研究途径来测试和参数化该定量框架。

Conclusion: 该三阶段模型在神经视觉处理与动物导航行为之间建立了统一的定量桥梁，既具有生理学基础又能复现实验数据，同时为后续实验验证和模型扩展提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Recent research grounded in experiments has connected neural ring models of vision to how animals navigate a complex landscape of attractive targets. In this paper we investigate the mathematical and biological implications of a three-stage model where animals pre-process visual stimuli to identify a discrete set of targets, process this input to select the dominant targets, and then post-process this information to navigate the landscape. Incorporating finite target sizes and a neural density allows consistent target acquisition and pursuit reflecting what is seen in nature. Mathematically, we show this model corresponds to an energy minimization problem, simplifying both its analysis and numerical implementation. Biologically, we argue that the model is physiologically motivated, reproduces experimental observations, and presents a fairly direct pathway to decoding neural geometry via empirical data. Our results also demonstrate an analysis pipeline for future model extensions and suggest specific avenues for experimental research to test and parameterize our quantitative framework.

</details>


### [240] [Adaptive self-organized criticality in deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28431)
*Simon Vock,Christian Meisel*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 研究表明深度神经网络可以通过纯局部稳态可塑性（只依赖突触后神经元活动）自主调节其全局动力学状态，使网络从亚临界或超临界初始条件收敛到临界状态，并在梯度学习时抵抗向超临界动力学的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络是依赖于跨层活动与扰动稳定传播的高维动力系统，维持合适的动力学位态对于鲁棒学习和防止训练过程中的动力学不稳定至关重要。作者希望探索能否通过纯局部的自组织机制来调节网络的全局动力学状态。

Method: 从输入的响应中推断神经元活动，仅使用突触后神经元的活动信息来强化或弱化单个突触（局部稳态可塑性规则）。该规则不测量任何全局网络属性，但能驱动网络从亚临界和超临界初始条件收敛到临界状态，通过反应活动传播守恒和有限时间Lyapunov指数消失来表征临界态。

Result: 该局部可塑性规则能够将网络从不同初始条件（亚临界和超临界）自主驱动到共同的临界状态；在结合梯度学习时，稳态适应能抵消训练引起的向超临界动力学漂移，同时揭示了动力学调节与任务优化之间的竞争关系。

Conclusion: 证明了深度神经网络可以实现自适应自组织，并通过纯局部可塑性控制网络的集体动力学工作点，为理解神经网络的鲁棒学习与稳定性提供了新机制。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are high-dimensional dynamical systems whose function depends on the stable propagation of activity and perturbations across many layers. Maintaining suitable dynamical regimes may therefore be essential for robust learning and for preventing dynamical instabilities during training. Here, we show that the global dynamical state of a deep neural network can be autonomously regulated by purely local homeostatic plasticity. Neuronal activity is inferred from responses across inputs, and individual synapses are strengthened or weakened using only the activity of their postsynaptic neuron. Without measuring any global network property, this rule drives networks from both subcritical and supercritical initial conditions toward a common critical state, characterized by conserved activity propagation and a vanishing largest finite-time Lyapunov exponent. When combined with gradient-based learning, homeostatic adaptation counteracts the training-induced drift toward supercritical dynamics, while revealing a competition between dynamical regulation and task optimization. Our results demonstrate how adaptive self-organization can be implemented in deep neural networks and how local plasticity can control their collective dynamical operating point.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Beyond Relative Geometry: Metric-Aware Geometry Perception for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27497)
*Fengjun Zhong,Congjia Chen,Zhaoxu Liu,Jinyang Du,Yuchen Gong,Enqi Mao,Ruihao Gong,ShuJie Wang,Xianglong Liu,Zhongliang Qiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGP（Metric-Aware Geometry Perception），一个端到端、即插即用的度量几何重建框架，旨在解决现有具身模型仅能重建相对几何（尺度任意）导致的空间感知与机器人真实世界动作尺度不一致的问题。MAGP通过度量尺度等变增强和灵活的度量条件化设计，实现了跨场景、视角和传感配置下几何一致的度量重建，在ETH3D、MegaDepth和ScanNet++上将绝对误差从2.01m降至0.07m，并显著提升了多个机器人策略在LIBERO、RoboTwin和LIBERO-Plus上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身模型虽然越来越多地利用几何表示来提升空间推理和机器人操作能力，但已有的重建方法仅能恢复具有任意尺度的相对几何，导致预测的物体尺寸和空间距离在不同场景、视角和输入配置间不一致。这种尺度不一致性使得几何感知无法直接与定义在真实世界尺度上的机器人动作对齐，从而限制了机器人的实际操作性能。

Method: MAGP包含两个核心设计：（1）Metric Scale Equivariant Augmentation（度量尺度等变增强）——通过相机参数和深度观测引导模型重建度量几何，确保重建结果遵循观测指定的度量尺度；（2）Flexible Metric Conditioning（灵活的度量条件化）——支持任意数量的视角以及相机和深度输入的不同组合，增强对异构机器人传感配置的鲁棒性。整体架构采用端到端、即插即用设计，可无缝集成到机器人策略中。

Result: 在ETH3D、MegaDepth和ScanNet++数据集上，MAGP在保持强相对几何精度的同时，将绝对误差从2.01m降低到0.07m（降低超过一个数量级）。在机器人策略集成实验中，MAGP在LIBERO、RoboTwin和零样本LIBERO-Plus上均一致提升了性能，其中在RoboTwin上最高提升了6.26%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，MAGP有效解决了具身模型中几何重建尺度不一致的瓶颈问题，证明了度量几何对机器人操作的有效性和泛化性。该框架作为一个即插即用的通用模块，有望成为具身智能系统中几何感知的标准组件。

Abstract: Recent embodied models increasingly leverage geometric representations to improve spatial reasoning and robotic manipulation. However, existing reconstruction methods only reconstruct relative geometry with arbitrary scales, causing predicted object dimensions and spatial distances to vary across scenes, viewpoints, and input configurations. This inconsistency prevents geometric perception from being directly aligned with robotic actions defined on the real-world scale. To address this limitation, we propose Metric-Aware Geometry Perception (MAGP), an end-to-end, plug-and-play framework for metric geometry reconstruction that can be seamlessly integrated into robotic policies. At its core, Metric Scale Equivariant Augmentation encourages the model to reconstruct metric geometry from camera parameters and depth observations, ensuring that the reconstructed geometry follows the metric scale specified by observations. Flexible Metric Conditioning further enables MAGP to support arbitrary view counts and combinations of camera and depth inputs, improving robustness to heterogeneous robotic sensing configurations. Together, these designs produce geometrically consistent reconstructions with stable object dimensions and spatial distances across scenes and sensing conditions. Experiments on ETH3D, MegaDepth, and ScanNet++ demonstrate that MAGP maintains strong relative geometry accuracy while reducing the absolute error by over an order of magnitude, from 2.01m to 0.07m. When integrated into multiple robotic policies, MAGP consistently improves performance on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, with gains of up to 6.26% on RoboTwin. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of metric geometry for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [242] [Remote Human and Robot Interaction for Greenhouse Gardening Using Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27545)
*Daniel Udekwe,Hasan Seyyedhasani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在温室环境中使用VR远程操控机器人（无人地面车+机械臂+相机）进行叶片病害检测和土壤湿度评估的有效性。叶片检查周期完成时间为3.3-8.0秒，病害检测准确率最高达88%；土壤湿度评估准确率最高达64.3%。事后分析发现植物冠层形态是土壤湿度评估可靠性的显著预测因子（p<0.01），宽叶单叶冠层植物成功率100%，而密集复叶冠层植物仅16.7%，表明遮挡是主要限制因素而非操作员技能或控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室环境中的植物健康监测（叶片检查与土壤湿度评估）需要人机交互。传统的人工检查效率低且劳动密集，而全自动系统在复杂环境中可靠性不足。本研究旨在评估将虚拟现实（VR）与远程机器人操作相结合的方法，探索此类技术能否有效替代人工检查，并识别影响系统性能的关键限制因素。

Method: 系统由搭载相机的无人地面车辆（UGV）和机械臂组成，通过运动学模型控制导航和机械臂操作。实验在温室中对14株不同植物进行两组VR远程操作实验，设置了明确的研究问题和假设。叶片检查实验评估周期完成时间和病害检测准确性；土壤湿度评估实验评估浇水需求判定的成功率。采用统计检验（p值）评估实验间差异，并通过事后分析检验冠层形态等植物特性对成功率的预测作用。

Result: 叶片检查周期完成时间3.3-8.0秒，病害检测准确率最高达88%；第二次实验中病斑检测在数值上有提升但无统计学显著性（p=0.378）。土壤湿度评估在64.3%的植物（9/14）中成功判定浇水需求，但两次实验间提升无统计学显著性（p=0.50）。事后分析揭示冠层形态是强预测因子（p<0.01）：宽叶单叶冠层植物在第二次实验中达100%成功率，密集复叶冠层植物仅16.7%。操作员对密集冠层植物的操作速度提升但成功率未相应提高，表明相机遮挡是主导限制因素而非操作技能或努力不足。

Conclusion: VR远程操作用于温室植物检查具有可行性，但系统的关键瓶颈在于相机遮挡造成的传感限制，而非控制或训练缺陷。要提高系统准确性和鲁棒性，需要根据冠层密度调整相机视角和传感策略。

Abstract: This study evaluates the effectiveness of remote human-robot interaction using virtual reality for leaf inspection and soil moisture assessment in a greenhouse environment. The robotic system comprised an unmanned ground vehicle and a robotic manipulator equipped with cameras, governed by kinematic models for navigation and manipulator control. Fourteen distinct plants were inspected across two experiments utilizing VR teleoperation, guided by a set of pre-specified research questions and hypotheses. In the leaf inspection experiments, cycle completion times varied from 3.3 to 8.0 s, and plant-based disease detection was achieved up to 88% accuracy; diseased-spot detection improved numerically in the second experiment, though this change was not statistically significant (p=0.378). For soil moisture assessment, the experiments achieved successful determination of watering needs in up to 64.3% of plants (9 of 14), with consistent success observed for plants 1, 2, 3, 8, 9, 10, and 13; however, this improvement was likewise not statistically significant (p=0.50). A post hoc analysis instead revealed that soil moisture assessment reliability was strongly and significantly predicted by plant canopy morphology (p<0.01): plants with broad, single-leaf canopies reached 100% success by the second experiment, versus only 16.7% for dense, compound canopies. A secondary analysis showed operators became measurably faster at attempting dense-canopy plants without a corresponding gain in success, indicating that camera occlusion, not operator skill or effort, is the dominant limiting factor. These findings show occlusion imposes a sensing limitation rather than a control or training deficiency, and that adapting camera viewpoint and sensing strategy to canopy density is needed to improve the system's accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Data Scaling: Representation-Centric Continued Pre-training for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27550)
*Senqiao Yang,Chengyao Wang,Yuxin Chen,Zixuan Wang,Longxiang Tang,Haokun Gui,Jinhui Ye,Changsheng Lu,Xiaoyang Wu,Mingkang Zhu,Pengguang Chen,Shu Liu,Zhuotao Tian,Hengshuang Zhao,Bei Yu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAct，一种面向VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的VLM骨干网络，通过表示中心化的持续预训练（在多种embodiment机器人数据上预训练）来突破机器人数据稀缺的瓶颈。VLAct在模拟、真实世界和未见embodiment迁移中都显著提升了下游任务性能，在多个基准上超越工业级VLA系统，且仅用20%的下游轨迹数据就能超越使用全量数据的GR00T-N1.6基线，展示了在有限计算预算下表示质量相对于数据规模的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人轨迹数据的采集成本高昂且物理世界覆盖稀疏，导致机器人数据的规模化远难于网页级图文数据。在固定机器人数据预算下，预训练必须将有限的轨迹转化为可迁移的视觉-动作知识而非仅仅拟合动作，因此表示质量成为核心瓶颈。本文旨在解决如何在不扩大数据规模的前提下提升VLA模型表示质量的问题。

Method: 提出VLAct，一种VLA导向的VLM骨干网络，在任务特定微调之前，先在大规模、异构、多embodiment的机器人数据上进行持续预训练。其核心方法包括三个策略：(1) VLM先验保持——保留广泛的VLM先验知识；(2) 多头连续动作共监督——鼓励跨embodiment共享动作语义；(3) 部分统一的跨embodiment动作布局——在不同embodiment间统一动作表征，同时在微调阶段允许任务特定的动作头。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0上分别达到82.6%和92.5%的成功率，超越ABot-M0和LingBot-VLA等工业级系统；在RoboDojo上成功率排名第六并超越所有明确指定的世界动作模型（WAM）；在未见的人形embodiment RoboCasa-GR1上，仅用20%的下游轨迹数据就超越了使用全量数据的GR00T-N1.6基线。所有结果基于完全开源数据和仅16-GPU的训练配置。

Conclusion: 表示中心化的持续预训练是在有限数据预算和中等计算资源下提升VLA性能的重要独立研究方向，它与数据扩展并行而非替代关系。VLAct证明了在有限计算预算下通过精心设计的表示学习可以获得具有竞争力的性能，为VLA的进步提供了超越纯数据规模之外的新路径。

Abstract: Scaling robot data is crucial for building generalist Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, yet robot trajectories are harder to scale than web-scale image-text data because embodied collection is costly and sparsely covers the physical world. This makes representation quality a central bottleneck: under a fixed robot-data budget, continued pre-training must turn limited trajectories into transferable visual-action knowledge rather than merely fit actions. We propose VLAct, a VLA-oriented VLM backbone trained on broad, heterogeneous, multi-embodiment robot data before task-specific fine-tuning. VLAct preserves the broad VLM prior and encourages shared action semantics across embodiments through VLM-prior preservation, multi-head continuous action co-supervision, and a partially unified cross-embodiment action layout, while allowing task-specific action heads during fine-tuning. Across simulation, real-world, and unseen-embodiment transfer, VLAct consistently improves downstream performance under fixed fine-tuning protocols. On LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0, VLAct surpasses industrial VLA systems including ABot-M0 and LingBot-VLA, achieving success rates of 82.6% and 92.5%. On RoboDojo, VLAct ranks sixth among all policies by success rate and outperforms all explicitly designated world-action model (WAM) entries on both metrics. Most notably, on RoboCasa-GR1, an unseen humanoid embodiment, VLAct using only 20% of downstream trajectories outperforms the full-data GR00T-N1.6 baseline. These results are obtained using fully open-source data and only a 16-GPU training setup, showing that representation-centric continued pre-training can deliver highly competitive performance under a modest compute budget and is an important independent axis of VLA progress beyond data scaling.

</details>


### [244] [PHR-VLA: Planning Horizon Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27609)
*Davood Soleymanzadeh,Kaidi Zhang,Zhiyuan Zhang,Bihao Zhang,Xiao Liang,Yu She,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHR-VLA 通过在训练时将 VLA 的内部表征与从未来观测中提取的潜在动力学对齐，引入了规划时域推理能力，提升了机器人精细操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）仅基于当前观测预测动作，缺乏对未来任务动态进行显式推理的机制，这在需要细粒度、接触丰富的操作任务中尤为重要。

Method: PHR-VLA 引入一个轻量级的辅助"未来头"（future head），在训练阶段将 VLA 的内部表征与从未来观测中提取的潜在动力学表示进行对齐。该方法支持从腕部相机和第三人称相机提取层级的潜在动力学监督。

Result: 局部、接触中心、patch 级别的腕部相机潜在动力学监督将 LIBERO 成功率从 84.1% 提升至 88.4%，真实世界拆卸任务从 63.3% 提升至 82.5%；第三人称相机的 patch 级别监督将 Meta-World 从 56.70% 提升至 57.8%。

Conclusion: 特权潜在动力学对齐为增强 VLA 策略的预期性推理提供了有效的训练信号，显著改善了机器人在精细操作任务中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have shown strong promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation by mapping language instructions and vision observations directly to actions. However, most VLAs primarily condition action prediction on current observations and lack an explicit mechanism for reasoning over future task dynamics, which is particularly important for fine-grained, contact-rich manipulation. We present PHR-VLA, a framework that enables planning-horizon reasoning in VLAs through privileged latent representations of future dynamics. PHR-VLA introduces a lightweight auxiliary future head that, during training, aligns the VLA's internal representations with latent dynamics extracted from future observations. Evaluation results demonstrate that local, contact-centric, patch-level latent dynamics supervision from the wrist camera improves success rate on LIBERO from 84.1% to 88.4% and on real-world disassembly tasks from 63.3% to 82.5%. Patch-level supervision from a third-person camera also improves performance on Meta-World from 56.70% to 57.8%. These results demonstrate that privileged latent dynamics alignment provides an effective training signal for improving anticipatory reasoning in VLA policies. Project website: \href{https://davoodsz.github.io/PHR-VLA.github.io/}{https://davoodsz.github.io/PHR-VLA.github.io/}

</details>


### [245] [One year in a forest: Analyzing the challenges of autonomous navigation in subarctic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27628)
*Matěj Boxan,Nicolas Lauzon,Veronica Vannini,Mathis Turgeon-Roy,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报告了在亚北极北方森林中为期一年的移动机器人部署现场试验，评估了9种里程计、定位与建图方法在64公里数据上的跨季节性能表现。结果表明季节性环境变化显著损害了先进技术的性能，复杂SLAM算法相对本体感觉基线只有有限的精度提升却显著增加系统脆弱性，视觉SLAM对季节变化尤为敏感，而雷达和视觉方法在跨季节定位中容易失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚北极地区有望在林业、采矿和环境监测等领域大规模部署自主机器人，但这些环境中GNSS和云计算因茂密树冠和大气衰减而不可靠，需要机载感知与数据处理。然而现有的外感受模态（相机、激光雷达、雷达）通常在结构化城市环境或无显著季节变化的环境中评估，缺乏在具有强烈季节性变化场景下的系统性验证，因此需要开展跨季节的长期实地评估。

Method: 在亚北极北方森林中进行为期一年的移动机器人部署，收集64公里数据，使用9种里程计、定位和建图方法进行评估，涵盖多个季节的性能对比。研究还通过将位置漂移与特征和置信度权重分布关联分析视觉SLAM对季节变化的敏感性，并进行了跨季节先验地图定位实验以及多季节的Teach and Repeat（T&R）评估（使用雷达和激光雷达管线）。

Result: 实验发现：1）剧烈的环境变化显著妨碍了最先进技术的性能，在自相似场景或高雪堆条件下的脆弱性增加；2）复杂SLAM算法相比本体感觉基线只有有限的精度增益但显著增加系统脆弱性；3）视觉SLAM方法特别受季节变化影响；4）激光雷达方法能成功完成跨季节定位，而雷达和视觉方法即使在同一季节内也因匹配特征稀少而容易失败。

Conclusion: 亚北极森林的剧烈季节变化对自主导航系统的性能构成重大挑战，当前最先进的SLAM技术在非结构化季节性环境中的鲁棒性不足。激光雷达方法显示出更强的跨季节鲁棒性，而视觉和雷达方法需要进一步改进以应对特征匹配稀疏的问题。该研究为在极端季节性环境中设计可靠的自主系统提供了重要的实证依据和经验教训。

Abstract: Subarctic regions have the potential to see increased deployment of autonomous robots in applications including forestry, mining, and environmental monitoring. In these conditions, an autonomous system's reliance on GNSS or cloud computing is precarious due to dense tree canopies and atmospheric attenuation, necessitating onboard sensing and data processing. However, established exteroceptive modalities, including cameras, lidars, and radars, are typically evaluated in structured urban settings or in environments that lack significant seasonal variations. To address this, we present a field report on a year-long deployment of a mobile robot in a subarctic boreal forest. We evaluate 64 km of data using nine odometry, localization, and mapping methods and assess their performance across seasonal changes. The performed experiments suggest that the environment changes significantly hinder the performance of state-of-the-art techniques, which show increased fragility when subject to conditions characterized by self-similar scenes or tall snowbanks. Additionally, complex Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) algorithms offer limited accuracy gains over a proprioceptive baseline while significantly increasing system fragility. Furthermore, by correlating the position drift with features and confidence weight distribution, we show that visual-based SLAM methods are particularly affected by the seasonal changes. Additionally, we investigate the task of cross-season localization in a prior map. While lidar-based methods successfully completed localization runs between seasons, radar and visual methods are prone to failure due to a few matching features between runs, even within the same season. Finally, we detail the challenges and lessons learned from this year-long trial, including a multi-season Teach and Repeat (T&R) evaluation using both radar and lidar-based pipelines.

</details>


### [246] [Distributed Model-Based Diffusion: Finite Horizon Contraction under Bounded Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27685)
*Seth Golembeski,Keith L. Gibson,Alexander Gross,Shreyas Kousik,Anirban Mazumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种分布式基于模型的扩散算法（Distributed Model-Based Diffusion），用于多智能体非线性非凸系统的模型预测控制，并证明其在通信延迟下具有收缩性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体轨迹联合优化面临非线性、非凸性和维度灾难的挑战，且智能体间的通信延迟进一步增加了求解难度。现有方法通常依赖大量简化假设，难以应用于真实世界场景。

Method: 提出分布式基于模型的扩散方法，这是一种基于采样的模型预测控制算法，适用于高度非线性、非凸、非光滑的多智能体系统，并给出了针对多智能体非凸问题的收缩性和对延迟鲁棒性的理论证明。

Result: 在circleswap任务、中保真度协作驾驶任务和空中战斗场景中进行了测试。相比集中式基于模型的扩散方法，在引入延迟的情况下，circleswap完工时间改善了31%，空中战斗胜率提高了25%。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式算法在存在通信延迟的条件下，不仅具有理论上的收缩性与鲁棒性保证，在实际任务中相比集中式方法还展现出显著的性能优势，证明了其适用于真实世界的多智能体约束场景。

Abstract: Simultaneously optimizing the trajectories of multiple agents is a challenging problem plagued by nonlinearity, nonconvexity, and the curse of dimensionality. A collection of interacting aerial vehicles or self-driving cars in an intersection are examples of complex multi-agent systems that remain difficult to solve without many simplifying assumptions. The presence of communication latency between agents further increases the difficulty. In this paper, we analyze Distributed Model-Based Diffusion: a sampling-based Model-Predictive Control method suitable for highly nonlinear, nonconvex, nonsmooth, multi-agent systems. We prove contraction and robustness to latency for multi-agent, nonconvex problems, showing applicability to real-world constraints. We test the algorithm on a circleswap task, a cooperative medium-fidelity driving task, and in an aerial combat scenario. Despite the addition of latency, our algorithm improves circleswap makespan by 31% and increases aerial combat win rate by 25% compared to centralized Model-Based Diffusion.

</details>


### [247] [Coordinated Motion Planning for Multi-Arm Systems via Iterative LQ Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27726)
*Junyoung Kim,Hanwen Ren,Lei Zhang,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于迭代线性二次（LQ）博弈的框架，用于多机械臂共享工作空间中的运动规划。每个机械臂被建模为独立智能体，通过求解一系列局部LQ博弈（Riccati反向递归得到反馈Nash策略）来实现碰撞感知的轨迹生成，并结合自碰撞和臂间碰撞的可微惩罚项，实验证明该框架在高维场景中能生成平滑、安全且高效的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机械臂共享工作空间中的运动规划仍是基础且具挑战性的问题。集中式规划器可扩展性差，去中心化方法面临鲁棒性和安全性问题。博弈论为建模智能体交互提供了有前景的思路，但应用于关节式多臂系统仍有限，因此需要一种适用于多机械臂的高效博弈论运动规划方法。

Method: 提出迭代LQ博弈框架：(1) 将每个机械臂建模为独立智能体，基于共享全局状态和碰撞约束优化自身目标；(2) 通过线性化动力学并沿名义轨迹近似代价，求解一系列局部LQ博弈；(3) 利用Riccati反向递归获得反馈Nash策略；(4) 在优化管线中引入自碰撞和臂间碰撞的可微惩罚项，实现协调的碰撞感知轨迹生成。

Result: 实验表明该框架能在高维设置下生成平滑、安全且高效的轨迹，性能优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了微分博弈公式对多机器人操作的有效性，为多机械臂共享工作空间的运动规划提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Multi-agent motion planning for high-degree-of-freedom robotics manipulators in shared workspaces remains a fundamental yet challenging problem. Centralized planners often suffer from poor scalability, while decentralized approaches face robustness and safety concerns. Game-theoretic formulations offer a promising approach for modeling agent interactions, potentially overcoming these limitations. However, their application to articulated multi-arm systems remains limited. This paper presents an iterative Linear Quadratic (LQ) game framework for multi-manipulator motion planning, where each manipulator is modeled as an independent agent optimizing its own objective while interacting with other agents based on shared global states and collision constraints. The method solves a series of local LQ games by linearizing the dynamics and approximating the cost around a nominal trajectory, with Riccati backward recursions yielding feedback Nash strategies. To address the challenges of articulated systems, we incorporate differentiable penalties for self-collision and inter-arm collision into the optimization pipeline, enabling coordinated, collision-aware trajectory generation. Experiments demonstrate that our framework produces smooth, safe, and efficient trajectories in high-dimensional settings, outperforming traditional methods. This highlights the effectiveness of differential game formulations for multi-robot manipulation.

</details>


### [248] [CAVE-NAV: VLM-Based Autonomous 3D Navigation in Underwater Cave Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27793)
*Zhenqi Wu,Yuanjie Lu,Yisheng Zhang,Miao Yu,Xuesu Xiao,Jaejeong Shin,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）与思维链（CoT）推理的水下洞穴自主导航框架，利用RGB图像、深度图和声纳垂直间隙测量等多模态输入推断可航行方向，实现受限洞穴通道中的安全3D导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下洞穴中传统导航系统依赖密集视觉特征，但视觉退化会破坏基于特征的定位；声纳建图可能产生过度保守的障碍物表示；通信限制排除了实时人工引导。这些限制使得传统方法难以在洞穴环境中实现可靠自主导航。

Method: 提出一个利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）结合思维链（CoT）推理的自主导航框架，从光照强度梯度、通道形态和几何复杂度等环境线索中推断可航行方向，融合RGB图像、深度图和声纳垂直间隙测量等多模态输入，以支持受限洞穴通道中的安全3D导航。

Result: 在多种洞穴拓扑的高保真仿真中，该框架在所有评估的端到端穿越任务中均无碰撞完成，并保持与洞穴边界的足够安全距离。

Conclusion: 所提出的VLM+CoT框架能够有效克服水下洞穴环境中视觉特征退化、声纳建图保守以及通信受限等问题，实现可靠的无碰撞自主导航，验证了多模态感知与语言模型推理结合在该场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in underwater cave environments is essential for search-and-rescue operations, scientific exploration, and emergency egress. Traditional navigation systems commonly depend on dense visual features for localization and mapping. In underwater caves, however, visual degradation can undermine feature-based localization, sonar-based mapping may yield overly conservative obstacle representations, and communication constraints preclude real-time human guidance. To address these limitations, we propose an autonomous underwater cave navigation framework that leverages a vision-language model (VLM) with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to infer navigable directions from environmental cues, including light intensity gradients, passage morphology, and geometric complexity, captured through multimodal inputs comprising RGB imagery, depth maps, and sonar-based vertical-clearance measurements, thereby supporting safe 3D navigation through confined cave passages. High-fidelity simulations across multiple cave topologies demonstrate that the proposed framework completes all evaluated end-to-end traversals without collisions while maintaining safe clearance from cave boundaries.

</details>


### [249] [Plan Along the Way: Event-Triggered Foundation-Model Planning for TAMP Execution in Partially Observable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28075)
*Puru Ojha,Narendhiran Vijayakumar,Nav Singhal,Girish Varma,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROBUST TAMP，一种模块化的LLM/VLM引导的规划框架，用于部分可观测环境下的反应式任务与运动规划（TAMP）。该框架将基础模型规划器限制在当前可见的关联场景状态中，通过严格的执行接口验证生成的任务级动作，并针对对象发现事件触发场景重建与重规划。在六个RLBench/CoppeliaSim环境变体中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中的操作需要在不完整场景信息下进行规划，现有基础模型引导的TAMP系统通常假设拥有完全指定的场景状态，或在子目标/细化/执行失败后才进行模型级重规划，无法有效处理执行过程中新对象（包括隐藏的任务相关对象和非目标对象）突然可见的情况。

Method: 1) 将基础模型规划器限制在当前可见的关联场景状态；2) 通过严格的执行接口验证生成的任务级动作；3) 将验收的动作路由到场景特定的执行适配器；4) 将对象发现视为独立的重规划事件，在稳定的执行视界后重建场景状态并使用完成动作历史和结构化重规划事件上下文进行重规划。

Result: 在六个RLBench/CoppeliaSim厨房和烧烤环境变体上进行了评估，涉及隐藏对象、非目标对象发现、铰接容器交互和时间操作流程等场景，系统比较了不同规模的纯文本LLM和VLM规划器，报告了任务成功率、部分目标完成度、发现/失败触发的重规划行为、隐式非目标对象处理以及规划器推理成本等指标。

Conclusion: 该工作表明将基础模型规划器限制在可见场景状态并显式处理对象发现事件，能够提升部分可观测环境下TAMP的鲁棒性和任务完成能力，同时通过统一的验证、执行、监控和重规划管线对不同规模的LLM/VLM规划器进行了公平比较。

Abstract: Manipulation in partially observable environments requires planning under incomplete scene information. In such settings, an initially valid plan may execute successfully yet remain insufficient for task completion. Existing foundation-model-guided task and motion planning (TAMP) systems can generate useful long-horizon task decompositions, subgoals, or constraints, but they often assume having access to a fully specified scene state or invoke model-level replanning after a subgoal, refinement, or execution attempt fails. We present ROBUST TAMP, a modular LLM/VLM-guided planning framework for reactive TAMP where unseen task-relevant and non-target objects may become visible during execution. The framework restricts the foundation-model planner to the currently visible relational scene state, validates generated task-level actions against a strict executable interface, and routes the accepted actions to scene-specific execution adapters. Object discovery is treated as a distinct replanning event and, after a stable execution horizon, the system reconstructs the visible scene state and replans using completed-action history and structured replanning event context. Evaluations are performed on six RLBench/CoppeliaSim kitchen and grill variants involving hidden objects, non-target object discovery, articulated-container interaction, and temporal manipulation procedures. We compare text-only LLM and VLM planners of different sizes under the same validation, execution, monitoring, and replanning pipeline, reporting task success, partial goal completion, discovery- and failure-triggered replanning behavior, implicit non-target-object handling, and planner inference cost.

</details>


### [250] [Stay Seated: Learning Omnidirectional Humanoid Locomotion on a Passive Mobile Chair with Casters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28090)
*Kango Yanagida,Kazuki Miyazawa,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究人形机器人在被动移动椅子上的全方位坐姿移动（seated locomotion）。通过扩展标准站立速度跟踪环境，加入被动椅子模型、坐姿奖励、评论家专用椅子观测和任务定制接触设置，策略仅使用本体感觉和速度命令（无接触传感或椅子状态）即在随机命令评估中几乎全程跟踪成功，部分坐姿策略在速度跟踪上优于站立策略。消融实验显示脚滑正则化降低CoT但增加跟踪误差，与对称正则化或命令课程结合可避免局部最优。策略零样本仿真到实物迁移至Unitree G1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 站立人形机器人准直驱执行器需持续产生关节力矩，而人类坐姿工作时将体重支撑委托给椅子。为迈向坐姿移动操作（seated loco-manipulation），本文首先研究被动移动椅子上的全方位坐姿移动，其特点是骨盆-座椅接触不固定以及机器人-椅子系统间歇性足地推进。

Method: 在标准站立速度跟踪环境中扩展：(1)被动椅子模型；(2)坐姿状态奖励；(3)仅评论家使用的椅子观测；(4)任务定制接触设置。策略无需运动模仿奖励，actor仅使用本体感觉和速度命令，不含接触传感或椅子状态。通过2^3全因子实验比较对称正则化（SY）、脚滑正则化（FS）和命令课程（CC）的组合效果。

Result: 随机命令评估中策略在几乎全部20秒回合内跟踪全方位命令；最佳坐姿策略在速度跟踪上优于站立策略。FS降低CoT（阻力系数）但增加跟踪误差，部分仅FS策略收敛到静止局部最优；FS与SY或CC结合可避免此失败。SY改善纵向运动时双腿对称性。方向分解显示CoT排序为后退<侧移<<前进，后两种方向带植腿伸展，前进方向在脚跟触地后出现膝关节屈曲。零样本迁移至Unitree G1成功。

Conclusion: 本文验证了无需运动模仿、仅依赖本体感觉和速度命令即可在被动椅子上学习全方位坐姿移动，且部分性能优于站立策略。脚滑正则化需与对称正则化或命令课程组合以避免静止局部最优。该工作为坐姿移动操作提供了首步基础，并展示了零样本sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots with quasi-direct-drive actuators continuously generate joint torque while standing, whereas seated humans delegate weight support to chairs during desk work. As a first step toward seated loco-manipulation, we study omnidirectional seated locomotion on a passive mobile chair, requiring unfixed pelvis-seat contact and intermittent foot-floor propulsion of the robot-chair system. We extend a standard standing velocity-tracking environment with a passive-chair model, seated-state rewards, critic-only chair observations, and task-tailored contact settings. The policy is learned without motion-imitation rewards; its actor uses only proprioception and velocity commands, without contact sensing or chair states. In random-command evaluation, the policies tracked omnidirectional commands through nearly all 20-s rollouts, and the best seated policies could outperform the Standing policy in velocity tracking. Across four training seeds, a $2^3$ full-factorial comparison of symmetry regularization (SY), foot-slip regularization (FS), and command curriculum (CC) showed that FS reduced CoT but increased tracking error and that some FS-only policies converged to stationary local optima. Combining FS with either SY or CC avoided this failure without retuning FS, while SY improved bilateral leg symmetry during longitudinal motion. Direction-resolved analysis showed CoT ordered backward $<$ lateral $\ll$ forward, with planted-leg extension in backward and lateral motion and knee flexion following heel contact in forward motion. The learned policy achieved zero-shot sim-to-real transfer to a Unitree G1 and generated omnidirectional seated locomotion.

</details>


### [251] [DeicticVLA: Unifying Instruction Modes Based on Language and Deictic Gestures in a Single VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28108)
*Kango Yanagida,Tatsuya Aoki,Yuichiro Yoshikawa,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeicticVLA框架，将语言指令、视觉-语言指令和纯视觉指令三种模态统一为文本提示加指示性掩码，使得一个预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型能够同时处理三种指令模式，并通过两阶段训练策略在真实世界任务中显著超越传统语言指令方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）允许用户用自然语言指定操作任务，但在区分同类别或外观相似的物体时，需要极其详细的描述，而VLA可能无法可靠地使用这些详细表达。因此需要一种更直观、更鲁棒的指令接口来消除物体歧义。

Method: DeicticVLA将语言指令（LI）、视觉-语言指令（VLI）和视觉指令（VI）统一规范化到文本提示和指示性掩码中，通过文本提示补全和指示性手势定位实现。使用共享骨干网络、相同演示数据和匹配的训练步数，在仿真中对比了两种RGB视觉提示方法、两种分离通道掩码提示方法和三种训练策略（两阶段训练、联合训练等）。

Result: 在仿真中，两阶段训练下四种提示方法均达到较高的分布内成功率，但在未见布局中使用指示性掩码的能力存在差异。训练策略消融表明两阶段训练提升了指示性掩码的使用能力，而保留第二阶段的语言指令数据可缓解遗忘且不降低VLI和VI性能。在三个真实世界任务中，单个策略支持所有模式。对于未见类别，VLI和VI均达到100%成功率，而联合训练的LI仅16.7%。

Conclusion: DeicticVLA实现了三种指令模式的统一接口，VLI和VI在未见表达、外观变化和新颖物体场景下显著优于LI，证明了指示性输入在消除歧义和泛化方面的优势，并为DeicticVLA的设计提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) allow users to specify manipulation tasks in natural language, but distinguishing a target or placement goal among objects of the same category or similar appearance requires detailed expressions that VLAs may not use reliably. We propose DeicticVLA, which canonicalizes Language Instruction (LI), Vision-Language Instruction (VLI), and Visual Instruction (VI) into a text prompt and deictic masks through text-prompt completion and deictic gesture grounding, enabling a single pretrained VLA to handle all three instruction modes. With a shared backbone, demonstrations, and matched training steps, we compare two RGB visual prompting methods, two separate-channel mask prompting methods, and three training strategies in simulation. Under two-stage training, the four prompting methods achieve high in-distribution success but differ in their ability to use deictic masks in unseen layouts. Across methods, training-strategy ablations show that two-stage training improves such use, while retaining second-stage LI data mitigates forgetting without reducing VLI and VI performance. In three real-world tasks, one policy supports all modes. VLI and VI outperform LI under unseen expressions, appearance changes, and novel objects. For unseen categories, both achieve 100% success, compared with 16.7% for jointly trained LI. These results demonstrate the unified three-mode interface and guide DeicticVLA design.

</details>


### [252] [Contact-Guided Exploration for Non-Prehensile Locomanipulation with Multi-Critic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28140)
*Simone Tolomei,Mayank Mittal,Franco Angelini,Manolo Garabini,Paolo Salaris,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于接触引导探索的多批评家强化学习框架，通过任务相关的密集接触奖励引导末端执行器探索有意义的接触点，并渐进衰减其影响以恢复任务最优策略，适用于非抓取移动操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取操作因混合动力学复杂性和接触稀疏性，导致基于模型和无模型的方法都面临挑战，需要有效的探索策略来引导智能体发现有意义接触点。

Method: 在多批评家强化学习框架中，训练一个专门的探索批评家，使用密集的接触追求奖励引导末端执行器趋向有意义的接触点；该批评家的影响随训练进度逐步衰减，以恢复任务最优策略。候选交互点来自通用抓取算法，使探索机制能泛化到不同物体几何形状。

Result: 在箱子推动、椅子搬运和洗碗机开门等多项任务上进行了评估，并在四足移动操作平台上对椅子搬运策略进行了大量真实世界实验验证。

Conclusion: 该接触引导探索策略能够有效应对非抓取操作的混合动力学和接触稀疏性挑战，并在真实世界四足移动操作平台上实现了可部署的非抓取操作。

Abstract: Non-prehensile manipulation offers versatile skills for moving and rearranging heavy or bulky objects, particularly when combined with a mobile manipulation platform. However, both model-based and model-free approaches struggle with the complex hybrid dynamics and the sparsity of the contact in these tasks. To address these challenges, we propose a contact-guided exploration strategy implemented within a Multi-Critic Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework. A dedicated exploration critic is trained with a dense contact-seeking reward that guides the end-effector toward meaningful contact points; its influence is progressively decayed to recover a task-optimal policy. We obtain candidate interaction points from a general-purpose grasping algorithm, enabling the exploration mechanism to generalise across various object geometries. We evaluate the approach on multiple tasks, including box pushing, chair transportation, and a dishwasher opening task. Finally, we validate the chair transportation policy through extensive experiments on a quadrupedal mobile manipulator, demonstrating deployable non-prehensile manipulation in the real world.

</details>


### [253] [From Small Talk to Rapport: Exploring Robot Self-Disclosure in Collaborative Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28154)
*Kaitlynn Taylor Pineda,Anvii Mishra,Brian Chien,Angela Guo,Toluwani Williams,Ziang Xiao,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了人机协作中机器人自我表露（self-disclosure）对话策略的设计。开发了由LLM驱动的机械臂，采用低表露或高表露两种策略进行闲聊，并通过50人用户研究意外发现：低表露条件下参与者反而自我表露更多，且报告更强的团队协作与协调感，该效应在有机器人协作经验的用户中更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们在协作中自然地闲聊并分享个人信息以建立默契和维系社交联系。随着机器人越来越多地与人共同工作，如何有效设计这些社交行为以增强参与感和支持团队协作变得尤为重要。虽然已有研究表明机器人发起的闲聊有益于人机协作，但关于如何设计这类闲聊（特别是自我表露策略）仍缺乏深入研究。

Method: 研究分两步进行：（1）开发了一个LLM驱动的工业机械臂，使其能够参与闲聊，并采用低自我表露与高自我表露两种策略；（2）进行了N=50的用户研究，比较这两种自我表露策略对人机互动动态的影响，重点关注参与者自我表露程度、团队感与协调感的差异。

Result: 与预期相反，低自我表露条件下参与者的自我表露程度更高，并报告了更强的团队协作与协调感。该效应在曾有机器人协作经验的用户中尤为显著。结果表明，提高机器人自我表露并不必然促进默契建立、社交联系或互动式自我表露。

Conclusion: 在工业机械臂场景下，增加机器人的自我表露并不一定有利于人机协作关系的建立；相反，低表露策略可能更有效。研究者建议在设计机器人小聊时，需考虑用户先前的HRI经验等其他因素，而非一味追求更高的自我表露水平。

Abstract: People naturally chat while collaborating and share personal information (i.e., self-disclose) to build rapport and maintain social connections. As robots are increasingly developed to work with people, the effective use of these social behaviors to enhance engagement and support teamwork becomes ever more important. While prior work has shown that robot-initiated small talk can benefit human-robot collaboration, less is known about how best to design such small talk. In this work, we explore how self-disclosure may be designed to support small talk within a human-robot team---especially when the robot is an industrial manipulator that lacks anthropomorphic cues and performs physical work. We first developed an LLM-driven manipulator capable of partaking in small talk, adopting either a low-disclosure or high-disclosure strategy. We then conducted a user study (N = 50) to investigate how self-disclosure in small talk influences human-robot dynamics. Unexpectedly, participants disclosed more in the low-disclosure condition and reported stronger teaming and coordination than those in the high-disclosure condition. This effect was more pronounced among users with prior experience teaming with robots. These results suggest that increasing robot self-disclosure does not necessarily foster rapport, social connection, or reciprocal disclosure; other factors, such as prior HRI experience, should be considered.

</details>


### [254] [Picking Bins Empty: A Hierarchical Hybrid Approach with Online Self-Learning of Grasp Points for Reliable Industrial Bin-Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28175)
*Florian Töper,Samarth Kishor Yelvande,Jan Niklas Ewertz,Rudolph Triebel,Peter Ohlhausen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种四层分层混合方法，结合基于模型的抓取管线（作为鲁棒主干）与无模型探索代理（解决死锁并发现新抓取点），辅以在线自学习机制（利用夹持器行程反馈和Wilson得分区间自动排序抓取候选），在三个汽车零件上实现100%的料箱清空率（基线仅50.9%），显著提升了抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 料箱抓取是现代制造的核心，但完全自动化的料箱清空仍是关键挑战。基于模型的方法精度高但容易因抓取点被遮挡或感知失败而陷入死锁，且新零件需要大量人工调整；无模型方法灵活但缺乏生产所需的可靠性。现有工作将两种技术割裂处理，缺乏结合两者优势的统一框架。

Method: 提出四层分层混合架构：底层是基于模型的管线作为鲁棒主干；上层引入无模型的"探索代理"处理死锁情况并发现新抓取点；再辅以在线自学习机制，利用夹持器行程反馈和Wilson得分区间对抓取候选进行自主排序，减少人工调试工作量。

Result: 在三个汽车零件上验证：混合方法显著优于无模型基线（抓取成功率更高），并将基于模型基线的料箱清空率从50.9%提升至100%，在所有实验中实现了完全的料箱清空。

Conclusion: 实现从部分清空到完全清空的转变，标志着朝着真正自主、无需人工干预的工业生产操作迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Bin-picking is a cornerstone of modern manufacturing, yet achieving complete bin clearance without manual intervention remains a critical challenge. While model-based methods provide high precision, they frequently suffer from deadlocks when predefined grasps are occluded or perception fails. Labor-intensive fine-tuning of grasp points is commonly required to reach a satisfactory performance for new parts. Model-free algorithms offer a more flexible alternative with "out-of-the-box" versatility but lack the reliability and repeatability required for production. Unlike existing work, which treats the two techniques in isolation, we propose a fourtiered hierarchical hybrid approach to combine the best of both worlds. A model-based pipeline serves as a robust backbone, while a model-free "exploration agent" resolves deadlock situations and discovers new grasp points. This is supported by an online self-learning mechanism that uses gripper-stroke feedback and Wilson score intervals to autonomously rank grasp candidates, reducing manual commissioning effort. Validation on three automotive parts demonstrates that our method significantly outperforms a model-free baseline in grasp success rate while improving the bin clearance rate of the model-based baseline from 50.9% to 100% across all experiments. This transition to full bin clearance marks a significant step towards truly autonomous, intervention-free industrial operation.

</details>


### [255] [PAMoR: Parameterized Affective Motion Generation in Real Time for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28213)
*Yan Pan,Lingfan Bao,Tianhu Peng,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAMoR提出一种将情感（affect）转化为可量化控制参数的方法，通过从机器人运动学中闭合形式计算效价-唤醒（V-A）坐标作为生成条件，无需人工标注，在29-DoF Unitree G1机器人上实现实时全身动作生成，同时保持动作与情感的可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感驱动的运动生成主要针对人类化身，且风格来源于参考片段或情感词，这些都无法进行定量参数化。作者旨在将情感转化为可测量的控制参数，使机器人运动生成能够定量控制情感表达。

Method: PAMoR从机器人运动学中闭合形式计算姿态扩张（postural expansion）和运动能量（movement energy），得到效价-唤醒（V-A）坐标作为生成条件。采用共享潜在空间中训练的动作先验和两个情感先验，在每个去噪步骤中组合：动作先验确定执行什么动作，情感先验调制如何执行。整个身体运动在29-DoF Unitree G1上以自回归方式实时生成，动作和情感均可编辑。

Result: 生成的移动在完整范围内跟踪指令的V-A坐标，同时文本到动作的保真度与纯文本基线相当。在感知研究中，评估者在38%的试验中识别出指令情感，高于两个基线，接近真人表演报告的44%。

Conclusion: PAMoR成功将情感转化为可量化的V-A控制参数，实现了机器人情感化运动生成的定量控制，且识别率接近人类动作表演的水平，证明了从机器人运动学派生情感测量的有效性。

Abstract: People read a humanoid robot's motion in social settings not only for the action performed but for the affect conveyed. Motion carrying that affect has so far been generated for human avatars, where style is taken from a reference clip or an emotion word, neither of which can be quantitatively parameterized. We present PAMoR, which turns affect into a measured control parameter: a valence-arousal (V-A) coordinate computed natively on robot kinematics. It is obtained in closed form from postural expansion and movement energy, and these measurements serve directly as generation conditions, with no human annotation. An action prior and two affect priors, trained in a shared latent space, are composed at each denoising step: the action prior fixes what is performed, the affect priors modulate how. Whole-body motion rolls out autoregressively on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 in real time, with action and affect both editable. Generated motion tracks the commanded V-A over its full range while text-to-motion fidelity still matches text-only baselines. In a perceptual study, raters identify the commanded emotion on 0.38 of trials, above both baselines and approaching the 0.44 reported for acted human bodies.

</details>


### [256] [Probabilistic Multi-Robot Gas Source Localization with Uncalibrated Sensors: A Distributed Estimation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28214)
*Wanting Jin,Marc Zoel Arias Mitjà,Alcherio Martinoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式概率框架，用于多机器人源定位任务，在传感器未校准且异构的情况下实现免校准估计。核心思想是利用基于秩的特征捕捉观测的相对变化，该特征对传感器缩放和非线性具有不变性；各机器人独立估计局部信念，并通过专家乘积方式融合为全局一致的估计；同时引入信息区域分配与路径规划策略以提高团队协调效率。高保真仿真验证了该方法在强传感器异构条件下显著优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统估计环境状态时，若配备未校准且异构的传感器，其非线性和不一致的响应会阻碍可靠的信息融合，导致源定位等任务难以获得准确的全局估计。现有方法通常依赖传感器校准，而这在实际部署中代价高昂且难以实现。因此，亟需一种能够在传感器异构条件下仍能有效融合信息的免校准分布式估计框架。

Method: 1）每个机器人利用基于秩的特征独立估计局部信念，该特征反映观测的相对演化，对传感器缩放和非线性具有不变性；2）通过专家乘积（Product of Experts）公式融合各局部信念，获得团队一致性的全局估计；3）引入信息丰富的区域分配与路径规划策略，减少冗余探索并在探索与利用之间取得平衡。

Result: 在高保真仿真中使用真实气体传感器模型进行验证。结果表明，所提出的方法在强传感器异构条件下显著优于基于标准测量聚合的基准方法，能够实现可靠的源定位精度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了免校准传感表示可以有效地扩展到分布式机器人系统，为其他涉及异构传感器的估计任务提供了新的思路和可行路径。

Abstract: Estimating environmental states with multi-robot systems becomes particularly challenging when robots are equipped with uncalibrated and therefore heterogeneous sensors, whose nonlinear and inconsistent responses prevent reliable information fusion. In this paper, we propose a distributed probabilistic framework for source localization tasks that enables calibration-free estimation in the presence of sensor heterogeneity. The key idea is that each robot independently estimates a local belief using a rank-based feature that captures the relative evolution of observations and is invariant to sensor scaling and nonlinearities. These local beliefs are then fused through a product of experts formulation to obtain a consistent global estimate across the team. To further improve the efficiency of team coordination, we introduce an informative region allocation and path planning strategy that reduces redundant exploration while balancing exploration and exploitation. We validate the proposed framework using high-fidelity simulations with realistic gas sensor models. Results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms a benchmark method based on standard measurement aggregation, achieving reliable source localization accuracy despite strong sensor heterogeneity. More broadly, this work demonstrates how calibration-free sensing representations can be effectively extended to distributed robotic systems, paving the way for their application to other estimation tasks involving heterogeneous sensors.

</details>


### [257] [Training-free Suction Grasp Detection for Deformed Aseptic Cartons Using Vision-Language Models and Geometric Surface Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28246)
*Marin Maletic,Goran Vasiljevic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练吸盘抓取系统，用于分拣变形的无菌饮料纸盒。系统通过开放词汇视觉语言模型进行目标识别，SAM2生成实例掩码，几何评分方法选择吸盘抓取点，在真实机器人上对不同形变程度的35个杂乱场景进行测试，单物体抓取成功率88.2%，端到端杂乱环境回收率72.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可回收废弃物分拣中，目标物体具有可变形性和几何不一致性（如压扁的饮料纸盒），传统抓取方法难以处理。文章旨在解决这一挑战，将目标识别与抓取点选择解耦，实现无需训练的吸盘抓取系统。

Method: 采用解耦架构：1）开放词汇视觉语言模型（OV-VLM）基于文本提示检测纸盒；2）SAM2将检测结果细化为实例掩码；3）几何评分方法结合表面平坦度和法线对齐选择吸盘点。比较了三种几何方法：k近邻PCA、Sobel叉积、RANSAC平面拟合。

Result: 在真实机器人上，针对三种形变程度和35个杂乱场景进行测试，单物体抓取成功率达88.2%，端到端杂乱环境回收率为72.6%。

Conclusion: 该免训练系统能有效处理变形纸盒的分拣任务，通过解耦识别与抓取点选择，结合几何评分方法，在保持无需训练的优势下取得了较高的抓取和回收成功率。

Abstract: Robotic sorting of recyclable waste is challenging due to the deformable and geometrically inconsistent nature of target objects. We present a training-free suction grasping system for sorting deformed aseptic beverage cartons, decoupling target identification from grasp-point selection. An open-vocabulary vision-language model detects cartons from a text prompt, SAM2 refines each detection into an instance mask, and a geometric scoring method selects the suction point by combining surface flatness with normal alignment. Three geometric methods are compared: k-nearest-neighbour PCA, Sobel cross-product, and RANSAC plane fitting. Evaluated on a real robot across three deformation levels and 35 cluttered scenes, single-object grasp success reaches 88.2% and end-to-end retrieval in clutter is 72.6%.

</details>


### [258] [CoCoBench: A Cooperative Coordination Benchmark for Embodied Multi-Agent Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28266)
*Yang Chen,Ye-Xin Xie,Lirong Che,Danyang Peng,Yuzhe Yang,Peiwen Lin,Xu Cao,Chuang Wang,Lei Yuan,Jian Su,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoBench，一个面向可执行家务任务的多智能体具身协调benchmark，包含897个经oracle验证的实例，围绕任务分配、顺序约束、互斥和交接协调四种协调构念进行细粒度评估，并提供构念级评分以诊断协调失败。在11个主流MLLM上的实验表明协调能力具有高度构念特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体benchmark对多智能体协调缺乏细粒度诊断能力。大多数benchmark要么只关注单智能体任务完成，要么仅以整体任务成功率概括多智能体行为，从而掩盖了重复工作、违反顺序约束、资源竞争和交接失步等具体协调失败。

Method: 构建CoCoBench benchmark，包含897个oracle验证的实例，围绕四种重复出现的协调构念（任务分配、顺序排序、互斥、交接协调）组织；在任务成功率之外，提供构念级评分来衡量智能体间的协调有效性；对11个主流MLLM在不同协调模式、观测输入和智能体数量下进行评估。

Result: 对11个领先MLLM的评估表明，协调能力具有高度的构念特异性：整体表现优异的模型并不一定在各类协调构念上具备均衡的能力。

Conclusion: 该发现为设计针对性的模型架构和提升多智能体协调能力指出了新方向。

Abstract: Agent systems powered by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced rapidly in recent years, yet existing embodied-agent benchmarks still lack fine-grained diagnostics for multi-agent coordination. Most benchmarks either focus on single-agent task completion or summarize multi-agent behavior with overall task success rates, which can obscure coordination failures such as duplicated work, violations of ordering constraints, resource contention, and desynchronized handoffs. In this paper, we introduce CoCoBench, a construct-level benchmark for evaluating multi-agent embodied coordination in executable household tasks. CoCoBench contains 897 oracle-validated instances organized around four recurring coordination constructs: task allocation, sequential ordering, mutual exclusion, and handoff coordination. In addition to task success rate, CoCoBench provides construct-level scores that measure whether agents coordinate effectively. We evaluate 11 leading MLLMs across different coordination modes, observation inputs, and numbers of agents. The results show that coordination ability is highly construct-specific: strong overall performance does not imply balanced competence across different coordination types. These findings point to new directions for designing targeted model architectures and improving multi-agent coordination ability.

</details>


### [259] [Spatial-Semantic Reasoning using Large Language Models for Efficient UAV Search Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28270)
*Marin Maletic,Marijana Peti,Tamara Petrovic,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于无人机实时语义导航的框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）解析自然语言指令并进行语义推理，以优先搜索高概率区域，提升目标导航（ObjectNav）任务的时间效率。系统结合实时目标检测、3D空间映射和多项式样条插值实现平滑可行的轨迹规划，并在仿真和真实场景中验证了减少任务时长的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机目标导航方法依赖离线推理或受限于模拟器约束的动作空间，无法在真实环境中实时更新语义相关信息，导致任务时间效率低下。

Method: 该框架的核心是LLM驱动的语义推理模块，用于解释自然语言指令并结合检测到的物体和空间上下文对搜索区域进行优先级排序；同时集成了实时目标检测、3D空间映射以及多项式样条插值模块，实现平滑且可行的无人机轨迹规划，并基于新观测连续更新语义相关性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，该框架在保持高搜索精度的同时显著缩短了任务持续时间，验证了LLM引导推理对时间高效的无人机目标导航的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM引导的语义推理能够有效提升无人机目标导航的时间效率，所提出的实时框架在仿真和真实场景中均表现出优越性，为后续研究提供了可行方向。

Abstract: We present a real-time semantic navigation framework for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) focused on improving time efficiency in the Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) task. Central to our approach is a Large Language Model (LLM) that interprets user-provided natural language instructions and performs semantic reasoning over detected objects and spatial context to prioritize high-probability search regions. The system combines real-time object detection, 3D spatial mapping, and polynomial spline interpolation for smooth and feasible UAV trajectory planning. Unlike prior methods that rely on offline reasoning or simulator-constrained action spaces, our framework can operate in real time, continuously updating semantic relevance based on new observations. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings demonstrate reductions in mission duration while maintaining high search accuracy, underscoring the effectiveness of LLM-guided reasoning for time- efficient UAV-based ObjectNav.

</details>


### [260] [STEGNav: Spatio-Temporal Event Graph Reasoning for Multimodal Lifelong Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28279)
*Yang Chen,Zhenyu Huang,Wenbo Fu,Danyang Peng,Shi-Yu Tian,Kun-Yang Yu,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STEGNav，一种无训练框架，将传统语义场景图扩展为时空事件图（空间轴做查询条件实例定位与探索前沿联合表征，时间轴用轨迹感知双窗口记忆），使VLM导航智能体能区分相似实例、复用跨子任务经验，在GOAT-Bench等基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义场景图的多模态终身导航方法存在三大局限：难以区分相似实例、无法联合表征语义目标与探索前沿、未有效利用导航记忆和轨迹经验。这些问题导致智能体在复杂环境中导航可靠性和跨子任务经验复用能力不足。

Method: STEGNav构建时空事件图：空间轴上执行查询条件实例定位，联合表征语义目标和考虑可达性、路径代价、探索效用的占用感知探索前沿；时间轴上采用轨迹感知双窗口记忆，保留近期决策-轨迹事件和已验证的跨子任务导航结果。VLM导航智能体基于时空事件图推理，选择目标实例或探索前沿作为下一导航目标。

Result: STEGNav在GOAT-Bench上达到66.3% SR和39.7 SPL，在HM3Dv1和HM3Dv2上分别获得64.0%和69.4%的SR。消融实验和错误分析验证了空间与时间两条轴线的互补效应。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的时空表征能够有效提升多模态终身导航的可靠性和跨子任务经验复用能力，验证了将场景图扩展为时空事件图的思路是解决现有方法局限的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal lifelong navigation requires an agent to autonomously explore unseen environments while sequentially completing navigation tasks specified by object categories, language descriptions, or reference images. Existing methods primarily accomplish these tasks by constructing state-centric semantic scene graphs. By treating scene graphs as persistent repositories of semantic observations, these methods struggle to distinguish similar instances, jointly represent semantic targets and exploration frontiers, and effectively exploit navigation memory and trajectory experience. To address these limitations, we propose Spatio-Temporal Event Graph Navigation (STEGNav), a training-free framework that extends conventional scene graphs into spatio-temporal event graphs along complementary spatial and temporal axes. The spatial axis performs query-conditioned instance grounding and jointly represents semantic targets and occupancy-aware exploration frontiers characterized by reachability, path cost, and exploration utility. The temporal axis employs trajectory-aware dual-window memory to retain recent decision--trajectory events and verified cross-subtask navigation outcomes. A VLM-based navigation agent reasons over the resulting spatio-temporal event graph and selects either a target instance or an exploration frontier as its next navigation goal. STEGNav achieves 66.3% SR and 39.7 SPL on GOAT-Bench, as well as SR scores of 64.0% and 69.4% on HM3Dv1 and HM3Dv2, respectively. Ablation studies and error analyses validate the complementary effects of the two axes, demonstrating that event-driven spatio-temporal representations improve navigation reliability and cross-subtask experience reuse.

</details>


### [261] [MaCoPlanner: LLM-Assisted Manual-Compiled Task Planning with Proactive Safety Verification for Robotic Industrial Panel Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28300)
*Guipeng Xin,Jiahe Xua,Mohammad Deghat,Chenhui Wan,Jie Liu,Youmin Hu,Zhongxu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MaCoPlanner框架，通过将设备手册编译为类型化中间表示，检索任务与状态相关证据来生成任务规划，并在执行前对候选计划进行符号化回放和合规性检查，对违规计划进行针对性修复或拒绝。在独立评估预言机下最终违规率降至2.7%，Level-2和Level-3任务成功率分别从62.8%提升至84.4%、25.9%提升至43.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人工业面板操作不仅需要精确的控制定位，还必须遵守分散在异构手册中的操作规程、安全规则和设备状态约束，现有方法难以系统性整合这些合规要求。

Method: MaCoPlanner包含四个核心环节：(1) 将设备手册编译为类型化中间表示；(2) 检索任务相关和状态相关的证据以支持规划生成；(3) 在驱动前对候选计划进行符号化回放，对照程序性和状态转移约束进行检验，检测违规并定位后进行针对性修复，未解决的计划被拒绝；(4) 执行接口将已验证的符号动作落地为物理控制并更新设备状态。

Result: 在独立评估预言机下，MaCoPlanner达到2.7%的最终违规率；修复分析中26.3%的运行在耗尽修复预算后被拒绝。相比Raw-Manual基线，Level-2任务成功率从62.8%提升到84.4%，Level-3从25.9%提升到43.2%。在无工业负载的控制器面板模拟器上验证了集成执行可行性，但不声称工业部署就绪。

Conclusion: MaCoPlanner通过手册知识编译与符号化预检验结合的思路，有效降低了面板操作任务中的合规违规率并显著提升任务成功率，证明了在执行前进行符号化滚动检查与针对性修复的价值，但当前结果仅在模拟器层面验证，尚不具备工业部署条件。

Abstract: Robotic industrial panel operation requires not only accurate control localization but also compliance with operating procedures, safety rules, and device-state constraints distributed across heterogeneous manuals. This study presents MaCoPlanner, a task-planning framework built on knowledge compiled from equipment manuals that converts equipment manuals into a typed intermediate representation, retrieves task- and state-relevant evidence, and uses it to support plan generation. Before actuation, candidate plans are symbolically rolled out and checked against procedural and state-transition constraints; detected violations are localized and returned for targeted repair, while unresolved plans are rejected. A separate execution interface grounds verified symbolic actions to physical controls and updates the device state. Under an independent evaluation oracle, MaCoPlanner achieves a final violation rate of 2.7%, and 26.3% of the runs in the repair analysis are rejected after exhausting the refinement budget. Compared with Raw-Manual, task success increases from 62.8% to 84.4% on Level-2 tasks and from 25.9% to 43.2% on Level-3 tasks. Experiments on a controller-panel simulator without an attached industrial load further demonstrate integrated execution feasibility under representative interaction conditions, without claiming industrial deployment readiness.

</details>


### [262] [PanelShield: Verifiable Closed-Loop Safe Planning for Robotic Industrial Panel Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28305)
*Guipeng Xin,Jiahe Xu,Chenhui Wan,Jie Liu,Youmin Hu,Zhongxu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PanelShield是一个面向工业面板操作的可验证闭环安全规划框架，结合基础模型语义规划能力与LTL和安全FSM双重形式化验证，在违规时输出结构化反例支持定向修复，将违规率降至2.7%、延迟4.1秒，并在仿真和真实机器人实验中验证了端到端可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业面板操作是知识密集且安全关键的，执行需满足操作手册和安全法规等约束。基础模型规划器虽具强语义能力，但缺乏可计算、可定位、可复现的违规检测与修复机制，因此需要一种可验证的安全规划方案。

Method: 框架包含三部分：(1) 从任务相关手册证据生成参数化动作原语序列；(2) 采用LTL与安全FSM双重形式化验证，分别保证跨步骤时间正确性与局部转换合法性；(3) 违规时输出包含最早违规步骤及其原因的结构化反例，实现定向修复与重新验证。同时构建了覆盖三个代表性工业设备面板的多层级长程规划基准。

Result: 在复杂安全约束任务上优于纯基础模型规划基线，违规率降至2.7%，总延迟4.1秒；真实世界实验验证了端到端可行性。

Conclusion: PanelShield提供了一种可验证的机器人面板操作方法，在灵活性、安全性和可审计性之间取得平衡，为工业安全关键场景中的机器人操作提供了可行的安全保障方案。

Abstract: Industrial panel operation is knowledge-intensive and safety-critical. Beyond control recognition and action generation, execution must satisfy constraints in operation manuals and safety regulations. While foundation-model-based planners show strong semantic capability, they typically lack computable, localizable, and reproducible mechanisms for violation detection and repair. To address this, we propose PanelShield, a verifiable closed-loop safety planning framework for manual-guided industrial panel operation. The framework generates parameterized action primitive sequences from task-relevant manual evidence and applies dual formal verification with LTL and a Safety FSM to enforce cross-step temporal correctness and local transition legality. When violations occur, it outputs a structured counterexample with the earliest violating step and cause, enabling targeted repair and re-verification. We build a multi-level long-horizon planning benchmark covering three representative industrial device panels, and evaluate the framework in simulation and real-world robotic experiments. Results show that PanelShield improves complex safety-constrained task performance over foundation-model-only planning baselines while reducing the violation rate to 2.7%, with 4.1 s total latency. Real-world experiments demonstrate end-toend feasibility. Overall, PanelShield offers a verifiable approach to robotic panel operation that balances flexibility, safety, and auditability.

</details>


### [263] [Cooperative Risk-Aware Exploration in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems Using Algorithmic Altruism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28409)
*Brooks A. Butler,Jair Certório,João P. Hespanha,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于博弈论框架的协同风险感知探索方法，受生态学中利他行为（Hamilton规则）启发，通过相关权重编码异构代理间的利他耦合，定义社交纳什均衡来塑造效用函数，使低价值机器人主动承担风险以惠及高价值队友，在仿真和硬件实验中验证了该方法能减少冗余探索、改善机器人间距并合理重新分配风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在危险环境中进行探索时，不仅需要决定在哪里收集信息，还需要考虑如何在异构团队成员之间分配风险。现有方法缺乏对团队异质性下风险分配与利他协作的系统性建模。

Method: 构建基于Hamilton规则的利他博弈框架：每个机器人选择有限时域轨迹以最大化信息增益，同时惩罚冗余探索和期望危险暴露；通过代理特定的价值参数和相关权重建模利他耦合，定义社交纳什均衡以改变代理行为的效用，使低价值机器人内化其轨迹选择对队友的影响；采用投影梯度的重规划视野路点优化算法。

Result: 仿真表明利他规划减少了冗余探索、改善了机器人间距，并根据代理价值重新分配风险且保持地图覆盖率可比；硬件实验通过轮式机器人的单积分控制器和屏障证书验证了规划路点的跟踪可行性。

Conclusion: 基于利他博弈的效用塑造机制能够有效引导异构多机器人团队在危险环境中进行风险感知探索，在保持覆盖性能的同时实现团队成员间的风险合理再分配。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are well-positioned for exploration in hazardous environments, but effective deployment requires deciding not only where robots should gather information, but also how risk should be distributed across heterogeneous team members. This paper develops a game-theoretic framework for cooperative risk-aware exploration based on ecologically inspired altruistic behavior. Each robot selects a finite-horizon trajectory to maximize information gain while penalizing redundant exploration and expected hazard exposure. Heterogeneity is introduced through agent-specific value parameters for encoding altruistic coupling, which is modeled through relatedness weights inspired by Hamilton's rule. We introduce a game-theoretic structure for trajectory planning that defines a Social Nash Equilibrium, which modifies the utility of agent actions according to agent relatedness. This utility shaping causes agents to internalize the effect of their trajectory choices on teammates, encouraging lower-valued robots to accept risk when doing so benefits higher-valued agents and improves team performance. We define an exploration utility for agents that rewards area coverage and uncertainty reduction, while also penalizing redundancy and risk, enabling projected gradient-based waypoint optimization in a receding-horizon planner. Simulations show that altruistic planning reduces redundant exploration, improves inter-robot separation, and reallocates risk according to agent value while maintaining comparable map coverage. We further demonstrate the approach in hardware experiments, where planned waypoints are tracked by wheeled robots using single-integrator controllers and barrier certificates.

</details>


### [264] [Linear Temporal Logic Translation via Human-Inspired Self-Constrained Reasoning for Robot Task Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28435)
*Haofei Hou,Fanxu Meng,Shunyi Zhao,Kairui Yang,Mengchen Cai,Lecheng Ruan,Qining Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Constrained Reasoning (SCR)框架，通过将结构约束内化到模型的决策过程中，而非作为外部过滤器事后施加，来解决LLM将自然语言指令翻译为LTL形式化规范时的约束违反与泛化能力之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based翻译器在将模糊、不完整的自然语言翻译为LTL规范时存在两难：开放式的推理可能违反领域约束，而事后的约束强制可能破坏对新颖指令的推理能力。

Method: SCR框架结合结构约束表示与层次化决策制定，将结构知识内化到模型的决策过程中，从而在形式上约束的空间内引导推理，同时保持对未见指令的适应性。

Result: 实验表明SCR在领域约束满足和泛化能力两方面均有提升，提供了有效且可解释的方法。

Conclusion: SCR通过内化结构约束而非外部过滤的方式，有效平衡了约束满足与泛化能力，为将人类意图翻译为可验证的机器人执行规范提供了新思路。

Abstract: Many robotic tasks are temporally extended and demand precise specifications of subgoals, constraints, and their temporal ordering. Yet human operators typically communicate such tasks in natural language, which is inherently ambiguous, underspecified, and context dependent. Translating human instructions into formal task specifications, such as Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), is therefore essential for verifiable and safe robotic execution. Existing LLM-based translators attempt to bridge this gap through open-ended reasoning or post-hoc constraint enforcement, but the former may violate domain constraints, whereas the latter can disrupt the reasoning needed for novel instructions. This paper proposes Self-Constrained Reasoning (SCR), a framework that mitigates this trade-off by internalizing structural knowledge into the model's decision-making process rather than imposing it as an external filter. By combining a structural constraint representation with a hierarchical decision-making formulation, SCR guides reasoning within a formally grounded space while preserving adaptability to unseen instructions. Experiments show that SCR improves both domain-constraint satisfaction and generalization, providing an effective and interpretable approach for translating human intent into verifiable specifications for robotic execution.

</details>


### [265] [ChainSplat: A Physics-Inspired Screw-Theoretic Model for Learning Deformable Linear Object Dynamics from Multi-View RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28570)
*Seungyeon Kim,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChainSplat是一个物理启发的框架，仅从多视角RGB视频中联合学习可变形线性物体（DLOs）的3D几何、外观、运动学和动力学。它将DLO表示为通过旋转关节连接的刚性连杆开放链结构，结合高斯泼溅技术，实现高保真RGB渲染和任意状态重建，并在真实实验中达到动力学预测、3D几何重建和RGB渲染的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（如电缆、绳索、软管）的动力学和3D几何建模对机器人精确操作至关重要，但由于其高维构型空间和材料属性差异带来的多样行为，这一任务极具挑战性。现有方法依赖多阶段流水线和辅助深度输入，在动态交互下容易出错，且高维状态表示导致基于模型的控制计算开销大。

Method: ChainSplat将DLO表示为刚性连杆通过旋转关节连接的开放链结构，利用螺旋理论得到解析模型，用关节构型参数化紧凑状态表示。该公式与高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）集成，实现DLO动力学、运动学感知的3D几何和外观的联合学习，并能从任意状态进行高保真RGB渲染。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，ChainSplat在动态交互下的动力学预测、3D几何重建和RGB渲染方面均达到最先进性能。此外，它还支持实时状态和力估计，以及精确的基于模型的轨迹优化。

Conclusion: ChainSplat通过物理启发的紧凑链式表示结合高斯泼溅，仅凭多视角RGB视频即可同时恢复DLO的动力学、几何和外观，为真实世界机器人操作可变形线性物体提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Identifying the underlying dynamics and 3D geometry of deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as cables, ropes, and hoses, is essential for accurate robotic manipulation, but remains challenging due to their high-dimensional configuration spaces and diverse behaviors arising from varying material properties. Existing methods often rely on multi-stage pipelines and auxiliary depth inputs, which are prone to errors under dynamic interactions, while their high-dimensional state representations make model-based control computationally expensive. In this paper, we introduce ChainSplat, a physics-inspired framework that jointly learns the 3D geometry, appearance, kinematics, and dynamics of DLOs solely from multi-view RGB videos. ChainSplat represents a DLO as an open-chain structure of rigid links connected by revolute joints, yielding an analytic, screw-theoretic model with a compact state representation parameterized by joint configurations. By integrating this formulation with Gaussian splatting, ChainSplat jointly recovers DLO dynamics, kinematics-aware 3D geometry, and appearance, while enabling high-fidelity RGB rendering from arbitrary states. Through real-world experiments, we demonstrate that ChainSplat achieves state-of-the-art performance in dynamics predictions, 3D geometry reconstruction, and RGB rendering across dynamic interactions. ChainSplat further enables real-time state and force estimation, as well as accurate model-based trajectory optimization, highlighting its practical utility for real-world robotic manipulation of DLOs. Accompanying source code and video are available at: https://chainsplat.github.io.

</details>


### [266] [Aero Hand Open: A Simulation-Ready Tendon-Driven Hand for Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28578)
*Nan Wang,Mohit Yadav,Jonathan Wulff,Aidan Rosenbaum,Kezhou Chen,Yuvan Sharma,Xu Dong,Yiwei Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Aero Hand Open，一个仿真就绪的腱驱动拟人手，包含缆绳传动的仿真模型、识别出的致动映射（含拇指三向耦合）以及强化学习训练包，使策略可完全在仿真中训练并直接迁移到实体手，无需微调或状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动手具有拟人外形，将执行器移出关节降低了制造成本（可使用更小更便宜的电机、一个电机驱动多个关节）。然而，欠驱动传动的表示在仿真器中很困难，且同一缆绳驱动的关节无法独立控制，导致这类手比直驱手更难通过强化学习训练策略。

Method: 发布Aero Hand Open仿真就绪的腱驱动拟人手，包含三部分：(1) 复现缆绳传动本身的仿真模型；(2) 识别出的致动映射，双向连接仿真模型与电机命令，包括拇指的三向耦合；(3) 面向该手的强化学习训练包。同时发布机械设计、仿真模型、识别映射、训练环境和部署栈。

Result: 所发布的内容使策略可以完全在仿真中训练，随后直接部署到实体手上运行，无需微调且无需状态估计。

Conclusion: 通过将仿真模型、致动映射和训练环境完整开源，解决了腱驱动欠传动手在仿真-实物迁移中的核心障碍，实现零微调的策略部署。

Abstract: Tendon-driven hands are anthropomorphic, and moving the actuators off the joints is what makes a hand of this capability affordable to build. Two effects produce that saving. Routing force through a cable removes the requirement that a motor fit inside the joint it drives, so smaller and cheaper motors suffice, and one motor can drive several joints through a single cable, so fewer motors are needed. They are also harder to learn on than a direct-drive hand. The underactuated transmission that produces the saving is itself difficult to represent in a simulator, and the joints one cable drives are not independently commandable. We present Aero Hand Open, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand that is released simulation-ready. Three things ship with it. A simulation model reproduces the cable transmission itself. An identified actuation map connects that model to the motor commands in both directions, including the three-way coupling of the thumb. A reinforcement learning package trains policies for the hand. Together they let a policy be trained entirely in simulation and run on the hand with no fine-tuning and no state estimation. We release the mechanical design, the simulation model, the identified mapping, the training environment and the deployment stack.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Marginal Coverage Credit Reduces Redundant Exploration in Parallel State-Entropy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27507)
*Junhao Cao,Hongyi Xia,Jianian Wu,Xiaopeng Yi,Lixia Huang,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCC-PGPSE方法，通过leave-one-policy-out覆盖度与状态所有者特化相结合的策略信用估计，为并行策略分配非负辅助内在奖励，以减少冗余访问并促进互补覆盖，在多个基准测试中相较Entropy基线取得了正向增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGPSE虽然通过并行策略扩大状态空间覆盖，但其池化团队熵只能衡量集体探索程度，无法识别哪些策略贡献了非冗余覆盖。缺乏策略级信用归因，导致冗余探索无法被有效抑制，互补覆盖难以被激励。

Method: MCC-PGPSE将leave-one-policy-out覆盖度与state-owner特化相结合来估计策略特定信用；保留PGPSE的池化目标函数，但根据这些信用将非负辅助内在奖励重新分配，且不改变奖励总量。该重分配设计用于抑制冗余访问并促进互补覆盖。

Result: 在受控实验环境、七个公开离散状态基准以及PGPSE原始协议的Room和Maze设定中，MCC-PGPSE相较Entropy基线在归一化团队状态熵和状态支撑集上均产生了正的最终窗口增益。受控任务对比和固定基准集的公开聚合结果显著，五种子原始协议对比方向一致。消融与信用对齐控制表明增益主要来自leave-one-policy-out覆盖度，而非非均匀加权、错误信用或神经新颖性。

Conclusion: 贡献条件化的辅助奖励分配是一种可解释的有效方法，可在离散状态空间中改善并行策略间的互补覆盖，为多智能体探索中的信用归因提供了新思路。

Abstract: Policy Gradient for Parallel State Entropy maximization (PGPSE) expands state-space coverage by training independently parameterized policies in replicated copies of the same environment. However, its pooled team-entropy score measures only collective exploration and cannot identify policies that contribute non-redundant coverage. We introduce Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE), which combines leave-one-policy-out coverage with state-owner specialization to estimate policy-specific credit. MCC-PGPSE preserves PGPSE's pooled objective and redistributes non-negative auxiliary intrinsic rewards according to these credits without changing their total mass. This redistribution is designed to discourage redundant visitation and promote complementary coverage. We evaluated MCC-PGPSE in controlled environments, seven public discrete-state benchmarks, and representative Room and Maze settings from the original PGPSE protocol. Across all tested settings, MCC-PGPSE produced positive final window gains in normalized team state entropy and state support over the Entropy baseline. Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [268] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型后训练量化并非语义中立的部署优化，而是可能触发隐蔽后门攻击的漏洞。作者提出了"量化行为等价类"(QBECs)形式化框架，证明源精度阶段的认证并不能保证量化后部署配置的行为等价性，并通过三阶段对抗性微调框架在翻译和立场分析两个场景中成功验证了这种攻击的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练量化工作流存在"验证-部署"结构性缺口：在完整精度源检查点上评估模型并将量化作为下游优化步骤，由于量化在参数空间上是多对一映射，源精度的安全性认证无法保证量化部署后的行为安全性。这一缺口使得量化触发型后门攻击成为可能，威胁边缘AI部署的安全性。

Method: 首先形式化定义量化行为等价类(QBECs)，并证明QBEC成员关系不蕴含行为等价性。其次构建三阶段对抗性微调框架，将潜在恶意负载嵌入满足源精度检查的模型中，使模型在INT8或4-bit量化压缩后激活目标对抗行为。最后在多语言编码器-解码器序列到序列模型上评估战术机器翻译和政治立场分析两个操作场景，并进行跨量化器可迁移性分析。

Result: 在翻译模型中，修复后的FP16模型零好友-敌人腐化，量化后反演率达到85.02%；立场分类器在压缩后测得意识形态偏移高达ΔBias=0.33。跨量化器分析表明攻击持续性因量化方案和架构而异，而非由标称位宽单独决定。

Conclusion: 源精度审计不足以排除量化触发行为，最终部署配置必须纳入行为认证范围，以保障可信边缘AI。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [269] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAMP是一种针对Gated DeltaNet和Kimi Delta Attention语言模型中循环状态的后训练量化方法，通过结合量化误差能量和衰减持久性识别高风险通道，将大部分通道量化为INT8而保留少量高精度通道，在9.9 bits/state的精度下维持接近FP32的准确率，同时减少69.1%的循环状态存储并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
