Enforcement is then a deterministic origin check over an audited set of sensitive tool parameters, and the only reliance on a language model involves solely the trusted user request, out of the attacker's reach. Our approach admits two provable guarantees: 1) at every step of a trajectory, no value whose only origin is attacker-writable content reaches an origin-guarded parameter, and 2) no rewording of an injection changes an admission decision. We evaluate across four agent models on open-ended agent suites, ROPE holds attack success rate to 1.6--2.6\% while retaining 82--100\% of undefended clean utility, significantly exceeding state-of-the-art system-level defenses in utility while attaining comparable or better security. Further, we show that optimizing the injection against ROPE is largely ineffective, while long-horizon attacks that defeat prior system-level defenses achieve zero success rate. Our code and logs are available at https://github.com/xhOwenMa/ROPE .

</details>


### [336] [Circuit Discovery Helps Detect LLM Jailbreaking: A Mechanistic Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27504)
*Paria Mehrbod,Boris Knyazev,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky,Geraldin Nanfack*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对安全对齐的大语言模型（LLaMA-2-7B-chat-hf）进行机制可解释性分析，通过边缘归因修补和子网络探测识别出负责生成越狱响应的神经电路，消融这些电路可将攻击成功率降低高达80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型尽管经过安全对齐，仍易受越狱攻击，但以往研究多归因于安全训练不足，对模型内部如何处理对抗提示的机制理解十分有限。

Method: 采用边缘归因修补（Edge Attribution Patching）和子网络探测（Subnetwork Probing）两种机制可解释性技术，系统性地识别安全对齐LLM中负责对越狱提示生成肯定响应的计算电路。

Result: 在首个token预测阶段消融这些识别出的电路，可将攻击成功率降低高达80%；并揭示了关键注意力头和MLP通路如何介导对抗提示利用、覆盖安全约束。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对对齐LLM中对抗性漏洞的理解，为基于机制可解释性的、有针对性的可解释防御方法铺平了道路。

Abstract: Despite extensive safety alignment, large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that bypass safeguards to elicit harmful content. While prior work attributes this vulnerability to safety training limitations, the internal mechanisms by which LLMs process adversarial prompts remain poorly understood. We present a mechanistic analysis of the jailbreaking behavior in a large-scale, safety-aligned LLM, focusing on LLaMA-2-7B-chat-hf. Leveraging edge attribution patching and subnetwork probing, we systematically identify computational circuits responsible for generating affirmative responses to jailbreak prompts. Ablating these circuits during the first token prediction can reduce attack success rates by up to 80\%, demonstrating its critical role in safety bypass. Our analysis uncovers key attention heads and MLP pathways that mediate adversarial prompt exploitation, revealing how important tokens propagate through these components to override safety constraints. These findings advance the understanding of adversarial vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs and pave the way for targeted, interpretable defense mechanisms based on mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [337] [eBPF-Based Cybersecurity Mechanisms: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27511)
*Stamatios Kostopoulos,Panagiotis Tsakonas,Evangelos K. Markakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文采用PRISMA系统综述方法，从6个数据库筛选3,735篇文献最终纳入54篇（2018-2026年），对基于eBPF的网络安全机制进行系统分类与综合分析，构建了涵盖DDoS缓解、入侵检测、IoT安全、容器安全、微服务保护、网络和安全工具七个领域的分类体系，并识别出多租户隔离、对抗ML鲁棒性、生产验证和标准化评估框架等关键研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eBPF作为内核级网络安全机制具有巨大潜力，但现有研究在不同领域、评估方法和部署场景上高度碎片化，缺乏统一的系统综述来整合分散的知识并识别研究空白。

Method: 采用PRISMA（系统综述和元分析优先报告条目）方法论，对六个数据库进行结构化文献筛选，从3,735条记录中筛选出54篇主要研究，通过七领域分类体系（DDoS缓解、入侵检测、IoT安全、容器安全、微服务保护、网络、安全工具）对文献进行分类和综合分析。

Result: eBPF实现了低开销安全执行（CPU开销中位数2.4%，范围1.1-8.6%，从毫秒级低频钩子到内核热路径10-20%），检测准确率94-99%。主要挑战包括：验证器约束限制算法复杂度、85.1%的研究需要低级编程专业知识、内核版本碎片化阻碍可移植性、96.2%的研究未解决eBPF自身漏洞。识别出多租户隔离、对抗ML鲁棒性、生产验证和标准化评估框架四大研究空白。

Conclusion: 本文通过整合碎片化知识并揭示安全性与表达力之间的架构权衡，为下一代eBPF安全系统奠定基础，并为内核可编程性研究提供可操作方向。

Abstract: Extended Berkeley Packet Filter (eBPF) has emerged as a kernel-level framework enabling dynamic security enforcement in modern operating systems. While eBPF's cybersecurity potential has attracted significant attention, existing work remains fragmented across domains, evaluation methodologies, and deployment contexts. This systematic literature review applies PRISMA methodology to identify, categorize, and synthesize peer-reviewed research on eBPF-based cybersecurity mechanisms. Following a structured screening of 3,735 records from six databases, 54 primary studies (2018-2026) were analyzed and organized into a seven-domain taxonomy: DDoS mitigation, intrusion detection, IoT security, container security, microservice protection, networking, and security tools. Analysis reveals eBPF enables low-overhead security enforcement (median 2.4% CPU overhead [1.1-8.6%], ranging from nanosecond-scale costs for infrequent hooks to 10-20% for kernel hot paths) with high detection accuracy (94-99%). It particularly excels in kernel-level monitoring, real-time packet processing, and cloud-native workload protection. However, significant challenges persist: verifier-imposed constraints limit algorithm complexity, 85.1% (46/54) of studies require low-level programming expertise, kernel version fragmentation hinders portability, and 96.2% (52/54) of research fails to address eBPF's own vulnerabilities. This review identifies critical research gaps in multi-tenant isolation, adversarial machine learning (ML) robustness, production validation, and standardized evaluation frameworks. By consolidating fragmented knowledge and highlighting architectural trade-offs between safety and expressiveness, this work provides a foundation for next-generation eBPF security systems and actionable directions for kernel programmability research.

</details>


### [338] [Fully Unleashing the Multimodal Attacker: Meta-Adaptive Jailbreaking of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27531)
*Benlei Cui,Shen Pang,Yuke Wang,Xuemei Dong,Yuwen Zhai,Jingqun Tang,Haiyang Yu,Hui Xue,Longtao Huang,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Meta-Adaptive Multimodal Jailbreaking (MAMJ)，一种在元层面优化攻击者自身的多模态越狱方法，通过双层优化——LLM-based critique精炼攻击策略提示θ和基于群体聚合ASR奖励更新攻击者权重φ——在MM-SafetyBench上对GPT-4o、Gemini-3-Pro-Preview和Seed 2.0分别达到81.0%、78.9%和82.3%的ASR，超越最强样本级基线最多24.1个百分点，且学习到的攻击者可无需再训练地迁移到未见过的受害模型并抵抗代表性防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视觉语言模型的多模态越狱攻击在元层面是静态的：基于模板的攻击冻结了图像-文本的布局，而迭代式攻击虽然只适应图像-文本内容，但攻击策略和攻击者参数是固定的。作者希望突破这种静态性，实现攻击者自身的元层面自适应优化。

Method: MAMJ沿两个轴优化攻击者本身：(1) 攻击策略提示(ASP) θ，控制攻击迭代过程，由LLM-based critique在攻击轨迹组上精炼；(2) 攻击者权重φ，决定攻击有效性，通过跨攻击轨迹组的群体聚合攻击成功率(ASR)奖励进行更新。两者形成双层优化闭环。

Result: 在MM-SafetyBench上，MAMJ对GPT-4o、Gemini-3-Pro-Preview和Seed 2.0分别达到81.0%、78.9%和82.3%的ASR，超越最强样本级基线最多24.1个百分点。学习到的攻击者(θ*, φ*)可无需重新训练迁移至未见过的受害模型，且在代表性防御下仍然有效。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了前沿视觉语言模型对元自适应越狱攻击存在系统性漏洞，这一发现激励防御方需要针对元层面攻击者进行对抗防御设计。

Abstract: The safety of large vision-language models is increasingly stress-tested by multimodal jailbreaks, yet existing attacks remain largely static at the meta level: template-based attacks freeze the image--text layout, while iterative attacks adapt only the image--text content with fixed attack strategies and frozen attacker parameters. We propose Meta-Adaptive Multimodal Jailbreaking (MAMJ), which instead optimizes the attacker itself along two axes: an attack strategy prompt (ASP) $θ$ governing attack iteration and attacker weights $φ$ determining attack effectiveness. Across groups of multimodal attack trajectories, an LLM-based critique first refines $θ$, after which group-aggregated attack-success-rate (ASR) rewards update $φ$. On MM-SafetyBench, MAMJ achieves $81.0\%$, $78.9\%$, and $82.3\%$ ASR against GPT-4o, Gemini-3-Pro-Preview, and Seed 2.0, respectively, outperforming the strongest sample-level baseline by up to $24.1$ percentage points. The learned attacker $(θ^\star,φ^\star)$ also transfers without retraining to unseen victims and remains effective under representative defenses. These results reveal a systemic vulnerability of frontier VLMs to meta-adaptive jailbreaks and motivate defenses against meta-level adversaries. Code is available at https://github.com/Alibaba-VELLDEPTH/MetaJailbreak-VLM.

</details>


### [339] [FISGuard: Defending Against Membership Inference via Fixed Input Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27836)
*Haocheng Jiang,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FISGuard，一种轻量级防御方法，通过使用独立公开数据构造并固定低维表示子空间，限制联邦学习微调（Adapter和LoRA）中通过梯度暴露的私有表示信息，从而有效防御ProjRes成员推断攻击（将攻击AUC降至接近0.5的随机猜测水平），同时保持模型下游任务性能接近未防御水平，实现了良好的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型联邦微调场景中，虽然客户端只共享梯度而不直接上传原始数据，但梯度仍可能泄露训练样本的成员信息。ProjRes攻击（S&P 2026）进一步加剧了这一风险——攻击者仅基于梯度诱导子空间的投影残差即可区分成员与非成员，且无需访问模型输出。现有防御方法（梯度扰动或正则化）不仅损害模型效用，而且无法有效防御利用梯度几何结构的ProjRes攻击。

Method: FISGuard的核心思想是：利用独立公开数据构造并固定一个低维表示子空间，从而限制私有表示通过梯度暴露的空间范围，同时保留下游任务所需的主要信息。通过这种方式，显著缩小成员与非成员之间的投影残差差异，从根本上削弱ProjRes攻击所依赖的几何信号。

Result: 在三个NLP数据集、两个LLM以及两种微调策略（Adapter和LoRA）上，FISGuard与五种代表性防御方法进行了对比评估。结果显示，FISGuard在大多数设置下将ProjRes攻击的AUC降至接近随机猜测水平（0.5），同时保持下游任务性能接近未防御模型，且仅引入有限的计算开销，实现了良好的隐私-效用平衡。

Conclusion: FISGuard是应对联邦LLM微调中ProjRes成员推断攻击的有效轻量级防御方案。通过构造固定低维表示子空间的思想，它能够在不显著牺牲模型效用和计算效率的前提下，将成员推断攻击成功率降至随机水平，为联邦学习中的隐私保护提供了新的防御思路。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly adopted in federated learning, protecting user privacy while performing parameter-efficient fine-tuning on distributed private data has become an important challenge. Although clients only share gradients instead of directly uploading raw data, the shared gradients may still leak membership information about training samples. ProjRes (S&P, 2026) further increases this risk: with less information and without accessing model outputs, an attacker can effectively distinguish members from non-members solely based on the projection residual between a candidate representation and the subspace induced by server-observable gradients. Existing defenses against membership inference mostly rely on gradient perturbation or regularization, which can not only degrade model utility but also fail to effectively defend against the membership inference attack introduced by ProjRes, which exploits the geometric structure of gradients.
  To address this issue, we propose FISGuard, a lightweight defense. Its key idea is to construct and fix a low-dimensional representation subspace using independent public data, thereby restricting the space through which private representations are exposed via gradients while preserving the primary information required for downstream tasks. This substantially reduces the projection-residual discrepancy between members and non-members.
  We evaluate FISGuard against five representative defense methods across three NLP datasets, two LLMs, and two fine-tuning strategies, Adapter and LoRA. The results show that FISGuard reduces the ProjRes attack AUC to near the random-guessing level of 0.5 in most settings, while maintaining downstream task performance close to that of the undefended model and introducing only limited computational overhead, thereby achieving a favorable privacy--utility trade-off.

</details>


### [340] [FlyBlind: Cross-Slice Timeliness Attacks on UAV Situational Awareness over 5G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27604)
*Wagner Comin Sonaglio,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,André Elias Melo,Guevara Noubir,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FlyBlind攻击，揭示在5G SA网络的BVLOS无人机系统中，共享用户面的软隔离机制下，合法共租户可通过维持上行需求导致GCS状态老化（Silent State Staleness），而传统链路监控无法检测到该"假健康"状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BVLOS无人机系统依赖5G SA网络的共享用户面传输C2数据和视频回传，运营商通过延迟和可用性指标评估链路质量，依赖网络切片间的软隔离。然而，授权的共租户可能导致GCS状态过时而不中断连接的风险尚未被充分研究，传统监控手段存在盲区。

Method: 作者提出FlyBlind时序攻击，利用软隔离下空闲资源共享的特性，让相邻切片的授权共租户维持合法上行需求，将授权竞争转化为GCS状态老化。作者将这种效应形式化为Silent State Staleness（静默状态陈旧性），定义为可证伪的"假健康"谓词，并在专用测试平台（testbed）上进行验证。

Result: 在专用测试平台上，GCS处遥测数据年龄饱和在约12秒，GCS位置估计与地面真实位置误差达数十米，而单向延迟（OWD）p99保持在几十毫秒，可用性超过99.9%，飞行器在GUIDED模式下持续运行且未触发失效保护机制。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在具有非对称上行链路强制策略的部署中，验证目的地（GCS）的状态新鲜度至关重要，而不能仅依赖链路健康指标。

Abstract: Beyond Visual Line of Sight (BVLOS) Uncrewed Aerial Systems (UAS) operating over 5G Standalone (SA) networks use a shared User Plane for both command-and-control (C2) data and video feedback. Operators assess link quality through latency and availability, relying on soft isolation between network slices. However, the risk that an authorized co-tenant could make the Ground Control Station (GCS) state outdated without disrupting the connection remains underexplored. This work introduces FlyBlind, a timeliness attack in which an authorized co-tenant on a neighboring slice maintains legitimate uplink demand, causing state aging at the GCS without a rogue gNB or direct interference with C2 traffic. Our key insight is that, under soft isolation, sharing idle resources turns authorized competition for grants into state aging that conventional link monitors fail to detect. We formalize this effect, termed Silent State Staleness, as a falsifiable false-healthy predicate. On a dedicated testbed, telemetry age at the GCS saturates at approximately 12 seconds, with discrepancies of tens of meters between the GCS position estimate and ground truth, while the one-way delay (OWD) p99 remains in the tens of milliseconds, availability exceeds 99.9%, and the vehicle keeps operating in GUIDED mode without triggering failsafe mechanisms. These findings indicate that, in deployments with asymmetric uplink enforcement, verifying state freshness at the destination is essential rather than relying solely on link health.

</details>


### [341] [Semantic Watermarking with Order-Robust Detection over Sub-sentence Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27666)
*Abdulrahman Diaa,Jonathan Petit,Florian Kerschbaum*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EDA（自适应嵌入位移攻击），利用公开改写器和替代编码器，通过对内容保持编辑（改写、重排序、重新分段）最大化嵌入位移来移除语义水印。在5%假阳性率和90%内容保留阈值下，EDA能在32.6%-47.9%的文档上成功移除四种水印方案中的标记。为抵御此类攻击，作者设计了k-SwordStamp，一种基于子句单元的阶鲁棒性检测语义水印方案，能将最强无盒攻击成功率降至10.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义水印将标记绑定到句子语义而非令牌选择，以应对内容保持编辑。然而，攻击者提供的文本可能被改写、重排序或重分段，这些编辑均会导致嵌入位移，使检测器检测到与水印时不同的嵌入，从而丢失标记。现有被动改写测试未充分考虑这些威胁，需要更全面的鲁棒性评估方法。

Method: 本文提出两种方案：（1）EDA（自适应嵌入位移攻击）：将改写、重排序、重分段统一为单一目标——最大化嵌入位移，使用公开改写器和替代编码器，无需访问提供商生成器或密钥；（2）k-SwordStamp：基于子句单元的语义水印，采用阶鲁棒性检测，降低对攻击者选择结构的敏感性，以轻微质量代价换取鲁棒性。

Result: 在5% FPR和90%内容保留阈值下，EDA在四种水印方案上成功移除标记的成功率为32.6%-47.9%，优于其他测试攻击。针对k-SwordStamp，最强无盒攻击（EDA变体）成功率仅10.8%；但若攻击者可访问检测器和密钥，成功率可达39.7%，显著低于k-SemStamp上的65.5%。

Conclusion: EDA比被动改写能更全面地评估语义水印方案的鲁棒性，揭示其在结构编辑下的脆弱性。k-SwordStamp通过子句级阶鲁棒检测有效提升了抵抗能力，虽然在有盒攻击下仍有改进空间，但已显著优于现有方案。

Abstract: Semantic watermarks tie the mark to sentence meaning rather than token choices, promising robustness to content-preserving edits. However, the detector only observes attacker-supplied text, which can be reworded, reordered, or resegmented to evade detection without content loss. Rewording, reordering, and resegmentation all cause embedding displacement: detection tests embeddings different from those selected during watermarking and can therefore lose the mark. Our adaptive embedding displacement attack (EDA) admits all three edits under a single objective that maximizes this displacement. It uses a public paraphraser and surrogate encoder without access to the provider's generator or secret key. At a 5% false-positive rate (FPR) and content-preservation threshold $\bar{q}=90\%$, EDA successfully removes the mark on between 32.6% and 47.9% of documents across four schemes, the highest among the tested attacks. Therefore, EDA evaluates the schemes' robustness more thoroughly than passive paraphrasing.
  To address these vulnerabilities, we design (k)-SwordStamp: semantic watermarks with order-robust detection over sub-sentence units, reducing sensitivity to attacker-chosen structure at a small quality cost. Against k-SwordStamp, the strongest no-box attack we test is an EDA variant adapted to its design, with a 10.8% attack-success rate. A stronger EDA with access to the provider's detector and secret key reaches a 39.7% attack-success rate, compared with 65.5% on k-SemStamp. Our code is available at https://github.com/D-Diaa/SwordStamp.

</details>


### [342] [Revisiting Continuous Noise Sampling for Multi-Party Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27766)
*Yucheng Fu,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现了MPC+DP中广泛使用的连续噪声采样方案（sample-and-scale）存在安全漏洞：缩放操作将噪声限制在稀疏的公开值集合中，攻击者可借此推断数据集来源。作者提出了基于离散偏置位采样的改进方案，在安全性和效率上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC与DP结合使得多方可以在无信任中心的情况下发布聚合统计量，其核心原语是在有限精度算术下从连续分布中采样噪声。然而现有连续噪声采样协议存在安全漏洞，直接修复要么大幅损失效用，要么增加精度位导致采样开销过高，因此需要同时解决安全性和效率问题。

Method: 作者首先识别出sample-and-scale构造中缩放操作的安全漏洞，证明缩放将噪声限制在稀疏的公开值集合，使攻击者可观察发布的噪声查询以判断数据集来源，并在Orchard和DP-BREM+两个系统上实现了近100%攻击成功率的攻击演示。然后转向比特粒度的离散偏置位采样，对采样器进行了多项优化并证明了其安全性。

Result: 在Orchard和DP-BREM+两个系统上实现了近100%的攻击成功率（任何实际使用的噪声缩放器s≥2）。改进后的离散采样器相比现有安全离散采样器实现了4倍到612倍的加速，相比不安全的sample-and-scale范式实现数量级的加速，且与理想连续机制的效用损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: sample-and-scale连续噪声采样方案存在内在的缩放漏洞，不可安全使用；基于偏置位粒度离散采样方案可以同时实现安全性和高效率，是替代连续噪声采样的有效方案。

Abstract: Combining secure multi-party computation (MPC) with differential privacy (DP) enables multiple parties to release aggregate statistics without a trusted curator, and the core primitive is the protocol to sample noise from a continuous distribution under finite-precision arithmetic. In this paper, we revisit the continuous noise sampling protocols and present several improvements in both security and efficiency.
  We start by identifying a vulnerability in widely used sample-and-scale constructions. We demonstrate that the scaling operation in arithmetic circuits confines the noise to a sparse, publicly known set of values, so that an adversary can observe the released noisy queries and decide which dataset produced them. As concrete demonstrations, we instantiate attacks on two systems employing such ``flawed'' sampling protocols: Orchard (OSDI'20) for DP secure aggregation and DP-BREM$^+$ (USENIX Sec'25) for DP federated learning. We report a near-$100\%$ attack success rate on both systems, under any noise scaler $s\geq 2$ used in practice.
  The leakage we reveal is intrinsic to the scaling operation, and direct repairs either substantially sacrifice utility or add significant precision bits to make the sampling more expensive. To address the security and efficiency issues together, we turn to discrete sampling at the granularity of individual biased bits. We make several optimizations to the sampler and prove its security. Our implementation achieves $4\times \sim 612\times$ speedup over existing secure discrete samplers and orders-of-magnitude speedup over the insecure sample-and-scale paradigm, with negligible utility loss compared to the ideal continuous mechanism.

</details>


### [343] [Relaxed Sender Anonymity for CBDC Interbank Settlement: A Zero-Knowledge Approach on Permissioned EVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28529)
*Pietro Tiberi,Gabriele Marcelli,Vitangelo Lasorella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种面向许可型以太坊兼容网络的机密银行间结算协议，通过放松发送者匿名模型（发送方公开可识别，接收方、金额及业务载荷加密隐藏）兼顾区块链透明性与监管合规要求，基于Hyperledger Besu和QBFT共识实现，支持shield/confidential transfer/unshield三种状态转换，实验表明端到端结算8-16秒，证明验证约1ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBDC银行间结算系统基于DLT面临根本性权衡：区块链透明性支持无需信任的验证，但会向所有网络参与者暴露商业敏感的双方交易流水。现有方案无法同时满足监管AML/CFT要求与商业隐私保护，需要一种兼顾监管可审计性与交易机密性的协议。

Method: 采用放松的发送者匿名模型：发起机构在链上保持公开以保障问责与合规，接收机构、转账金额和业务载荷通过密码学手段混淆。技术栈包括：Groth16零知识证明（BN254曲线）、Poseidon哈希承诺（增量Merkle树）、多接收方ECIES载荷加密、链上NoteRegistry合约（仅追加式加密笔记账本日志），消除可信链下托管服务器。支持shield、confidential transfer、unshield三种状态转换。

Result: 在五节点网络（三家商业银行、一家央行运营商、一家证券存管机构）上，端到端结算耗时8-16秒；通过EVM预编译合约，证明验证开销约1ms（约220k gas）；客户端在商用ARM硬件上生成证明需4-12秒。协议层面建立了接收方机密性，但当前PoC的NoteRegistry使用所有者索引事件，可通过统一事件广播在生产环境中解决该权衡。

Conclusion: 该协议在许可型Ethereum网络的DLT结算环境中有效平衡了隐私保护与AML/CFT监管合规，性能开销可接受，但接收方隐私的完备性依赖于生产部署中对事件广播机制的进一步改进。

Abstract: Central Bank Digital Currency (CBDC) interbank settlement systems operating on Distributed Ledger Technology (DLT) face a fundamental trade-off: blockchain transparency enables trustless verification but exposes commercially sensitive bilateral transaction flows to all network participants. We propose a confidential interbank settlement protocol for permissioned Ethereum-compatible networks that resolves this tension through a relaxed sender anonymity model tailored to regulatory AML/CFT requirements. In this model, the initiating institution remains publicly identifiable on-chain for accountability and compliance, while the receiving institution, transfer amount, and business payload are cryptographically obfuscated. We realize the protocol on Hyperledger Besu using QBFT consensus, combining Groth16 zero-knowledge proofs over BN254, Poseidon hash commitments in an incremental Merkle tree, multi-recipient ECIES payload encryption, and an on-chain NoteRegistry contract that stores encrypted notes as an append-only ledger log, eliminating trusted off-chain custody servers. The protocol supports shield, confidential transfer, and unshield state transitions. Experimental evaluation across a five-node network (three commercial banks, a central bank operator, and a securities depository) demonstrates end-to-end settlement in 8-16 s, proof verification overhead of about 1 ms (around 220k gas) via EVM precompiles, and client proof generation in 4-12 s on commodity ARM hardware. While receiver confidentiality is established at the protocol level, the current proof-of-concept NoteRegistry uses owner-indexed events, a trade-off addressable in production via uniform event broadcasting.

</details>


### [344] [Memorization Is Not Extraction: Tight Differential-Privacy Bounds and Audit Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27782)
*Xujun Che,Depeng Xu,Shuhan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次精确定义了大语言模型中两种关键记忆度量（反事实记忆与自适应提取）在差分隐私下的精确常数，证明二者互不控制，并揭示基于损失的审计和遗忘验证存在双侧盲区，该盲区在十亿参数模型上依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的记忆化定义繁多且形式关系未知，差分隐私（DP）被笼统地当作所有记忆定义的统一代理，但缺乏对两种实际最关键的度量——反事实记忆（counterfactual memorization）和自适应提取（adaptive extraction）——之间的精确关系及各自由度下DP常数大小的严格刻画。

Method: 在f-DP框架下推导自适应提取协议成功的精确上界（列表预算m时成功概率至多1-f(κ)，常数κ为无意识基线），证明界在稠密基线上紧；用最小熵H_∞给出独立于先验分布的分布无关界限；对反事实记忆，证明f-DP将任何有界分数限制在优势泛函η(f)内，纯DP下等于tanh(ε/2)，并推导出k≥2份重复副本时的精确常数（由几何噪声计数达到的闭式阶梯函数，揭示朴素ε↦kε界限不可达）；通过构造具体机制展示两种度量在此类上的分离，并在十亿参数模型上进行实证验证。

Result: DP对提取和记忆的控制是精确且分层的：纯ε-DP下提取的成功概率由基线可猜测性的阈值决定，最小熵条件H_∞≥εlog2 e+log2(m/τ)可独立于先验确保提取风险低于τ；反事实记忆的最优上界在纯DP下为tanh(ε/2)，多副本情形下的精确常数为阶梯函数而非朴素线性叠加；两种度量可以完全分离——存在被记忆但不可提取的机制，也存在完全可提取但对所有基于损失的分数都不可见的机制。

Conclusion: DP并非对所有记忆定义统一的控制工具，而是对反事实记忆和自适应提取各自给出精确但有区别的约束，且两者互不蕴含。这意味着基于损失的记忆审计和遗忘验证具有系统性的双侧盲区，即使是在十亿参数规模的真实模型上，恶意释放也可绕过常规审计。

Abstract: Memorization in large language models is measured through a zoo of definitions whose formal relations are unknown, and differential privacy (DP) is treated as a proxy against all of them at once. We pin down the exact DP constant for the two that carry the practical weight, counterfactual memorization and adaptive extraction, and show that they do not control each other. Under $f$-DP, every adaptive extraction protocol with list budget $m$ succeeds with probability at most $1-f(κ)$ for the oblivious baseline $κ$, and the bound is tight on a dense set of baselines: DP uniformly controls extraction exactly up to a threshold in how well the secret can be guessed a priori. Min-entropy certifies that baseline distribution-free, since $H_\infty\geε\log_2 e+\log_2(m/τ)$ holds extraction below a risk level $τ\le1/2$ under pure $ε$-DP for every prior, and is exact on uniform priors. On the memorization side, $f$-DP caps the counterfactual memorization of any bounded score at an advantage functional $η(f)$, equal to $\tanh(ε/2)$ under pure DP; for $k\ge2$ duplicated copies the naive $ε\mapsto kε$ bound $\tanh(kε/2)$ is unattainable, the exact constant being a closed-form staircase attained by geometric noisy counting. That cap is attained inside the local score class used in practice, and it is there that the two measures separate: one mechanism is memorized yet unextractable, another fully extractable yet exactly invisible to every loss-based score. The two-sided blind spot this opens for loss-based auditing and unlearning verification survives on billion-parameter models: a reserved-trigger release is recovered verbatim from one prompt while the audits practitioners deploy certify it clean.

</details>


### [345] [ContextLeak: Exfiltrating LLM Agent Context via Malicious Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27800)
*Yuqi Jia,Ruiqi Wang,Patrick Li,Yuepeng Hu,Peinian Li,Neil Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextLeak，一种针对LLM智能体的恶意工具攻击方法，通过强化学习精调攻击LLM来生成工具名称和描述，诱导智能体将运行上下文作为参数泄露给恶意工具，从而同时满足上下文窃取的三个条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的运行上下文（用户提示、执行轨迹、工具列表）泄露会造成严重的安全和隐私风险。现有恶意工具攻击主要关注条件(1)（智能体选择恶意工具）和条件(3)（工具实现传输输入），而对条件(2)（智能体将上下文作为参数传给工具）缺乏研究，而该条件对于成功窃取上下文至关重要。

Method: ContextLeak通过精心构造恶意工具的名称和描述来诱导智能体同时选择该工具并将其上下文作为输入参数泄露。具体方法：(1) 使用攻击LLM自动生成恶意工具的名称和描述；(2) 通过强化学习在具有多样化模拟智能体上下文的shadow用户集上精调攻击LLM；(3) 设计针对上下文窃取目标定制的奖励函数，实现基于强化学习的有效精调。

Result: 大量评估表明，即使shadow用户上下文与受害者用户上下文差异很大，ContextLeak仍然高度有效。同时，ContextLeak在被适配到该设置时显著优于现有的恶意工具攻击方法。

Conclusion: ContextLeak填补了恶意工具攻击中条件(2)（上下文泄露）未被充分探索的研究空白，并通过强化学习驱动的攻击LLM精调实现了高效的上下文窃取，展示了此类攻击的严重威胁和防御的必要性。

Abstract: Exfiltrating an LLM agent's runtime context -- such as the user prompt, execution trajectory, and tool list -- poses severe security and privacy risks to users. Such attacks can be carried out via malicious tools and typically require three conditions: (1) the agent selects the malicious tool for task execution, (2) the agent passes its runtime context as input arguments to the tool, and (3) the tool's implementation transmits these inputs to an attacker-controlled endpoint. Existing work primarily focuses on conditions (1) and (3), leaving condition (2) largely unexplored, despite its critical role in enabling successful context exfiltration.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by developing ContextLeak, a malicious tool attack that induces the agent to both select the tool and disclose its context as input arguments. We realize this attack by carefully crafting the tool's name and description using reinforcement learning. Specifically, ContextLeak employs an LLM, referred to as the attack LLM, to automatically generate the malicious tool's name and description. To improve attack effectiveness, we fine-tune the attack LLM via reinforcement learning on a set of shadow users with diverse, simulated agent contexts. Our key technical contribution is the design of novel reward functions tailored to the context exfiltration objective, enabling effective reinforcement-learning-based fine-tuning of the attack LLM. Extensive evaluation demonstrates that our attack remains highly effective even when the shadow users' contexts differ substantially from those of the victim users. Moreover, ContextLeak significantly outperforms existing malicious tool attacks when adapted to this setting.

</details>


### [346] [A User-Centric Context-Aware Permission Governance Framework for Privacy Control in Default Mobile Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27914)
*Asmau Yetunde Adeniran,Adeniran Kolade Ademuwagun,Fatimah Adamu-Fika,Samaila Musa Abdullahi,Freeman Bitrus,Fortune Daberechi Ifeanyi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向默认移动应用的、基于上下文的敏感权限治理框架，引入"需要时允许"的基于功能的授权选项，并通过加权评分系统评估隐私影响，利用Web模拟平台进行早期评估，初步证明该框架能改善用户理解和决策清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 默认/预装应用深度集成于系统、具有较高权限且难以被用户审查，现有权限模型通常对应用会话授予持久或临时访问权限，无法区分具体功能，导致用户不清楚数据在何时出于何种原因被访问。

Method: 提出面向默认应用的上下文敏感、以用户为中心的权限治理框架，包含：(1) 基于功能的"Allow When Needed"授权选项，将访问限制在需要数据的具体功能而非整个应用会话；(2) 基于权限敏感度和授权类型的加权评分系统，估算用户选择的隐私影响；(3) 开发Web模拟平台，建模30种真实权限请求场景覆盖6类常用默认应用；(4) 采用横断面调查（104名受访者）与形成性可用性测试（8名参与者）相结合的探索性评估。

Result: 调查结果显示用户不会持续检查默认应用权限，且偏好授予访问权前获得上下文解释。研究结果为上下文感知的权限治理能够改善用户理解和决策清晰度提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 该基于模拟的研究是评估功能级授权与隐私反馈机制的原生部署前初始步骤，为后续在真实移动操作系统中的实现提供了基础。

Abstract: Mobile operating systems provide runtime permission controls intended to improve user control over sensitive data. However, default or pre-installed applications are deeply integrated into the system, may operate with elevated privileges, and are difficult for users to scrutinize. Existing permission models generally grant persistent or temporary access for an application session without distinguishing among individual features, leaving users uncertain about when and why data are accessed. This paper presents a context-sensitive, user-focused permission governance framework for default mobile applications. It introduces a feature-based authorization option, "Allow When Needed," that restricts access to the functionality requiring the data rather than the entire application session. A weighted scoring system estimates the privacy implications of user choices based on permission sensitivity and authorization type. A web-based simulation platform was developed to model 30 realistic permission-request situations across six commonly used default application types and support controlled early-stage evaluation before native implementation. The exploratory assessment combined a cross-sectional survey of 104 respondents examining permission awareness and behavior with formative usability testing involving eight participants interacting with the prototype. Survey findings indicate that users do not consistently examine default-application permissions and prefer contextual explanations before granting access. The results provide preliminary evidence that context-aware permission governance can improve user understanding and decision clarity. This simulation-based study represents an initial step toward evaluating feature-level authorization and privacy-feedback mechanisms before native mobile deployment.

</details>


### [347] [GraftyVul: Synthesising Insecure Programs Through Real-World Vulnerability Grafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27928)
*Omri Ram,Mitchell Horner,Ron Van der Meyden,Alsharif Abuadbba,Hammond Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GraftyVul系统，通过将真实世界漏洞移植到开源项目中，生成具有多样性、可复现性和真实性的漏洞数据集。该系统生成了212个经过验证的可利用漏洞程序，涵盖5种编程语言和23个CWE类别，并通过跨语言语义嵌入证明了其保真度，案例研究展示了其在工业漏洞修复系统中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据集在多样性（语言或漏洞类型的覆盖）、可复现性/可执行性、以及真实性三个属性上至少牺牲其一，难以同时满足安全研究（如漏洞检测、自动修复和安全代码生成）的需求。因此需要一种能够同时保证这三方面质量的漏洞数据集构建方法。

Method: GraftyVul通过将真实世界漏洞移植（grafting）到开源项目中构建漏洞程序，利用已知良好的构建和测试环境，并通过利用验证脚本确保引入的漏洞确实改变了程序行为。此外，作者提出一种语言和上下文无关的语义嵌入方法，从漏洞点（sink）、机制和宿主特征而非表面代码比较漏洞。

Result: GraftyVul生成了212个经过验证和可利用的漏洞程序，覆盖5种编程语言（Python、TypeScript、Java、Go、C#）和23个CWE类别。提出的语义嵌入在跨语言克隆和CWE分类任务上优于标准代码嵌入，且GraftyVul样本保留了与源漏洞的强语义特征。与13个广泛使用的数据集相比，GraftyVul在多样性上具有竞争力，同时是唯一具有广泛语言和CWE覆盖的可复现利用数据集。

Conclusion: GraftyVul成功解决了漏洞数据集在多样性、可复现性和真实性之间的权衡问题，通过工业案例研究验证了其在实际漏洞修复系统中的实用性，为安全研究提供了高质量的漏洞数据资源。

Abstract: Vulnerability datasets underpin a wide range of security research, including vulnerability detection, automated remediation, and secure code generation. However, existing datasets sacrifice at least one of three desirable properties: diversity (of language or vulnerability type), reproducibility/executability, or realism. We therefore present GraftyVul, a system that constructs vulnerable programs by grafting real-world vulnerabilities into open-source projects. This grounds the dataset in vulnerabilities observed in real-world contexts while harnessing known good build and test environments, enabling exploit-verification scripts to guarantee that an introduced vulnerability successfully alters a program's behaviour. Using GraftyVul, we generate 212 verified and exploitable vulnerable programs spanning five programming languages (Python, TypeScript, Java, Go, and C#) across 23 CWE categories. To evaluate fidelity, we introduce a language- and context-agnostic semantic embedding that compares vulnerabilities by sink, mechanism and host-feature rather than surface code. This approach outperforms standard code embeddings on cross-language clone and CWE classification. These embeddings demonstrate that GraftyVul samples retain a strong semantic signature to their source vulnerability. We additionally compare GraftyVul against 13 widely used datasets, where it attains competitive diversity while being the only reproducible-exploit dataset with broad language and CWE coverage. Finally, we illustrate GraftyVul's practical utility through an industrial case study evaluating a production vulnerability remediation system.

</details>


### [348] [Not to Break, but to Attest: Adversarial Probes for Privacy-Preserving LLM Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27954)
*Cameron Wilding,Mina Shaker,Fatemeh Ganji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于zk-SNARK的隐私保护审计框架，用于检测大语言模型（LLM）部署后可能发生的隐秘修改（logit漂移）。框架支持三种探测系列（token级、embedding级、压力级），在不通访问模型下平衡灵敏度与部署成本。实验表明黑盒的token级探测灵敏度最强，且Groth16证明系统在探测规模扩展时保持实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在部署后的修改可能改变行为但常规输出变化有限，当模型权重为专有属性时，AI治理面临审计挑战。需要一个既能检测模型篡改、又不暴露专有权重的隐私保护审计方案。

Method: 提出基于zk-SNARK的审计框架：设计类似对抗样本的探测（probes）来放大已批准模型与篡改部署之间的logit差异。框架包含三种互补探测系列——token级探测（黑盒，仅需输入接口、分词器和词表）、embedding级探测（灰盒，需嵌入接口）、压力探测（需额外接口能力但无需白盒权重访问）。使用Groth16 zk-SNARK实现隐私保护的证明生成与验证。

Result: 跨多种LLM架构、篡改场景和GPU平台的实验表明：token级探测虽然运行在黑盒设置中，但始终提供最强的平均灵敏度。Groth16 zk-SNARK工作流在探测集从1扩展到50时保持实用性：证明时间从1.02秒增至1.78秒，验证时间稳定在约0.84秒，证明大小保持恒定。

Conclusion: 所提出的隐私保护审计框架在检测LLM部署后篡改方面有效且实用，token级探测作为黑盒方案在灵敏度方面表现最佳，同时zk-SNARK证明系统保证了审计过程的可扩展性和效率，为专有模型场景下的AI治理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Post-deployment changes to large language models can alter behavior while leaving routine outputs largely unchanged, creating a challenge for AI governance when model weights are proprietary. We present a privacy-preserving zk-SNARK-based audit framework that searches for probes designed in the spirit of adversarial examples to amplify logit drift between an approved model and a modified deployment. Our framework explores complementary probe families under different access models. Token-based probes operate in a black-box setting and require only the input interface, tokenizer, and vocabulary. Embedding-based probes require gray-box access to the embedding interface. Stress probes rely on additional interface capabilities but do not require full white-box access to model weights or architecture. This range allows probe selection to balance sensitivity, access requirements, and deployment cost. We evaluate probe constructions across LLM architectures, model-tampering scenarios representative of post-deployment attacks, and GPU platforms. Importantly, our experimental results demonstrate that token-based probes consistently deliver the strongest mean sensitivity across models and GPU platforms, although operating in a black-box setting. Our Groth16 zk-SNARK workflow remains practical as the probe set scales from 1 to 50, where proving time increases from 1.02 to 1.78 seconds, verification remains near 0.84 seconds, and proof size remains constant.

</details>


### [349] [DisCTI: Who Needs to Know Timely? Automated Sector-Aware Cyber Threat Intelligence Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27967)
*Fajar Wijitrisnanto,Alsharif Abuadbba,Yansong Gao,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对网络威胁情报（CTI）分发中关键信息基础设施部门映射缺失的问题，将部门定向CTI分发建模为多标签分类问题，构建了一个包含872个部门标注CTI事件的新数据集，并应用BERT模型实现CTI事件到部门的自动映射，采用STIX格式实现跨平台互操作，最终取得宏平均F1分数0.89和Hamming损失0.055的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 及时的网络威胁情报分发对组织应对攻击至关重要，但现有的CTI平台（如MISP）虽然提供部门标签功能，实践中98%的事件未被分类，这严重限制了共享情报的运营价值，尤其是关键信息基础设施部门面临较大风险暴露。

Method: 将部门定向CTI分发建模为多标签分类问题；利用CTI结构和部门特定威胁模式的领域知识，从威胁情报平台（TIP）构建了包含872个部门标注CTI事件的新数据集；采用BERT（基于Transformer的模型）自动将CTI事件映射到部门；使用STIX格式实现跨平台互操作性。

Result: 在自建数据集上达到宏平均F1分数0.89，Hamming损失0.055，即94.5%的个体部门标签分配是正确的。

Conclusion: 研究证明了部门感知的自动化CTI分发的可行性，并强调将专家领域知识嵌入机器学习设计能够填补威胁情报管道中的关键空白，使防御行动更快且更具情境相关性。

Abstract: The timely dissemination of cyber threat intelligence (CTI) is critical for organizations to mount swift and effective incident response. When valid CTI is delivered to the right sector at the right time, identical attacks can often be contained or mitigated. However, today's rapidly expanding CTI landscape overwhelms analysts, who must sift through massive and heterogeneous feeds. Existing platforms such as the Malware Information Sharing Platform (MISP) provide sector tagging features (e.g., energy, finance, government), but in practice, these remain largely unmapped (98% of events are left uncategorized). This lack of automated and timely sector mapping severely limits the operational value of shared intelligence, leaving organizations that belong especially to the critical information infrastructure sector exposed.
  To address this gap, we formulate sector-targeted CTI dissemination as a multilabel classification problem. Leveraging deep field knowledge of CTI structures and sector-specific threat patterns, we construct a novel data set of 872 sector-labelled CTI events from a threat intelligence platform (TIP). We then apply BERT, a transformer-based model, to automate the mapping of CTI events to sectors. Using the structured threat information expression (STIX) format for cross-platform interoperability, our approach achieves a macro-averaged F1-score of 0.89 at a Hamming loss of 0.055 on the custom dataset, i.e. 94.5% of individual sector-label assignments are correct. These results not only demonstrate the feasibility of sector-aware, automated CTI dissemination but also highlight how embedding expert field knowledge into machine learning design fills a crucial gap in the threat intelligence pipeline, enabling faster and context-relevant defensive action.

</details>


### [350] [CHISEL-ing Back Source Code with AI-enabled Iterative Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27981)
*Varun Kohli,N Raghava,Biplab Sikdar,Dinil Mon Divakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CHISEL框架，一种无需测试套件的反编译代码迭代恢复方法，利用编译器静态分析和模糊测试差异分析作为反馈信号，在ExeBench基准上实现96.1%可重编译率和79.8%可重执行率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反编译器生成难以阅读且不可编译的伪C代码，而近期LLM辅助方法虽然生成可读代码但语义不正确。已有的LLM辅助迭代恢复方法依赖用户提供的测试套件，这在实际场景中往往不可用，因此需要一种无需测试套件的语义恢复方法。

Method: CHISEL框架从Ghidra生成的伪C代码出发，使用编译器静态分析（编译反馈）和覆盖引导模糊器的差异分析（差分反馈）作为迭代信号，并引入丰富的可观测性用于接地分歧检测与反馈、跨迭代分歧记忆和最佳候选保留等增强机制。

Result: 在120个ExeBench函数（x86-64架构，O0-O3四种优化，strip和unstripped变体）上使用Gemma4:31b LLM评估，CHISEL达到平均96.1%的可重编译率和79.8%的可重执行率，平均仅需2.1次迭代，恢复26%的首代执行错误，反馈预言机仅错误接受9.4%候选，性能显著优于两个近期LLM辅助反编译工作。

Conclusion: CHISEL证明无需测试套件也能实现高质量的反编译代码语义恢复，通过编译器与模糊器双反馈机制和多种增强策略，在可编译性、可执行性和效率上均取得显著优势，为LLM辅助反编译领域提供了新的可行方向。

Abstract: Decompilation aims to recover high-level, compilable, and semantically equivalent code from binaries. Traditional decompilers produce pseudo-C that is difficult to read and does not compile, while the recent LLM-assisted approaches generate readable, but semantically incorrect code. LLM-aided iterative recovery is an emerging branch of research, but prior works rely on supplied test suites for semantic recovery. In this work, we present CHISEL, a test suite-free framework to iteratively recover source code from Ghidra-derived pseudo-C. CHISEL uses simple yet effective feedback from a compiler (static analysis) and a coverage-guided fuzzer (differential analysis), augmented by rich observables for grounded divergence detection and feedback, cross-iteration divergence memory, and best candidate retention. We systematically evaluate CHISEL for compilation and semantic recovery, feedback oracle soundness, and iteration overhead on 120 ExeBench functions compiled for the x86-64 architecture, across four optimizations (O0-O3), in both stripped and unstripped variants, using the open-weight Gemma4:31b LLM. CHISEL, with all recommended features, achieves an average of 96.1% re-compilability and 79.8% re-executability rates at an average of 2.1 iterations. Significantly, CHISEL recovers 26% of first-generation execution errors. At the same time, CHISEL feedback oracle falsely accepts only 9.4% candidates. Lastly, CHISEL performs significantly better than two recent prior work on LLM-assisted decompilation.

</details>


### [351] [CAITLYN: Can LLM Agents Autonomously Synthesize Defenses against Emerging Injection Attacks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27990)
*Zi Liang,Xiaoyu Xu,Yanyun Wang,Minxin Du,Qingqing Ye,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CAITLYN，一个针对LLM智能体提示注入攻击的防御中间件，通过双系统架构（即时防御+自适应防御合成）解决了现有防御在运行时效率、上下文精度和适应性之间的三难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM智能体的提示注入防御虽然对已知攻击有效，但在实际部署中因攻击变体和新兴威胁而面临挑战；同时现有方案普遍存在运行时效率、上下文精度和适应性之间的三难权衡问题。

Method: CAITLYN采用双系统架构：系统I通过两层库（Tier-0基于规则的检测脚本和Tier-1基于优化LLM的精确推理）提供即时的已知攻击防御；系统II监控异常信号并自动合成新型防御，以应对未知威胁。该方法与具体智能体无关，可作为防御中间件部署。

Result: 在标准基准上，CAITLYN以更低token开销匹配了最先进防御的检测性能；在新增的Emerging基准（包含新型注入技术）上，系统II能自主合成经验证的防御能力，在三个不同智能体环境中显著降低攻击成功率。

Conclusion: CAITLYN通过即时防御与自适应防御合成相结合的双系统架构，有效解决了LLM智能体提示注入防御三难问题，兼具高效性、精确性和适应性，为智能体环境中的注入威胁提供了可靠的防御方案。

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks on Large Language Model (LLM) agents seek to introduce malicious instructions or content into external text sources retrieved by agents, forcing the underlying LLMs to execute harmful actions outside their benign scope. While current defenses effectively counter known injection attacks, deploying them in LLM agent environments remains challenging due to attack variants and emerging threats. Moreover, existing solutions typically suffer from an inherent trilemma, i.e., a constant trade-off among runtime efficiency, contextual precision, and adaptability. To bridge this gap, we propose Continuous Agents for Injection Threats via Lifelong Yielding Nexus (CAITLYN), an agent-agnostic defense middleware. CAITLYN integrates two systems. System I focuses on immediate defense against existing attacks using a two-tiered library: Tier-0 for rule-based detection scripts and Tier-1 for optimized LLM-based accurate inference. System II, in contrast, is deployed to monitor potential abnormal signals and attempt to synthesize new defenses. On standard benchmarks, CAITLYN matches the detection performance of state-of-the-art defenses at lower token overhead than LLM-as-a-judge baselines. On Emerging, our new delivery-aware benchmark featuring novel injection techniques, static baselines and the standalone System I configuration remain vulnerable. In contrast, System II autonomously synthesizes verified defense capabilities, substantially lowering the attack success rate across three diverse agent environments.

</details>


### [352] [Moirae: A Multimodal Agent Collaborative Framework for Dynamic Android Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27994)
*Xueying Zeng,Youquan Xian,Yanze Li,bowen hu,Ziqi Shan,Xu Luo,DanPing Yang,Peng Liu,Lei Cui,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态智能体协作框架，用于动态安卓恶意软件检测。通过收集多模态运行时证据，利用基于ReAct的专门智能体分析互补行为视图，在无需微调的情况下达到90.06%的准确率，展现出对概念漂移的强零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习检测器因依赖随时间分布变化的实现特定特征而易受概念漂移影响；基于LLM的检测器又通常依赖代码中心或单一维度证据，易受混淆技术影响且难以进行全面行为分析。

Method: 提出多模态智能体协作框架，动态收集多模态运行时证据，采用基于ReAct的专门智能体分析互补行为视图。检测流程首先识别视觉欺骗线索，建模UI状态转换，并集成运行时API行为，在用户可见界面和隐藏后端操作之间融合多维证据。

Result: 在时间和分布上均未见过的数据集实验中，该框架无需微调即达到90.06%的准确率，优于现有最先进基线，展现出对安卓恶意软件概念漂移的强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该多模态智能体协作方法有效克服了传统检测器对概念漂移的脆弱性以及现有LLM检测器在处理混淆和全面行为分析方面的局限，为动态安卓恶意软件检测提供了更具鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: The Android ecosystem faces persistent and rapidly evolving malware threats. Existing machine learning detectors are vulnerable to concept drift because they rely on implementation-specific features whose distributions change over time. Large language models (LLMs) offer strong semantic understanding and zero-shot reasoning, but current LLM-based detectors typically depend on code-centric or single-dimensional evidence, making them susceptible to obfuscation and limiting comprehensive behavior analysis. We present {\sysname}, a multimodal agent collaborative framework for dynamic Android malware detection. {\sysname} dynamically collects multimodal runtime evidence and employs ReAct-based specialized agents to analyze complementary behavioral views. The detection process begins by identifying visual deception cues, modeling UI state transitions, and integrating runtime API behaviors to fuse multi-dimensional evidence across user-visible interfaces and hidden backend operations. Experiments on temporally and distributionally unseen datasets show that {\sysname} achieves an accuracy of 90.06\% without fine-tuning, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines and demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization against Android malware concept drift.

</details>


### [353] [The Impact of Magma: A Ground-Truth Fuzzing Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28016)
*Ahmad Hazimeh,Adrian Herrera,Srividya Subramanian,Thaqiya Aman,Sara Vaccino,Qiang Liu,Mathias Payer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Magma是一个开源、基于真实漏洞（ground-truth）的模糊测试基准，旨在实现统一的模糊测试器评估与比较。本文介绍了Magma的动机、设计、影响及其对原始基准的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模糊测试基准缺乏统一性和真实性，难以对不同模糊测试器进行公平、可复现的比较评估，因此需要构建一个基于真实漏洞的开源基准。

Method: Magma作为开源基准，基于真实世界漏洞（ground-truth bugs）设计，提供统一的评估框架和指标，支持公平的模糊测试器横向对比；本文还描述了对原始SIGMETRICS 2021论文所发布基准的扩展。

Result: Magma已成为模糊测试领域广泛使用的基准工具，其设计有效支撑了统一的模糊测试器评估与比较，并持续通过扩展更新来覆盖更多应用场景和漏洞类型。

Conclusion: Magma通过提供基于真实漏洞的开源基准，显著推动了模糊测试评估的标准化和公平性，其持续扩展进一步增强了基准的覆盖度和实用性。

Abstract: Magma is an open-source and ground-truth fuzzing benchmark that enables uniform fuzzer evaluation and comparison. Magma was originally released with a research paper published at ACM SIGMETRICS 2021. This short paper explains the motivation, the design, and the impact of Magma, with a description of extensions to the original benchmark.

</details>


### [354] [Compared to What? A Human-Anchored Security Benchmark for LLM-Generated Infrastructure-as-Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28021)
*Animesh Shaw*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GenIaC-SecBench，一个包含100个部署场景、12种模型配置、1,196个IaC工件的安全基准，并提供634个人类撰写的模板作为首个规模匹配的人类安全基线。研究发现漏洞密度与工件大小强负相关（ρ=-0.55），资源数量匹配后所有模型配置的漏洞密度为人类的3.21x-3.87x，且任务越简单差距越大。厂商扩展思维显著优于提示链式思维（-12.0%，p=0.0013），而提示链式思维与标准生成无显著差异。此外，可部署性与漏洞无关，经典Friedman检验在现实基准设计中不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对模型生成IaC的安全评估只报告原始漏洞数量，缺乏人类基线，无法判断模型是否真的比工程师更差。为了在可比条件下回答这一问题，需要引入规模匹配的人类安全基线并设计恰当的比较方法。

Method: 构建100个按架构复杂度分层的部署场景基准，使用12种模型配置生成1,196个IaC工件，并利用Checkov、Trivy、KICS三个策略引擎扫描；同时用相同工具链扫描634个人类模板作为基线。采用Spearman相关分析、资源数量匹配、生成/链式思维/扩展思维三种推理模式对比以及token工具化分析。

Result: 漏洞密度与工件大小呈强负相关（ρ=-0.55, p<10^-77）；资源匹配后模型漏洞密度为人类3.21x-3.87x，单资源任务差距达4.9x，二十资源以上差距缩至1.4x；扩展思维优于提示链式思维（-12.0%, p=0.0013），但仅使用不到1%的输出预算；可部署性与漏洞无关（r=0.158, p=0.625）；经典Friedman检验在现实设计中不可行。

Conclusion: 未匹配大小的比较会混淆尺寸因素与安全问题。模型在安全上确实比人类差（约3-4倍），但差距随任务复杂度增加而缩小。推理增强的收益有界，需要新的基准设计方法论（如Skillings-Mack统计）来支撑未来IaC安全评估。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to author Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC), where a single insecure default can be deployed directly into production. Prior evaluations report raw vulnerability counts for model-generated IaC, but without a human baseline they cannot determine whether models are actually worse than engineers. We introduce GenIaC-SecBench, a benchmark of 100 deployment scenarios stratified by architectural complexity, evaluated across 12 model configurations from four vendors, producing 1,196 IaC artifacts scanned by three independent policy engines (Checkov, Trivy, KICS). Critically, we also scan 634 human-authored IaC templates with the same toolchain, providing the first size-matched human security baseline.
  Vulnerability density is strongly inverse to artifact size (Spearman $ρ= -0.55$, $p < 10^{-77}$), meaning unmatched comparisons measure size rather than security. When matched on declared-resource count, all model configurations fall within 3.21x--3.87x the human vulnerability density, with the gap widening for simpler tasks (4.9x at one resource, 1.4x at twenty or more).
  We decompose reasoning into standard generation, prompt-engineered chain-of-thought, and vendor extended-thinking APIs. Vendor extended thinking significantly outperforms prompted chain-of-thought ($-12.0\%$, $p = 0.0013$), while prompted chain-of-thought is indistinguishable from standard generation ($-1.3\%$, n.s.). Token instrumentation shows extended thinking uses under 1\% of the output budget, explaining its bounded effect.
  Two negative results also emerge: deployability does not correlate with vulnerability ($r = 0.158$, $p = 0.625$), and classical complete-case Friedman testing is infeasible for realistic benchmark designs, motivating the Skillings-Mack statistic. All code, data, and regeneration scripts are released.

</details>


### [355] [TagZilla: Automated Owner and Abuse Type Tagging for Indicators of Compromise in Threat Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28124)
*Gibran Gomez,Juan Caballero*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TagZilla平台，利用大语言模型自动从网络威胁情报报告中提取并标注失陷指标（IoCs）的上下文信息，包括所属威胁组织、恶意软件家族和滥用类型。在100份威胁报告人工标注数据上，所有者标注F1达0.94，滥用类型标注F1达0.93，并在765份报告中成功标注15,583个IoCs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTI报告中的IoC（如IP地址、URL、文件哈希、加密货币钱包）通常只以非结构化文本或列表形式呈现，缺乏上下文信息，限制了其可用性。现有的IoC标注方法难以自动关联IoC与威胁组织、恶意软件家族等信息。

Method: TagZilla采用双分类框架：基于LLM的开放世界分类为IoC分配所有者标签（威胁组织/恶意软件家族），以及基于封闭世界分类为IoC分配29种滥用类型标签。系统可自动分析威胁报告文本并为其中描述的IoC添加上下文标注。

Result: 在包含1,534个IoC的100份威胁报告人工标注基准上，所有者标注F1为0.94，滥用类型标注F1为0.93。应用于765份报告后，识别出15,583个IoC，归属于637个恶意软件家族、113个威胁组织和162个其他实体。

Conclusion: TagZilla能够有效为IoC自动添加上下文标注，即使在描述多个威胁参与者和恶意软件家族的复杂报告中也能保持性能，并能据此为实体生成IoC画像。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) reports often describe Indicators of Compromise (IoCs) such as IP addresses, URLs, file hashes, and cryptocurrency wallets involved in cyberattacks. Those IoCs are typically described in the unstructured report's text, or listed at the end of the report with little context, limiting their usefulness. This paper presents TagZilla, a platform that, given a threat report, automatically analyzes its text and tags the IoCs it describes with contextual information about the threat group and malware family that the IoC belongs to and the type of abuse associated with the IoC (e.g., phishing, sextortion, command-and-control). TagZilla provides a novel LLM-based approach to assign owner tags to IoCs using an open-world classification, and assigns 29 abuse type tags to IoCs using a closed-world classification. We evaluate TagZilla on a manually generated ground truth of 100 threat reports containing 1,534 indicators, where it achieves an F1 score of 0.94 for owner tagging and 0.93 for abuse type tagging. Then, we apply TagZilla to tag 765 threat reports, identifying 15,583 IoCs belonging to 637 malware families, 113 threat groups, and 162 other entities. The results show that TagZilla can tag IoCs even in reports describing multiple actors and malware families, enabling the generation of IoC profiles for those entities.

</details>


### [356] [Layered LLM Defenses as an Ensemble: Access Tiers, Inference Cost, and the Measured Failure Correlation Between Defense Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28327)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Muhammad Shahid Jabbar,Sadam Al-Azani,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 实践者通过叠加防御层来保护LLM，假设各层能复合增强效果。本文发现这种复合仅在成员在不同输入上失败时成立（即独立性条件），但安全文献从未测量这一条件。作者提出两个工具（AATM对抗者访问层模型和成本模型）使其可测量，并通过实验证明七层防御栈中失败相关性均为正（φ=0.30-0.75），联合残余攻击成功率比乘法预测最多高0.172。相关性源于架构共性而非采样，因此多样性选择成员但无法预测整个栈的实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM防御实践中普遍采用堆叠多层防御的方式，但缺乏对其复合效果的理论与实证验证。安全文献建议成员须在不同输入上失败（独立性）才能复合，但从未测量这一条件是否成立。

Method: 提出两个测量工具：Adversary Access-Tier Model（按对抗者访问权限分级A0-A4）和成本模型（将防御按推理开销分为五类，其中两类需训练权重或读取激活）。由此推导堆叠行为，并运行一个自适应对抗者攻击七层防御栈，测量十五对防御层之间的失败相关性，通过分层、排列推理、多数投票评分和外部校准阈值进行稳健性检验。

Result: 所有十五对可测量层对的失败相关性均为正（φ=0.30-0.75），联合残余成功率超出乘法预测最多0.172。分层于行为难度后大部分关联消散（属常见因果），但经由排列推理、多数投票评分和外部校准阈值检验仍稳健。该栈拒绝了80%的良性提示，整体表现与其最强单层统计上不可区分。相关性是架构性的而非采样性的，扩大成员池不减弱相关性。

Conclusion: 防御堆叠的复合增益严重被高估：由于堆叠成员共享底层模型而架构性相关，独立性假设不成立。多样性只是成员选择的准则，不能预测已组装堆叠的效果，最终性能必须端到端测量。

Abstract: Practitioners defend large language models (LLMs) by stacking defenses, assuming the layers compound. A stack is an ensemble, and ensembles compound only under a condition the LLM security literature recommends but never measures: the members must fail on different inputs.
  Two instruments make that measurable. The Adversary Access-Tier Model (AATM) grades an adversary by the access it holds, from system-only (A0) to influence over training data (A4). A cost model sorts defenses into five classes of inference-time overhead; because two classes require training weights or reading activations, they tier the defender as AATM tiers the adversary. From these we derive how a stack behaves, and the quantities a defender cares about diverge: coverage saturates within a tier, cost rises by class, false refusals accumulate as a union, and residual attack success falls multiplicatively only under independence.
  We measure that independence. Running one adaptive adversary against a seven-layer stack, failure correlation is positive in all fifteen measurable pairs ($φ$ from $0.30$ to $0.75$), and the joint residual exceeds the multiplicative prediction by up to $0.172$. Stratifying on behavior difficulty dissolves most of the association, so the dependence is predominantly common-cause, but it survives permutation inference, majority-vote grader labels, and externally calibrated thresholds. The same stack refuses four in five benign prompts while remaining statistically indistinguishable from its strongest single layer.
  The dependence is architectural rather than sampling-based: members correlate through the model they all wrap, so no wider member pool weakens it. Diversity therefore selects stack members but does not predict what an assembled stack delivers, which has to be measured end to end.

</details>


### [357] [False-CSI Attacks in Power-Domain NOMA for 6G: A Threat Taxonomy and System-Level Impacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28351)
*Samira Jafarli,Aysha Ebrahim,Suleyman Uludag*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨功率域NOMA中虚假信道状态信息（CSI）攻击的安全问题，提出基于"幅度"和"排序效应"两个主轴的威胁分类体系，并将各类攻击映射到对功率分配、SIC可靠性、调度、公平性、吞吐量和保密性的系统级影响，强调应将虚假CSI视为6G NOMA的控制输入完整性问题而非单纯的估计误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率域NOMA的核心机制（功率分配、用户排序、配对、聚类、波束成形）高度依赖CSI的完整性。现有研究通常将CSI问题视为噪声或估计误差，但CSI被恶意偏置（而非仅噪声污染）时，NOMA的系统性能将系统性失真。作者旨在揭示虚假CSI作为攻击面的威胁空间，并提供一个可操作的分析框架。

Method: 提出一个紧凑的威胁分类法，包含两个主轴：（1）幅度轴（区分低报与高报）；（2）排序效应轴（区分保序、边界和逆序攻击）。在此基础上，进一步扩展到协调式虚假CSI行为、组变更攻击、方向伪造、导频欺骗、训练阶段注入以及RIS诱导信道操纵等攻击类型，最后将各攻击族映射到系统层影响。

Result: 建立了完整的虚假CSI攻击分类体系，覆盖从基础的两轴分类到多种高级攻击变体，并将每个攻击家族与功率分配、SIC可靠性、调度器行为、公平性、吞吐量及保密性等系统级影响建立对应关系。

Conclusion: 虚假CSI不仅是信道估计问题，更应被视为6G NOMA中的控制输入完整性问题。攻击者通过故意偏置CSI可系统性扭曲NOMA的核心运行机制，安全设计需要从这一全新视角进行防护。

Abstract: Power-domain non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) remains a widely studied technique for improving spectral efficiency and supporting dense connectivity in beyond-5G and 6G networks. Its main operating mechanisms, however, depend on the integrity of channel-state information (CSI). Power allocation, user ordering, pairing, clustering, and beamforming can all be distorted when the CSI consumed by the base station is deliberately biased rather than merely noisy. This article examines false CSI as an attack surface in power-domain NOMA. We organize the threat space using a compact taxonomy with two primary axes: magnitude, which distinguishes underreporting from overreporting, and ordering effect, which distinguishes order-preserving, boundary, and order-reversing attacks. We then show how coordinated false- CSI behavior, group-changing attacks, direction forgery, pilot spoofing, training-phase injection, and RIS-induced channel manipulation extend this basic taxonomy. Finally, we map each attack family to system-level impacts on power allocation, SIC reliability, scheduler behavior, fairness, throughput, and secrecy. The central message is that false CSI should be treated not only as a channel-estimation problem, but also as a control-input integrity problem for 6G NOMA.

</details>


### [358] [Optimal Adversarial Testing: Extracting Honest Test Results from Dishonest Test Takers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28362)
*Owen Cox,April Xu,Weiyu Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种即使在存在作弊者污染测试结果的情况下，仍能恢复所需测试结果的优化测试策略。该策略通过动态规划方法确定最优地重新测试选定的被试组，并采用不同的测试安全措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中，对物体或人的测试结果不仅天然带有噪声，还可能被受测者的对抗性行为（如作弊）所污染。随着AI技术的发展，利用AI作弊导致的结果失真日益普遍且严重，因此需要设计能抵御作弊影响的测试策略。

Method: 提出最优测试策略，该策略会选择特定的被试小组进行重新测试，并在重新测试时采用不同的安全措施。最优策略的确定通过动态规划方法实现。

Result: 所提出的测试策略即使存在作弊者污染测试结果的情况下，仍能恢复所需的测试结果，实现了对作弊行为干扰的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过动态规划优化的重新测试策略可以有效抵御作弊者（尤其是利用AI技术作弊）对测试结果的污染，确保测试质量的可靠性。

Abstract: In applications, it is often required to test objects or people to determine their qualities in terms of certain metrics. However, besides being naturally noisy, the test results can be corrupted by adversarial behaviors of objects or people being tested (test takers). For example, dishonest test takers can cheat in the exams to distort the test results. With the development of AI technologies, such distortions driven by cheating using AI technologies are becoming more commonplace and severe. In this paper, we propose optimal testing strategies which can still recover needed test results even if there are cheaters polluting the results. The proposed testing strategies will optimally re-test selected group of test takers using different testing security measures. We determine the optimal testing strategies using a dynamic programming method.

</details>


### [359] [CamoDocs: A Poisoning Attack Against Retrieval-Augmented Language Models Using Camouflaged Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28389)
*Jaewon Jung,Haizhong Zheng,Hongsun Jang,Jaeyong Song,Beidi Chen,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CamoDocs投毒攻击方法，通过将对抗性文档伪装成良性内容、使用分散令牌扩散嵌入并应用连贯性过滤，避免直接查询包含目标的词汇/嵌入痕迹，在多个RAG防御、LLM和基准上实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG投毒攻击通常依赖在毒化文档中直接插入目标查询来提升检索率，但这会在词法空间和嵌入空间留下可被简单过滤方法检测到的伪影。作者旨在设计一种不直接包含查询、更难被防御机制识别的投毒攻击方法。

Method: CamoDocs包含三个核心组件：(1) 将合成的良性和对抗性草稿分块；(2) 用分散令牌替换良性块中的选定令牌，使毒化文档的嵌入在向量空间中扩散，降低与良性文档的可区分性；(3) 应用连贯性过滤以限制可读性退化，同时保持攻击有效性。

Result: 在七种RAG防御、三个开源权重LLM和三个基准上，CamoDocs实现了强平均攻击成功率（ASR），且避免了简单查询检测所利用的查询重叠伪影。在专有模型上，对GPT-5.4-mini平均ASR达61.80%，对Claude-Haiku-4.5达55.09%。基于聚类且依赖擦除的防御（如TrustRAG）可降低ASR，但代价是检索依赖基准（如NeoQA）上显著的效用下降。

Conclusion: CamoDocs表明精心设计的嵌入空间伪装能有效规避现有RAG防御，现有聚类/擦除类防御虽有一定抵御能力但以重大效用损失为代价，RAG系统的安全性仍需更强健的防御方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) augments LLMs with external documents, but public or user-editable sources expose RAG systems to data poisoning: attackers can inject malicious documents to steer outputs toward targeted answers. Existing poisoning attacks often rely on query inclusion, inserting the target query into poisoned documents to improve retrieval; however, this creates lexical and embedding-space artifacts that make them easy to filter. We propose CamoDocs, a poisoning attack that avoids direct query inclusion by camouflaging adversarial documents among benign content. CamoDocs chunks synthesized benign and adversarial drafts, replaces selected tokens in benign chunks with dispersion tokens that spread poisoned-document embeddings, and applies coherence filtering to limit readability degradation. Across seven RAG defenses, three open-weight LLMs, and three benchmarks, CamoDocs achieves strong average ASR while avoiding query-overlap artifacts exploited by simple query detection. It also remains effective against proprietary models, achieving average ASRs of 61.80% on GPT-5.4-mini and 55.09% on Claude-Haiku-4.5. Finally, we show that erasure-heavy clustering defenses such as TrustRAG can reduce ASR, but only with substantial utility drops on retrieval-dependent benchmarks such as NeoQA. Code is available at https://github.com/jaewonalive/CamoDocs.

</details>


### [360] [BEACON: Behavior-Anchored Cross-Source Knowledge Graph Construction for Cyber Threat Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28394)
*Changze Li,Yutong Cheng,Tsania Camila Finnisa,Qian Cui,Wei Ding,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BEACON是一个LLM驱动的跨源CTI知识图谱构建框架。其核心洞察是将攻击行为映射到MITRE ATT&CK技术作为锚点，将上下文实体和IoC附加到这些锚点上，从而将每个报告的图对齐到同一规范空间。框架采用"先提出后验证"的提取范式和层级对齐策略合并跨源图，并在两个新构建的人工地标注数据集上（最大的报告级CTI提取数据集和首个跨源整合数据集）分别以至少23%和9%的优势超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTI大量存在于非结构化报告中，其体量和异质性远超人工分析能力。现有方法主要在单一报告内提取部分信息，未探索跨源场景——同一威胁在不同来源中可能使用不相关名称，导致知识难以整合。需要一种能够跨源统一构建CTI知识图谱的方法。

Method: BEACON包含两个阶段：（1）提取阶段：在propose-then-verify范式下将每份报告提取为图，将候选元素锚定在报告证据和官方ATT&CK定义上，以抑制LLM误分类和幻觉；（2）合并阶段：采用层级对齐策略，按确定性递减顺序应用信号——从字符级相似度、语义相似度到重叠的技术邻域——并在合并过程中迭代更新邻域池。

Result: 作者构建并发布了两个人工标注数据集：从34个来源构建的最大报告级CTI提取数据集（8,395个元素）和首个跨源整合数据集（3,487个元素）。在这些数据集上，BEACON在报告级提取任务上至少超越所有基线23%，在跨源整合任务上至少超越9%。

Conclusion: 通过将攻击行为作为锚点映射到标准化的ATT&CK目录，BEACON有效解决了跨源CTI知识图谱构建中实体命名不一致的问题，并提供了可复现的基准数据集以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Cyber threat intelligence (CTI) is foundational to modern cyber defense, yet much of it resides in unstructured reports whose volume and heterogeneity far exceed manual analysis, motivating research on automatically constructing knowledge graphs from CTI reports. However, existing approaches mainly extract partial information within a single report, leaving the cross-source setting unexplored, where the same threat is given unrelated names. Our key insight is that attack behaviors, once mapped to MITRE ATT&CK (a standardized catalog of attack techniques), can anchor the rest of a report. Attack behaviors are the adversarial actions a report describes, while contextual entities (e.g., threat actors, campaigns, and affected products) and Indicators of Compromise (IoCs; e.g., IP addresses) are their participants and traces. Attaching them to these anchors places every per-report graph in one canonical space.
  We realize this insight in BEACON, an LLM-driven framework for cross-source CTI knowledge graph construction. Its first stage extracts each report into a graph under a propose-then-verify paradigm, grounding candidates in report evidence and official ATT&CK definitions, to suppress LLM misclassification and hallucination. Its second stage merges these graphs with a hierarchical alignment strategy that applies signals in decreasing order of determinism, from character-level and semantic similarity to overlapping technique neighborhoods, iterating as merges pool neighborhoods. No existing benchmark links entities to technique anchors or provides cross-source alignment ground truth. We therefore construct and release two human-annotated datasets from 34 sources: to our knowledge the largest for report-level CTI extraction (8,395 elements) and the first for cross-source consolidation (3,487). On them, BEACON outperforms all baselines by at least 23% and 9%, respectively.

</details>


### [361] [When Verified Source Becomes Attack Input: Defending Smart Contracts Against LLM-Based Vulnerability Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28400)
*Mingyuan Huang,Zimo Ji,Yifan Mo,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DeLLMGuard框架，通过多地址部署分离源码与运行时执行，防御基于LLM的大规模漏洞扫描，同时保留公开源码披露和授权审计功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约项目通常公开源码以建立信任，但LLM智能体可利用公开源码进行大规模自动化漏洞扫描，威胁现有披露机制的安全模型。

Method: DeLLMGuard将披露源码与运行时执行分离到多个合约地址，迫使LLM智能体需先恢复代理（proxy）、委托（delegate）和工厂（factory）关系才能进行漏洞分析；内置Verification Layer验证部署关系、运行时字节码、源码和状态变更，确保转换不改变原始业务逻辑。

Result: 在387个真实漏洞合约上、基于SCONE-bench环境评估，DeLLMGuard将LLM智能体的根本原因定位正确率从23.5%降至6.6%，并优于闭源字节码基线；追踪和消融分析显示智能体常能恢复下游合约但无法识别漏洞，跨合约恢复仍是主要挑战。

Conclusion: 跨合约关系恢复是自动化LLM漏洞扫描的主要障碍，DeLLMGuard通过增加分析复杂度有效降低了恶意扫描成功率，同时不牺牲公开披露和授权审计能力。

Abstract: Smart contracts are financial programs deployed on blockchains to manage digital assets. To build trust with users and investors, smart contract projects typically publish their source code on blockchain explorers and verify it against the deployed bytecode, making the on-chain program accessible through a human-readable implementation. However, LLM agents are changing the threat model of this disclosure mechanism. By leveraging publicly disclosed source code, recent agent workflows make it increasingly practical to scan contract vulnerabilities for exploits at large scale.
  In this paper, we propose DeLLMGuard, a smart contract deployment framework that defends against malicious LLM-based vulnerability scanning while preserving public source disclosure and authorized auditing. DeLLMGuard can separate disclosed source code from runtime execution through multiple contract addresses in a real-world blockchain environment. LLM agents must therefore recover additional proxy, delegate, and factory relations before vulnerability analysis. A built-in Verification Layer checks deployment relations, runtime bytecode, source code, and state changes to ensure that the transformation preserves the original business implementation. We evaluate DeLLMGuard on 387 real-world vulnerable contracts with three LLM agents in an environment derived from SCONE-bench. DeLLMGuard reduces overall root-cause correctness from 23.5% to 6.6% and outperforms the closed-source bytecode baseline on the primary non-proxy set. Trace and ablation analyses further show that agents often recover downstream contracts but still fail to identify the vulnerability, indicating that cross-contract recovery remains a major challenge for automated LLM scanning.

</details>


### [362] [LongPIBench: A Long-Context Benchmark for Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28411)
*Yupei Liu,Yuqi Jia,Neil Zhenqiang Gong,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LongPIBench，一个面向长上下文场景的提示注入攻击基准，覆盖论文评审、简历筛选、代码审查和邮件摘要四个真实应用场景，每个场景包含合成和真实数据集，上下文长度从数千到数万token。评估发现现有防御在长上下文下的效果被大幅高估，即使是简单的启发式攻击也能绕过最先进的防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入基准主要聚焦于短上下文输入，长上下文场景下的攻击与防御研究几乎空白，导致当前防御方法的有效性被严重高估，缺乏系统评估长上下文提示注入风险的基准。

Method: 构建LongPIBench长上下文提示注入基准，涵盖4个现实应用场景（论文同行评审、简历筛选、代码审查、邮件摘要），每个场景同时构建合成数据集和真实数据集，上下文长度从数千到数万token，用于系统评估长上下文下的提示注入攻击与防御性能。

Result: 在LongPIBench上的评估显示长上下文设置下提示注入防御存在显著漏洞：简单的启发式提示注入攻击即可获得高成功率，并能频繁绕过最先进的防御方法。

Conclusion: LongPIBench可作为现实长上下文场景中系统评估提示注入防御的实用基准，填补了该领域空白，并揭示了长上下文下现有防御的脆弱性。

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks pose a serious security risk to large language models in real-world applications. However, existing prompt injection benchmarks primarily focus on short-context inputs, leaving the attacks and defenses in long-context settings largely unexplored. This gap leads to a substantial overestimation of the effectiveness of current defenses. In this paper, we bridge the gap by introducing LongPIBench, a long-context benchmark for prompt injection covering 4 realistic application scenarios: paper peer review, resume screening, code review, and email summary. For each scenario, we construct a synthetic dataset and a real-world dataset, with context lengths ranging from thousands to tens of thousands of tokens. The evaluation results on LongPIBench reveal significant vulnerabilities of prompt injection defenses under long-context settings: even simple heuristic prompt injection attacks achieve high success rates and frequently bypass state-of-the-art defenses. We hope LongPIBench can serve as a practical benchmark for systematically evaluating prompt injection defenses in realistic long-context scenarios.

</details>


### [363] [Exploiting Per-Core Leakage: Electromagnetic Side-Channel Monitoring of Multicore Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28412)
*Daehyeon Bae,Sujin Park,Insup Lee,YoungGiu Jung,Kyeongsik Lee,HeeSeok Kim,Seokhie Hong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次在多核嵌入式系统中实现了逐核心的电磁侧信道泄露利用，并提出了一种非侵入式的、达到逐核心粒度的侧信道监测方法，通过在异构SoC平台和树莓派4B上验证了其可行性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核处理器在嵌入式系统中日益普及以满足性能需求，但物理侧信道分析在多核架构上仍缺乏探索，因为获取可用的泄露信号本身具有挑战性，导致该领域的侧信道安全研究严重滞后，留下了关键的安全缺口。

Method: 该工作首先揭示了多核架构中的电磁泄露机制，然后首次展示了逐核心的泄露利用方法，使物理侧信道分析在多核系统中成为可能。作为实际扩展，提出了一种非侵入式的侧信道监测方法，能够达到逐核心的粒度；随后在带有射频前端的异构SoC平台上构建原型，并在商业现成的四核嵌入式系统（树莓派4B，ARM Cortex-A72核心）上进行评估。

Result: 在树莓派4B（四核ARM Cortex-A72）上成功验证了逐核心电磁泄露的提取与侧信道分析，证明所提非侵入式监测方法在实际多核嵌入式系统中具有可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了多核嵌入式系统物理侧信道安全研究的关键空白，首次实现了多核架构下的逐核心泄露利用和监测，为后续多核系统的侧信道安全分析提供了新的方法论基础和实践参考。

Abstract: Multicore processors are increasingly adopted in embedded systems to meet growing performance demands. However, physical side-channel analysis of multicore architectures remains underexplored, as obtaining usable leakage is inherently challenging. Consequently, side-channel security research on such systems has lagged far behind, leaving a critical security gap. To address this gap, we reveal the electromagnetic leakage mechanisms in multicore architectures and, for the first time, demonstrate per-core leakage exploitation, thereby enabling physical side-channel analysis for these systems. As a practical extension, we present a non-intrusive side-channel monitoring method that achieves per-core granularity. To validate its feasibility and practicality, we implement a prototype on a heterogeneous SoC platform with an RF front-end, and evaluate on a commercial off-the-shelf quad-core embedded system, the Raspberry Pi 4B with ARM Cortex-A72 cores.

</details>


### [364] [Quantum-Based Solutions for Security Enhancement in Open Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28480)
*Dzung Quoc Ngo,Tharmikka Raveendranathan,Tuan Anh Le,Vinod Sharma,Purav Shah,Huan X. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地探讨了开放无线接入网（O-RAN）面临的量子安全挑战，提出将后量子密码、量子密码、量子认证和量子增强威胁检测与零信任架构相结合，以应对量子时代的安全威胁，并分析了其在Near-RT RIC、O-Cloud和开放接口中的集成路径及未来6G网络的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN虽带来了灵活性、互操作性和智能性，但其解耦和软件定义的架构扩大了攻击面。传统的密码机制在量子敌手面前可能失效，亟需探索量子弹性机制来保障O-RAN的机密性、认证和信任。

Method: 采用综述性视角，系统分析量子安全技术在O-RAN生态中的应用，涵盖后量子密码（PQC）、量子密码、量子认证和量子增强威胁检测，并探讨其与基于持续验证、最小权限和微隔离的零信任架构的融合，以及各技术与O-RAN关键组件（Near-RT RIC、O-Cloud、开放接口）的集成。

Result: 提出了量子安全机制与O-RAN架构集成的综合分析框架，评估了实际部署考虑因素、技术成熟度和采用时间线，并指出了面向6G网络的安全、弹性、面向未来的O-RAN架构的开放研究方向。

Conclusion: 量子弹性机制（PQC、量子密码、量子认证、量子增强检测）与零信任架构相结合，是保障O-RAN在量子时代安全的可行路径，但技术成熟度和部署细节仍需进一步研究，以实现6G时代安全且可演进的O-RAN。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) introduce unprecedented flexibility, interoperability, and intelligence into next-generation wireless systems, but their disaggregated and software-defined architecture also expands the attack surface and creates new security vulnerabilities. Conventional cryptographic mechanisms, while effective against classical threats, may become insufficient in the presence of quantum-enabled adversaries. This article presents a comprehensive perspective on quantum security for O-RAN, examining how quantum-resilient mechanisms can enhance confidentiality, authentication, and trust across the RAN ecosystem. It discusses post-quantum cryptography (PQC), quantum cryptography, quantum authentication, and quantum-enhanced threat detection within a zero-trust architecture based on continuous verification, least privilege, and micro-segmentation. Their integration with the Near-Real-Time (Near-RT) RAN Intelligent Controller, O-Cloud, and open interfaces is analyzed, together with practical deployment considerations, technology maturity, and adoption timelines. Finally, open research directions are outlined toward secure, resilient, and future-proof O-RAN architectures for 6G networks.

</details>


### [365] [LLM-Based Agents for Software and Systems Security: Approaches, Applications, and Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28490)
*Jingjing Nie,Jiawei Guo,Krishna Meda,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对2023-2026年间基于大语言模型（LLM）的软件与系统安全自动化代理进行了系统性文献综述，涵盖技术方法（架构、感知、记忆、推理规划、动作空间、编排、自我改进）、应用场景和评估协议（数据集、指标、安全措施、基线），揭示了当前领域虽已构建出"能行动"的代理，但尚未实现权威有界、行为可审计的代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的安全代理正被快速采用以自动化安全分析工作流，而将安全决策委托给自主系统具有重大后果。然而该领域存在术语不一致（"agent"定义混乱）、应用风险差异大、评估协议不可比等问题，缺乏系统性的理解，亟需综合综述以指导未来研究。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对2023-2026年间同行评审文献进行结构化分析，围绕三个维度：(1) 技术方法——涵盖代理架构、感知、记忆、推理与规划、动作空间、编排与自我改进；(2) 应用——按所服务的安全任务进行分类；(3) 评估——分析数据集、结果与轨迹指标、安全措施及基线。

Result: 综述揭示了该领域当前的状态：已能构建具有行动能力的代理，但所构建的代理缺乏有界的权威（authority bounded）与可审计的行为（auditable behavior）。同时识别出当前技术方法、应用和评估设计中的局限性及挑战。

Conclusion: 在系统化知识的基础上，指出了现有方法、应用和评估设计的不足，为未来研究提供了有前景的方向，核心需求转向构建权威受约束、行为可审计的安全代理。

Abstract: Software and systems security workflows are typically procedural: analysts inspect heterogeneous artifacts, form hypotheses, invoke tools, interpret outputs, and revise plans. Large language model (LLM)-based agents, which can plan, use tools, retain state, and revise actions across multi-step workflows, are being rapidly adopted to automate this work. Given the consequences of delegating security decisions to autonomous systems, understanding how such agents are built, used, and assessed is crucial. Yet to this date, there remains a lack of systematic understanding of what has been done and how far we are in this field: the term "agent" is applied inconsistently, applications differ sharply in risk, and assessment protocols are often incomparable. To gain a comprehensive and coherent view of this area hence inform relevant future research, this paper provides a systematic literature review of the (1) technical approaches, including agent architecture, perception, memory, reasoning and planning, action space, orchestration, and self-improvement, (2) applications, with respect to the security tasks served, and (3) assessment, including the datasets, outcome and trajectory metrics, safety measures, and baselines considered, over the peer-reviewed literature spanning the emergence of this area (2023--2026). Our synthesis reveals a field that has built agents able to act but not yet agents whose authority is bounded or whose behavior is auditable. In addition to knowledge systematization, we also extend our insights into the limitations of and challenges faced by current approach, application, and assessment designs, which shed light on potentially promising future research directions.

</details>


### [366] [Recognition Without Enforcement: Configuration-Dependent Failures in LLM Agent Instruction Arbitration and External Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28502)
*Jun Wen Leong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM代理在指令仲裁中无法可靠地执行信任边界，存在"识别-执行差距"：模型虽能从激活中解码源格式特征并识别伪造权威，但仍可能执行冲突的工具调用。该差距不可归因于权重固有属性，可通过策略与提示缓解但无法根除。作者提出外部参考监视器（认证源路由+能力门控工具执行）作为外部强制方案，可确定性拒绝所有伪造、篡改、重放和未签名请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理需要仲裁来自系统提示、用户、记忆和工具的多方指令，但这种仲裁不能假设会强制实施信任边界。需要系统的安全性验证，并确定仅靠模型自身的识别能力是否足以防范安全威胁。

Method: 1) 通过交叉探针控制识别"识别-执行差距"；2) 在46个模型端点（6家供应商，含开放权重）上进行权威欺骗的群组评估，以及48个模型上的记忆冲突评估；3) 分析模型激活中源格式特征的可解码性；4) 测试提示层防御的泛化性；5) 实现外部参考监视器（认证源路由+能力门控工具执行），并通过自适应红队验证。

Result: 平均执行率在多样化新型攻击下为1.21% [0.5-2.1%]（基于29个模型14,294次欺骗试验的模型聚类置信区间），但漏洞集中在可复现的单元中，且随部署窗口变化（窗口内每指纹范围最高达47个百分点）。提示层防御无法跨模型和自适应公式泛化。外部参考监视器可确定性拒绝所有测试的伪造、篡改、重放和未签名请求，同时保留合法操作。红队仅发现一个已修复的时钟偏移准入实现缺陷，无密码学绕过。

Conclusion: 安全代理需要外部强制机制，而非仅仅依赖更好的识别能力。应将模型自我仲裁视为能力而非安全边界，外部参考监视器是必要且有效的安全方案。

Abstract: LLM agents arbitrate among instructions from system prompts, users, memory, and tools, but this arbitration cannot be assumed to enforce trust boundaries. We identify a recognition-enforcement gap: source-format features (role-template position, channel metadata, formatting cues) are linearly decodable from model activations, and models can explicitly identify forged authority when prompted, yet some configurations still produce the conflicting tool call. We use "recognition" in this specific decodable-source-format-plus-verbalized-detection sense; crossed-probe controls show it is not a unified abstract trust representation. The gap is not an immutable property of model weights. Restrictive policies and diverse prompts can eliminate execution on the same models, while permissive configurations and particular prompt-model pairs yield deterministic failures. Across a fleet evaluation (authority spoofing: 46 model endpoints across 6 vendors including open-weight; memory conflict: 48 models), average execution under diverse novel attacks is 1.21% [0.5-2.1%] (model-clustered CI over 14,294 spoofed trials from 29 models), but vulnerability is concentrated in reproducible cells and shifts across deployment windows (up to 47pp within-window per-fingerprint range). Prompt-layer defenses likewise fail to generalize across models and adaptive formulations. We therefore treat model self-arbitration as a capability rather than a security boundary and implement an external reference monitor combining authenticated source routing with capability-gated tool execution. It deterministically rejects all tested forged, tampered, replayed, and unsigned requests while preserving legitimate operations. A separate adaptive red-team found one implementation flaw (a since-patched clock-skew admission), not a cryptographic bypass. Secure agents require external enforcement, not merely better recognition.

</details>


### [367] [Offline-Verifiable Accountability for Cross-Organization Agent Messaging: A Preserved Evidence-Bundle Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28542)
*Adil Alshammari,Hayretdin Bahsi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向跨组织智能体工作流的保留证据束（evidence-bundle）模型及策略控制的离线验证器，使审计或争议审查可在无实时服务的情况下独立验证事件证据的充分性。原型评估显示300个完整工作流和1200个有效证据束均被正确验证，所有恶意或策略不充分的证据束均被拒绝，无误接受现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨组织智能体工作流需要保留可独立验证的证据以支持事后审计和争议审查，但现有实时系统可能不可用、由单方控制或不被所有参与者信任。现有机制（认证日志、委派语义、签名检查点、一致性检查）虽各有贡献，但缺乏一个以验证者为中心、可在离线条件下按显式策略检查证据充分性的事件级打包方案。

Method: 提出保留证据束模型和策略控制离线验证器。每个证据束包含：发送方认证、认证日志承诺、见证方背书的检查点证据、追加式连续性、委派感知的授权证据、以及（需要时）接收方签名的回执证据。验证器仅接受被选定策略要求的证据所支持的声明，不根据传输行为或日志包含推断投递或回执。

Result: 在300个完整工作流和1200个有效保留证据束的原型评估中，测量了不同策略配置和工作流事件证据要求下的离线验证器端延迟。检查点上下文锚定在原型中延迟最高，委派和工作流前置条件证据需要额外验证步骤。在定向负向证据测试中，所有被破坏或策略不足的证据束均被拒绝，未观察到误接受。

Conclusion: 该结果支持基于证据的审计和争议审查，无需依赖实时服务或平台特定日志，为跨组织智能体工作流提供了可独立验证的证据充分性评估基础。

Abstract: Cross-organization agent workflows require preserved evidence that remains independently verifiable during later audit or dispute review. They may involve multiple organizations, delegated actions, policy-relevant events, and disputed accountability claims. This is difficult when live systems are unavailable, controlled by one party, or not trusted by all participants. Existing mechanisms provide useful pieces, including authenticated logging, delegation semantics, signed checkpoints, and consistency checks. What remains missing is a verifier-centered event-level bundle for checking evidence sufficiency offline under an explicit policy. We propose a preserved evidence-bundle model and a policy-controlled offline verifier for agent-to-agent workflow events. Each bundle preserves policy-required evidence, including sender authentication, authenticated log commitment, witness-backed checkpoint evidence, append-only continuity, delegation-aware authorization evidence, and explicit receiver-signed receipt evidence when required. The verifier accepts only claims supported by the selected policy-required evidence, giving a later reviewer an offline basis for assessing evidence sufficiency. It does not infer delivery or receipt from transport behavior or log inclusion alone. In a prototype evaluation over 300 complete workflows and 1200 valid preserved bundles, we measure offline verifier-side latency across policy profiles and workflow-event evidence requirements. Checkpoint-context anchoring has the highest latency in the current prototype, while delegation and workflow-prerequisite evidence require additional verification steps. In targeted negative-evidence tests, all corrupted or policy-insufficient bundles were rejected, with no false acceptance observed. These results support evidence-based audit and dispute review without relying on live services or platform-specific logs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [368] [AI Hardware Accelerators for Large Language Models: Architectures and the Memory Wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28048)
*Siddharth Patel,Rohit Singh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了面向大语言模型（LLM）的AI硬件加速器全景，涵盖GPU、定制ASIC、FPGA、存内计算/近存计算以及新兴的神经形态与光子计算。通过Transformer计算结构和roofline分析框架，指出LLM加速的关键瓶颈不是算力而是内存：自回归解码阶段受带宽限制，KV缓存规模可媲美模型权重，数据搬运主导能耗。最终结论是当前没有单一架构能适配所有工作负载，未来取决于硬件-算法协同设计与异构内存中心系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
